## 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态模型在连续 latent 中进行 reasoning、planning、visual thought 或 chain-of-continuous-thought。

### [1] [SeeSE3: Emergence of 3D Space in Vision Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14228)
*Caroline Chen, Sayna Ebrahimi, Fedor Kitashov, Ming-Hsuan Yang, Leonidas Guibas, Viorica Pătrăucean, Maks Ovsjanikov*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维

Importance: 高优先级 (95.71) - Top 3 精读候选

TL;DR: 本文探究自监督视觉基础模型的特征空间是否蕴含3D欧几里得空间的拓扑与几何结构。通过互邻域度量和庞加莱适配器两种探针，发现视觉特征中存在与SE(3)变换强相关的潜子空间。基于此提出"潜空间导航"技术，可在不进行显式3D重建的情况下，直接在潜空间中完成视觉里程计与定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通过回归深度、法向等图像中心量来探测视觉特征的3D感知能力，但未直接研究视觉特征空间结构与3D欧几里得变换群SE(3)之间的关系。本文旨在回答：视觉基础模型是否构建了反映3D欧几里得空间内在属性的表征？

Method: 提出两组探针：(1) 互邻域度量（Mutual Neighborhood），从拓扑角度衡量特征空间邻域与空间拓扑的对齐程度；(2) 庞加莱适配器（Poincaré Adapter），从几何角度测试静态场景中潜位移对相机运动几何的线性可访问性。基于此发现，进一步提出"潜空间导航"技术，直接在潜空间中进行视觉里程计与定位。

Result: 自监督视觉模型（如DINOv2等）在未经过直接3D监督或主动代理训练的情况下，其潜子空间与三维欧几里得空间存在显著强相关。基于潜空间导航的方法可在无需显式3D重建的条件下完成视觉里程计与定位任务。

Conclusion: 视觉基础模型的特征空间中天然蕴含了3D空间结构信息，通过合适的探针可以揭示这种关联。潜空间导航技术为无需显式3D重建的视觉定位与里程计提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 论文研究视觉基础模型特征空间中是否蕴含3D欧几里得空间的拓扑与几何结构，并提出在潜空间中进行视觉里程计与定位（Latent-Space Navigation），属于在连续潜空间中进行空间推理与导航，与[连续多模态推理与潜空间思维]高度相关。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous multimodal latent space reasoning by showing 3D Euclidean structure emerges in vision features, enabling latent-space navigation without explicit 3D reconstruction. Signals: continuous language model, continuous multimodal model, multimodal latent reasoning, representation autoencoder, world model

Abstract: In this paper, we ask whether vision foundation models construct representations that reflect the intrinsic properties of 3D Euclidean space. Unlike previous works that probe 3D awareness of vision features by regressing image-centric quantities such as depth or normals, we investigate the relation between the structure of the space of visual features and the group of Euclidean transformations $SE(3)$. We propose a set of probes to evaluate this relation from both topological and geometric perspectives: a mutual neighborhood metric that measures the alignment between feature neighborhoods and spatial topology, and a Poincaré Adapter to test the linear accessibility of the geometry of camera motion from latent displacements in static scenes. We show that self-supervised vision models, which, in principle, have not been trained with direct 3D supervision or active agency, possess latent subspaces that are remarkably strongly correlated with three-dimensional Euclidean space, when probed correctly. Building on this insight we propose a new class of "Latent-Space Navigation" techniques that perform visual odometry and localization purely in the latent space, bypassing the need for explicit 3D reconstruction.

</details>

### [2] [Latent Communication Between Language Model Agents: Channels, Alignment, and the Limits of Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14103)
*Markus Wenzel*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.MA

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (93.17) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体间通过连续潜空间（SAE特征）进行通信的可能性。实验表明，SAE稀疏潜通道在28倍压缩下保持99.4%的探针准确率，而文本通道仅80.4%；文本序列化会破坏88%的SAE特征。然而，在任务级评估中，潜通道并未超越文本通道，且文本增强潜特征无额外收益，表明丢失的特征主要编码表面形式而非任务相关语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统依赖智能体间通信，通常使用明文文本传递消息。作者假设LLM可能拥有超越文本表达能力的"世界模型"，当需要传达复杂概念时，文本通信会造成信息损失。本文旨在通过结构化实验验证这一假设。

Method: 构建三种通信通道：密集潜通道（SAE特征）、稀疏潜通道（SAE稀疏特征）和文本通道。使用SAE特征分析量化信息损失，通过探针准确率、特征存活率等指标比较各通道的信息保留能力。进一步研究跨架构通信（Llama与Mistral之间）的潜空间对齐，使用Procrustes对齐和非线性对齐方法。

Result: 稀疏潜通道在28倍压缩下保持99.4%探针准确率，文本通道仅80.4%。文本序列化破坏88%的SAE特征。Procrustes对齐在跨架构通信中达到92%的top-1检索率，但带来3-10pp的性能损失。任务级评估中，潜通道在跨语言概念任务上与文本通道持平但未超越，文本增强潜特征无额外收益。

Conclusion: 初始假设未得到充分验证：丢失的特征主要编码表面形式而非任务相关语义。潜通信在现有任务上未展现出超越文本通信的明显优势。要确定潜通信的实际优势，需要更深入的任务（能激发复杂概念）和相应的分析框架。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM智能体之间通过连续潜空间（SAE特征空间）进行通信，探讨连续latent通信相对于文本通信的信息保留优势，属于连续语言模型与语言流（continuous_language_flow）范畴。

Importance reason: Directly tests continuous latent communication for LLM agents, a core priority, but finds limited practical advantage over text. Signals: SAE latent channel preserves 99.4% probe accuracy vs 80.4% text, text serialization destroys 88% SAE features, latent channel matches but does not exceed text in task evaluation, negative result for hypothesis, cross-architecture alignment via Procrustes shows 92% top-1 retrieval

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) are utilized in many contexts and many professions. Those MAS rely on inter-agent communication, usually implemented by clear-text message passing. We hypothesize that Large Language Models may have a world model at their disposal that exceeds expressibility in text when complex concepts need to be communicated. Our aim is to approach a proof of this hypothesis with structured experiments. In this work, we show that LLM agents communicating via text lose information, which we quantify via Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) feature analysis. We construct three communication channels and measure concept-discriminating information in each. We first show that the SAE-sparse channel retains a 99.4% probe accuracy at 28-fold compression over the dense-latent channel vs 80.4% for the text channel. We then proceed to examine the same for cross-architecture communication by using sparse latent space alignment. We find for Procrustes alignment a 92% top-1 retrieval between Llama and Mistral. Using a text round-trip, we perform feature survival analysis to find that text serialization destroys 88% of SAE features, replacing them with a different feature set. We attribute the loss to identity replacement, not attenuation. By our analysis, we were able to attribute a 3-10pp performance penalty to the linear Procrustes alignment, improving with nonlinear alignment methods. In a task-level evaluation we find that the latent channel matches the text channel on cross-lingual concept tasks but never exceeds it. Text augmentation with latent features provides no benefit, leading us to negative conclusions for the initial hypothesis: lost features mostly or completely encode surface form, not task-relevant semantics. To pinpoint the practical advantage of latent communication over a text channel, deeper tasks eliciting complex concepts and an corresponding analysis framework are needed.

</details>


### [3] [Token Time Continuous Diffusion for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14106)
*Parikshit Bansal, Sujay Sanghavi*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (96.67) - Top 2 精读候选

TL;DR: 本文提出 Token Time Continuous Diffusion (TTCD)，一种新的连续空间扩散语言模型。TTCD 在连续空间中确定性地将高斯噪声映射到最终 token 画布，无需额外采样，并引入逐 token 时间步概念——不同 token 以不同速率从噪声过渡到最终 token。连续空间建模避免了离散模型中并行采样多个 token 带来的不准确性，逐 token 时间步则更好地建模条件生成，让更确定的 token 以更快速度推进。在 OpenWebText 上训练的 160M 参数 TTCD 模型经自蒸馏后，在高加速比下无条件生成质量与现有模型相当，条件生成性能更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型在离散空间中迭代时，为加速推理需要并行采样多个 token，这在高加速比下会引入显著的不准确性。作者希望设计一种在连续空间中运行的扩散语言模型，避免并行采样的精度损失，同时通过引入逐 token 时间步来更好地建模条件生成和 token 间的差异化影响。

Method: TTCD 在连续空间中运行，将高斯噪声通过确定性映射转化为最终 token 画布，无需额外采样步骤。核心创新是引入逐 token 时间步（per-token times），即每个 token 拥有独立的时间进度，更确定的 token 以更快速度从噪声过渡到最终值。模型在连续空间中迭代，避免了离散空间并行采样多个 token 的精度问题。训练在 OpenWebText 上进行，使用 160M 参数模型，并采用自蒸馏技术优化推理速度。

Result: 在高加速比下，TTCD 的无条件生成质量与多个同等规模、相同数据训练并自蒸馏的现有模型相当；在条件生成任务上表现更优。在数独求解任务上也取得了类似的性能提升。

Conclusion: TTCD 通过连续空间建模和逐 token 时间步机制，有效解决了离散扩散语言模型在高加速比下的精度问题，在条件生成任务上展现出明显优势，为高效语言建模提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Token Time Continuous Diffusion (TTCD)，一种在连续空间中运行的扩散语言模型，引入逐 token 时间步概念。这属于连续语言模型范畴，与 canonical 子主题"连续语言模型与语言流"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model paradigm with per-token time diffusion, a top priority subtopic. Signals: continuous language model, per-token time diffusion, high speedup conditional generation, self-distillation, OpenWebText benchmark

Abstract: In this paper we introduce token time continuous diffusion (TTCD), a new diffusion language model which (a) operates in continuous space, deterministically mapping Gaussian noise to a final token canvas with no further sampling, and crucially (b) incorporates a new notion of per-token times, with some tokens proceeding from noise to token at a faster rate than others. Continuous space modeling helps TTCD avoid the parallel sampling of multiple tokens, which is a key source of inaccuracy at high speedups for models that iterate purely in discrete space. The notion of per-token times helps TTCD to better model conditional generation, allows for more sure tokens to proceed at a faster rate, and allows for differentiated inter-token influences during refinement. TTCD outperforms discrete models at high speedups. We train a 160M parameter TTCD model on OpenWebText, and then self-distill it; we find that at high speedups we are comparable in unconditional generation quality, and outperform in conditional generation, several existing models of similar size trained, on the same data, and self-distilled. We achieve similar gains in Sudoku solving as well.

</details>


### [4] [Mask-Aware Policy Gradients for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15200)
*Haran Raajesh, Kulin Shah, Adam Klivans, Philipp Krähenbühl*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Mask-Aware Policy Gradients (MAPG)，将 MDLM 的生成过程形式化为两阶段动作 MDP，策略梯度自然分解为 token 项和 masking 项，联合优化两项在数学推理和代码生成上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法在扩展至 Masked Diffusion Language Models (MDLMs) 时面临困难，因为对数似然估计难以处理。已有方法仅建模 token 预测而忽略生成过程中位置解掩的顺序，导致优化不充分。

Method: 将 MDLM 生成过程形式化为两阶段动作 MDP：每个步骤包含两个决策——(1) 在每个掩码位置放置什么 token；(2) 哪些位置需要重新掩码。策略梯度自然分解为 token 项和 masking 项，联合优化两者。

Result: 在 GSM8K 上达到 87.1%，在 MBPP 上达到 53.4%，均达到 SOTA 水平。

Conclusion: 联合优化 token 预测和 masking 策略能显著提升 MDLM 在推理和代码生成任务上的性能，为扩散语言模型的强化学习优化提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 Masked Diffusion Language Models (MDLMs) 的强化学习优化，MDLM 属于连续语言模型（扩散语言模型）范畴，与 canonical subtopic "连续语言模型与语言流" 直接相关。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model RL training, a top priority subtopic, with strong math/code results. Signals: continuous language model, policy gradient for MDLM, two-stage action MDP, SOTA on GSM8K and MBPP, RL for diffusion language models

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has proven effective for improving reasoning in large language models, but extending it to Masked Diffusion Language Models (MDLMs) remains challenging due to the intractability of the log-likelihood estimation. Existing approaches approximate this log-likelihood by modeling only the token predictions, ignoring the order in which positions are unmasked during generation. We observe that MDLM generation involves two decisions at each step: what tokens to place at each masked position and which positions to remask. We formalize this as a two-stage action MDP, showing that the policy gradient naturally decomposes into a token term and a masking term. Combining optimization of both terms leads to state-of-the-art outcomes on mathematical reasoning and coding benchmarks, with scores of 87.1% on GSM8K and 53.4% on MBPP.

</details>

### [5] [Semantic Audio-driven Understanding for Dynamic Humanoid Whole Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14182)
*J. M. A. Marcelo, M. Brienza, E. Bugli, L. Comito, D. Nardi, D. D. Bloisi, V. Suriani*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (54.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多模态编排框架，通过语义音频驱动仿人机器人实时自主选择和执行运动技能。系统将连续音频流分为音乐和语音分支：音乐通过音频指纹和语义嵌入实现曲目识别与时序对齐，语音则映射到模仿学习技能库。两者共享统一接口调度强化学习控制策略，在Unitree G1仿人机器人上验证了仿真到真实的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿人机器人运动策略大多依赖预编程序列或外部触发行为，缺乏对动态环境的自主响应能力。本文旨在通过语义音频驱动实现机器人对音乐和语音指令的实时自主运动选择与控制，提升机器人的自主性和交互响应能力。

Method: 提出多模态编排框架：1) 音频流经分类路由到音乐或语音分支；2) 音乐分支使用音频指纹和语义嵌入进行曲目识别与时序对齐，动态映射音乐片段到运动策略；3) 语音分支将语音指令映射到模仿学习技能库；4) 两种模态共享统一接口，通过强化学习控制管线调度技能执行。

Result: 在仿真环境和Unitree G1仿人机器人上验证了方法的有效性，展示了鲁棒的仿真到真实迁移能力以及一致的音频条件策略选择性能。

Conclusion: 本文证明了语义音频驱动的多模态编排框架能够有效实现仿人机器人的自主运动控制，支持音乐和语音两种模态的实时交互，为提升机器人自主性和环境响应能力提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于音频驱动的仿人机器人全身运动控制，属于具身智能中机器人动作规划与控制的范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]这一规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on humanoid control and audio-driven skills, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: humanoid whole body control, semantic audio-driven, reinforcement learning, sim-to-real transfer, multimodal orchestration

Abstract: Recent advances in humanoid robotics and reinforcement learning have enabled the acquisition of highly expressive whole-body motion policies. However, most robotic performances remain based on pre-scripted sequences or externally triggered behaviors, limiting autonomy and responsiveness to dynamic environments. In this work, we introduce a novel multi-modal orchestration framework for semantic audio-driven humanoid control, enabling robots to autonomously select and execute appropriate motion skills in real time. The system processes continuous audio streams and routes them into music or speech branches. Music input is handled via audio fingerprinting and semantic embeddings to retrieve track identity and temporal alignment, allowing dynamic mapping between musical segments and motion policies. Speech input is grounded into a discrete library of imitation-learned skills, enabling direct human-robot interaction. Both modalities share a unified interface that schedules skill execution over a reinforcement learning control pipeline. We validate the approach in simulation and on a Unitree G1 humanoid, showing robust sim-to-real transfer and consistent audio-conditioned policy selection. Abstract: Egocentric videos of human manipulation provide scalable supervision for embodied intelligence, yet existing resources rarely combine low-cost continuous capture, manipulation-level structured annotations, and reusable tools for robot learning. We present Open-AoE, an open, community-oriented egocentric manipulation dataset and toolchain spanning the full pipeline from smartphone capture to model training. Its first release contains approximately 2,000 hours of manipulation video collected in natural environments by 500+ contributors using 400+ smartphones. The dataset provides text annotations, MANO-based hand poses, camera trajectories, and temporally localized atomic actions. Open-AoE further includes a data processing pipeline that transforms raw recordings into structured samples through temporal action segmentation, semantic annotation, hand reconstruction, and camera trajectory reconstruction. Meanwhile, we provide a separate downstream toolchain supports visualization, cross-embodiment retargeting, model-specific data conversion, and training recipes for VLA policies, WAMs, and World Models. By integrating scalable capture, structured processing, and downstream adaptation, Open-AoE reduces the barriers to both data contribution and reuse, providing practical open infrastructure for embodied model training, human-to-robot transfer, and world modeling.

TL;DR: LIFT提出了一种力感知的后训练框架，通过在预训练VLA策略中注入6D末端执行器力反馈（通过因果力记忆和零初始化交叉注意力），并结合在线DAgger循环，使策略在接触式操作（如叠毛巾、插书、汉诺塔）中具备反应能力，相比纯视觉后训练收敛更快、性能更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的VLA策略主要依赖视觉信息，在接触式操作中面临遮挡、深度模糊和力误差等问题，导致策略偏离离线演示分布。现有方法缺乏对接触反馈的有效利用，因此需要一种能注入力反馈的后训练框架来增强接触鲁棒性。

Method: LIFT框架包含三个关键设计：(1) 在原始动作专家旁嫁接一个反应式动作专家，从预训练权重初始化；(2) 通过因果力记忆和零初始化交叉注意力注入最近6D末端执行器力，使动作可在执行中刷新；(3) 结合在线DAgger循环，在离线任务对齐数据和人工纠正的在线 rollout 混合数据上训练，解决接触反馈的策略依赖分布偏移问题。

Result: 在叠毛巾、插书和汉诺塔放置三个接触式操作任务上，LIFT比纯视觉后训练学习更快且达到更高性能。消融实验表明，反应式力记忆和在线纠正数据对鲁棒接触操作均至关重要。

Conclusion: LIFT证明了在VLA策略后训练中注入力反馈的有效性，为接触式操作提供了一种实用的增强方案，同时保留了预训练策略的通用操作知识。

Classification reason: 该论文提出LIFT框架，在预训练的VLA（视觉-语言-动作）策略基础上，通过力反馈注入和在线DAgger训练增强接触式操作的鲁棒性，属于具身智能中机器人操作与策略学习范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotics force injection for VLA, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Robotics, Force feedback, VLA post-training, DAgger, Contact-rich manipulation

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policies provide strong language-conditioned manipulation knowledge, but they remain largely vision-driven and can struggle once manipulation enters contact states where the scene is occluded, depth is ambiguous, or small force errors push execution off the offline demonstration distribution. We present LIFT (Late Reactive Injection of Force for VLA Post-Training), a force-aware post-training framework that adds contact reactivity to a pretrained VLA policy while preserving its general manipulation knowledge. LIFT grafts a reactive action expert beside the original action expert, initializes it from pretrained action weights, and injects recent 6D end-effector force through causal force memory and zero-initialized cross attention, enabling actions to be refreshed during execution. To address the policy-dependent distribution shift of contact feedback, LIFT further couples reactive force injection with an online DAgger loop that trains on a mixture of offline task-alignment data and human-corrected online rollouts. Across towel folding, book insertion, and Hanoi ring placement, LIFT learns faster and reaches higher performance than vision-only post-training, while ablations show that reactive force memory and online corrective data are both important for robust contact-rich manipulation. Our code and data will be publicly available.

</details>

TL;DR: MEMORA 提出了一种具身动作记忆（EAM）框架，通过第一人称视频构建环境、实体、活动和推断知识四类记忆存储，结合在线编辑与离线整合机制，为机器人长时程规划提供持久记忆上下文。在 EPIC-KITCHENS 扩展数据集上的实验表明，该方法显著提升了记忆评估准确率和分布外规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人长时程规划不仅需要预测动作的即时效果，还需要对过往具身经验的记忆支持。现有方法缺乏对第一人称视频中环境、实体状态变化、操作流程和参与者规律等结构化经验的持久记忆机制。

Method: 提出 Embodied Action Memory (EAM) 框架，包含形成-整合-检索生命周期和四类记忆存储（环境记忆、实体记忆、活动记忆、推断知识）。在线编辑模块维护物体身份和状态历史，离线整合模块将重复经验抽象为可复用流程和参与者特定规律。基于 EPIC-KITCHENS-100 扩展视频构建 MEMORA-Bench 评测基准。

Result: 在四个开源语言模型上，完整 MEMORA 框架在记忆评估任务上准确率提升最高达 20.5 个百分点，在分布外机器人规划任务上得分相对提升 16.6%。双任务机器人部署实验验证了记忆增强语言规划与下游控制的可行性。

Conclusion: 可编辑、可整合的具身记忆能够为机器人规划提供有效的上下文支持，是连接第一人称视频经验与长时程决策的关键组件。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Embodied Action Memory (EAM)，通过第一人称视频构建具身经验记忆（环境、实体、活动、推断知识），用于机器人长时程推理与规划。这直接属于世界模型方向下的具身世界模型与规划控制（embodied_world_model）这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Strong on world models and embodied planning, but lacks direct connection to your priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: world model, embodied action memory, egocentric video, long-horizon planning, EPIC-KITCHENS benchmark

Abstract: Long-horizon robot planning requires more than predicting what actions will do next; it also requires memory of the embodied experience that makes future goals interpretable. People do not plan from the present scene alone: they draw on remembered places, object-state changes, prior procedures, and regularities revealed through repeated action. We formulate Embodied Action Memory (EAM) as the capability to form, maintain, and use such experience as a persistent memory state for later decisions. MEMORA realizes EAM with a formation-consolidation-retrieval lifecycle and four typed stores: Environment Memory, Entity Memory, Activity Memory, and Inferred Knowledge. Online editing maintains object identities and state histories as new observations arrive; offline consolidation abstracts repeated experience into reusable procedures and participant-specific regularities. MEMORA-Bench evaluates this lifecycle on 45 hours of EPIC-KITCHENS-100 extension video across 18 participants through memory-grounded planning, including previously unseen goals, and a complementary memory-assessment task. Across four open-weight language models, full MEMORA--combining editing, typed stores, and consolidation--achieves the strongest aggregate results among the evaluated memory conditions. It improves memory-assessment accuracy by up to 20.5 points over the strongest controlled baseline and improves out-of-distribution Robot-Grounded Plan score by up to 16.6% relative. A qualitative two-task robot deployment study further illustrates how memory-grounded language plans can interface with downstream control, while the overall results show that editable, consolidated memory can supply remembered context for robot planning. </details>

*Hanyi Zhang, Khang Nguyen, Charith Munasinghe, Basu Hela, Tianyu Li, Zihong Luo, Hoan Nguyen, Hans Wernher van de Venn, Yalin Zheng, Ravi Prakash, Tung D. Ta, Anh Nguyen, Baoru Huang*

TL;DR: 本文提出 GCA-Bench，一个面向复杂机器人抓取任务的基准，涵盖场景级推理与语义约束的抓取场景，用于评估大模型在抓取从检测到执行全流程中的能力。实验表明现有方法在复杂场景下成功率低于70%，揭示了当前方法的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取基准主要关注孤立的、基于视觉的抓取姿态检测，未能捕捉需要多步推理和语义理解的复杂抓取任务，缺乏对从检测到执行全流程的评估。

Method: 提出 GCA-Bench 基准，包含需要场景级推理和语义约束的"复杂动作抓取"场景；设计新评估指标；在统一设置下评估多种基线方法（从传统抓取检测到端到端学习方法）。

Result: 在复杂抓取场景下，现有方法成功率低于70%，揭示了关键局限性；分析了典型失败模式。

Conclusion: GCA-Bench 有效揭示了现有抓取方法在复杂场景下的不足，为开发更鲁棒和可泛化的抓取策略提供了指导。

Classification reason: 该论文提出机器人抓取基准 GCA-Bench，关注复杂抓取任务中的场景级推理与语义约束，属于具身智能中的机器人抓取与规划控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic grasping benchmark, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: robotic grasping benchmark, complex action scenarios, scene-level reasoning, semantic constraints, large model evaluation

Abstract: Robust robotic grasping remains a fundamental challenge for complex real-world applications. Recent advances in large-scale models demonstrate promising capabilities for reasoning in robotic tasks. However, existing benchmarks for grasping primarily focus on isolated, visual-based grasp pose detection, failing to capture the complexity of grasping tasks that require multi-step reasoning and semantic understanding during execution. To address this gap, we propose GCA-Bench, a benchmark featuring challenging \textit{grasping with complex action} scenarios that involve both scene-level reasoning and semantic constraints. GCA-Bench enables the evaluation of recent large foundation models under the same settings. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our new benchmark, we implement a diverse set of baselines, ranging from traditional grasp detection pipelines to end-to-end learning methods. Empirical studies achieve success rates below 70\% on complex grasping scenarios, underscoring critical limitations. In addition, we propose new evaluation metrics, analyze critical failure models, and provide insights to guide the development of more robust and generalizable grasping strategies.

</details>


### [10] [ConFlow: Constraints-Guided Learning with Flow Matching for Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14424)
*Nutan Chen, Jianxiang Feng, Marvin Alles, Botond Cseke*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ConFlow 提出一种约束引导的流匹配框架，通过在训练目标中引入可微约束函数（如障碍函数、代价函数）和条件高斯过程替代标准高斯源分布，将任务约束直接融入训练过程，并在双机器人导航任务中验证了其相比标准流匹配基线更低的碰撞率和更高的轨迹质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配方法在机器人运动生成中通常仅在数据上训练，任务约束通过推理时引导（inference-time guidance）施加，导致训练与推理之间存在不一致。作者希望将约束信息直接融入训练目标，以缩小这一差距。

Method: 提出 ConFlow 框架：(1) 在流匹配训练目标中引入可微的障碍函数或代价函数，将约束信息直接加入回归损失；(2) 用条件高斯过程（Conditional Gaussian Process）替代标准高斯源分布，以编码平滑性和边界条件等设计规范；(3) 利用不可行演示作为负监督信号，提升约束满足能力，无需额外专家数据。

Result: 在双机器人导航任务上，ConFlow 相比标准流匹配基线（无论是否使用推理时引导）均实现了更低的碰撞率和更高的轨迹质量。

Conclusion: 训练时约束集成是弥合生成运动模型中训练-推理差距的有效方法，ConFlow 在约束满足和轨迹质量方面均优于现有方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究机器人运动生成中的约束引导流匹配方法，属于机器人动作规划与控制领域，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot motion generation with constraints, not directly aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: flow matching for motion generation, constraint-guided training, robot navigation, collision avoidance, conditional Gaussian process

Abstract: In recent years Flow Matching has become a prominent method for generative modeling robot motion generation. In its generic form Flow Matching is an ODE-based neural sampler that is trained by regressing empirical flow fields associated with motion samples as data. However, in robot motion generation we often have additional constraints that might not be present in the collected data. The majority of current approaches train the flow on the available data and use inference-time guidance to enforce task-specific constraints. To address this mismatch, we propose \textbf{ConFlow}, a constraint-guided flow matching framework that incorporates constraint information directly into the training objective via differentiable barrier or cost functions. To address design specifications such as smoothness and boundary conditions, we propose replacing the standard Gaussian source distribution used in flow matching training with a conditional Gaussian Process. Our approach also uses infeasible demonstrations as negative supervision, improving constraint satisfaction without requiring additional expert data. Experiments on a two-robot navigation task demonstrate that ConFlow achieves lower collision rates and higher trajectory quality than standard flow matching baselines, with or without inference-time guidance. These results validate training-time constraint integration as an effective approach to closing the training--inference gap in generative motion models.

</details>


### [11] [Motion Planning with Model-Based Diffusion via Constraint Optimization and Adaptive Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14455)
*Zhilin He, Bowei Li, Jianlin Dou, Yuner Zhang, Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (52.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MD-COAS，一种基于模型的扩散运动规划方法，通过统一软约束扩散先验（iALM）与硬投影算子（CFS），并自适应调度安全约束与扩散过程，在高度非凸约束环境中实现更安全、更高效的单机器人运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的扩散方法在约束环境中进行运动规划时，要么仅使用软可行性扩散先验，要么仅使用硬投影算子，缺乏统一框架；同时安全约束的施加是固定的，忽略了扩散调度的动态变化，导致在复杂非凸约束下性能受限。

Method: 提出 MD-COAS 框架，将不精确增广拉格朗日方法（iALM）作为软扩散先验与基于凸可行集（CFS）的硬投影算子统一结合，并引入自适应调度机制，在扩散过程中动态调整安全约束强度与扩散调度参数，实现联合优化。

Result: 在随机生成的高度非凸 2D 基准测试和 7-DoF 机械臂避障任务上，MD-COAS 相比基线规划器实现了更高的安全性与成功率、更快的收敛速度和更低的最终轨迹代价。

Conclusion: 统一软硬约束并自适应调度扩散过程能有效提升基于模型的扩散运动规划在复杂约束环境下的性能，为机器人安全运动规划提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究单机器人运动规划（SRMP），使用基于模型的扩散方法进行轨迹优化，涉及机器人运动规划、约束优化与安全控制，属于具身智能/机器人领域的规划与控制，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot motion planning, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: motion planning, model-based diffusion, constraint optimization, robotics, not user priority

Abstract: Single-Robot Motion Planning (SRMP) in highly non-convex constrained environments, where robots must satisfy collision-free guarantees, dynamic feasibility, and task-related constraints, is challenging under complex constraints and computational limits. Recent Model-Based Diffusion (MBD) approaches recast the SRMP as trajectory optimization that samples from a posterior over trajectories, using known dynamics, and analytically estimates the score function from rollout samples to guide diffusion denoising toward a low-cost, clean trajectory without demonstration learning. While existing works further adapt MBD to constrained environments and showcase promising performance, they are still limited by (1) enforcing safety either via soft feasibility diffusion priors or hard projection operators, but lack a unified framework to integrate both, and (2) fixing safety enforcement to neglect the changing of diffusion scheduling. Therefore, we introduce Model-Based Diffusion via Constraint Optimization and Adaptive Scheduling (MD-COAS) for SRMP that unifies the inexact Augmented Lagrangian Method (iALM) soft diffusion prior with a Convex Feasible Set (CFS)-based hard projection operator, and adaptively schedules and co-optimizes safety enforcement, along with diffusion scheduling. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves higher safety \& success rates, faster convergence, and lower final costs than baseline planners on randomly generated highly non-convex 2D benchmarks and a 7-DoF robot arm avoidance task.

</details>


### [12] [DRIFT: Drift and Aggregation for Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14507)
*Yining Xing, Zhiyuan Liu, Zehong Ke, Wenhao Yu, Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (56.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DRIFT提出一种固定深度的轨迹规划器，在紧凑的轨迹潜空间中通过一步漂移生成多假设提议，再通过轻量聚合头直接预测最终轨迹，在NAVSIM navtest上达到89.6 PDMS，推理仅需66.43ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端轨迹规划器需要在实时约束下表示多种合理的驾驶行为并生成单一可执行轨迹。现有基于提议的方法虽能生成多个候选，但如何将提议集转化为最终规划仍是一个关键设计问题。

Method: DRIFT包含一个DRIFT解码器，在紧凑的轨迹潜空间中一步生成48个提议特征（32个alpha=0.5，16个alpha=0.9），再通过轻量聚合头结合场景、导航和自车状态信息直接预测最终轨迹。训练使用专家轨迹模仿损失和地图导出的边界正则化项。

Result: 在NAVSIM navtest上，DRIFT达到89.6 PDMS和90.4 EPDMS，在可行驶区域合规性和自车进度方面表现优异。提议生成与聚合模块在RTX 4090上仅需10.82ms，完整模型推理（含视觉骨干）为66.43ms。

Conclusion: 一步潜空间提议生成与直接聚合为多假设运动规划提供了一种高效的设计范式，在实时性和规划质量之间取得了良好平衡。

Classification reason: 该论文提出DRIFT，一种端到端轨迹规划器，在紧凑的轨迹潜空间中结合一步漂移与场景感知的提议聚合，面向自动驾驶运动规划。这属于具身智能/机器人领域的动作规划与控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]（embodied_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on motion planning for autonomous driving, which is outside the user's core priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: motion planning, trajectory prediction, autonomous driving, latent space, efficient inference

Abstract: End-to-end trajectory planners need to represent multiple plausible driving behaviors while producing a single executable trajectory under real-time constraints. Proposal-based approaches address this ambiguity by generating multiple candidates, but converting the proposal set into a final plan remains a key design problem. We present DRIFT, a fixed-depth planner that combines one-step drifting in a compact trajectory latent space with scene-aware proposal aggregation. Conditioned on features from a pretrained visual encoder, the DRIFT Decoder generates 48 proposal features in a single batched pass, with 32 samples at alpha=0.5 and 16 samples at alpha=0.9. A lightweight Aggregation Head integrates these features with scene, navigation, and ego-state information and directly predicts the final trajectory without requiring trajectory-level quality labels for aggregation. Its output is trained with expert-trajectory imitation and a map-derived boundary regularizer that penalizes waypoints outside the drivable polygon and inside waypoints near its boundary. On NAVSIM navtest, DRIFT achieves 89.6 PDMS and 90.4 EPDMS, with strong drivable-area compliance and ego progress among the methods compared. The proposal-generation and aggregation module runs in 10.82 ms on an NVIDIA RTX 4090, while full-model inference including the visual backbone takes 66.43 ms. These results show that one-step latent proposal generation and direct aggregation provide an efficient design for multi-hypothesis motion planning.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过文本序列化将3D场景信息传递给VLM，存在表征鸿沟（representation gap），导致导航性能受限。作者希望通过直接将3D场景token注入VLM隐空间来弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: SoftNav使用3D编码器提取实体级连续表示（每个检测物体或前沿生成一个token），通过轻量投影器将这些软token注入冻结的VLM隐空间。3D编码器和VLM均冻结，仅训练投影器，约1200样本和17M参数。

Result: 在HM3D-OVON三个split上分别达到74.2%/68.3%/66.7% SR，超越所有先前方法；零样本迁移到GOAT-Bench（67.2% SR）、SG3D（47.2% s-SR）和真实机器人部署，无需重新训练或架构修改。

Conclusion: 直接将3D场景token注入VLM能够弥合表征鸿沟，实现高效、可迁移的具身导航，仅需极少量训练数据即可取得优异性能。

Classification reason: 该论文研究具身导航任务，将3D场景信息注入VLM用于目标导向导航，属于具身智能中的规划与控制，与[CANONICAL]具身世界模型与规划控制高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly injects 3D continuous tokens into VLM hidden space for embodied navigation, aligning with world model and continuous multimodal priorities. Signals: 3D continuous representation as soft tokens, world model for embodied navigation, minimal training data and parameters, zero-shot transfer to real robot, bridges representation gap for VLMs

Abstract: In goal-directed embodied navigation, where an agent must locate a specified target in an unseen environment, 3D scene understanding and navigation reasoning must work in concert. Current approaches transmit 3D scene information to vision-language models (VLMs) through text, suggesting a representation gap in our tested configurations; a controlled ablation confirms that direct embedding-level transfer significantly outperforms the evaluated text serialization formats. We introduce SoftNav, which injects entity-level 3D continuous representations -- one token per detected object or frontier -- into a VLM's hidden space as soft tokens through a lightweight projector. With the 3D encoder and VLM frozen, only ~1,200 samples and ~17M trainable parameters are needed. On HM3D-OVON, SoftNav achieves 74.2%/68.3%/66.7% SR across three splits, surpassing all prior methods in both SR and SPL; the same navigation policy transfers zero-shot to GOAT-Bench (67.2% SR), SG3D (47.2% s-SR), and real-world robot deployment without retraining or architectural modification. Injecting 3D scene tokens directly into VLMs bridges the representation gap, enabling transferable navigation with minimal training.

</details>


### [14] [Reflex: Real-Time VLA Control through Streaming Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14695)
*Yuanchun Guo, Bingyan Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (92.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Reflex 是一个面向流匹配 VLA 模型的实时流式推理框架，利用时间步不变性将注意力上下文划分为静态/滑动/动态区域，实现 O(1) 增量缓存更新；引入 AdaRMSNorm 防止 BFloat16 数值崩溃；通过异步流水线和算子融合最大化吞吐量。在 LIBERO 和 Kinetix 上实现 2.58 倍加速和 50Hz 稳定流式推理，延迟降低 54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配 VLA 模型虽能实现精确的连续控制，但其迭代去噪特性与实时机器人部署存在根本性矛盾：全局时间步注入使 KV-cache 失效，导致要么 O(N²) 重计算，要么数学上不正确的缓存复用。现有方法无法在保持精度的同时实现实时流式推理。

Method: 1) 发现时间步不变性（Timestep-Invariance Property）：感知编码器功能上独立于去噪循环；2) 将注意力上下文划分为静态区域（视觉 token）、滑动区域（历史动作 token）和动态区域（当前去噪 token），实现 O(1) 增量缓存更新；3) 提出 AdaRMSNorm，通过流相位门控防止 BFloat16 数值崩溃；4) 异步流水线解耦视觉编码与动作生成，结合算子融合减少内核开销。

Result: 在 LIBERO 和 Kinetix 基准上，Reflex 实现 2.58 倍推理加速和 50Hz 稳定流式推理，反应延迟降低 54%，且无性能退化。

Conclusion: Reflex 通过利用时间步不变性和精心设计的缓存策略，首次实现了流匹配 VLA 策略的实时流式推理，为具身智能中连续控制模型的实时部署提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Reflex 框架，通过流匹配（flow matching）实现视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的实时流式推理，面向机器人具身控制。核心贡献在于解决流匹配策略在实时机器人部署中的推理效率问题，属于具身智能中动作规划与控制的范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for continuous VLA models, a core priority subtopic with high weight. Signals: continuous language model, efficient inference, world model, flow matching, real-time streaming

Abstract: Flow matching Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models promise precise continuous control, but their iterative denoising nature introduces fundamental incompatibilities with real-time robotics: global timestep injection invalidates KV-caching, forcing a choice between slow $O(N^2)$ re-computation or mathematically incorrect cache reuse. We present \textbf{Reflex}, a framework that enables \textit{real-time streaming inference} for flow matching policies by exploiting the \textit{Timestep-Invariance Property} -- that perception encoders are functionally independent of the denoising loop. Reflex partitions the attention context into static, sliding, and dynamic regions, enabling $O(1)$ incremental cache updates while preserving full-batch-equivalent attention outputs for fixed inputs. To ensure stability under continuous high-frequency inference, we introduce \textit{AdaRMSNorm}, an adaptive normalization layer that prevents BFloat16 numerical collapse by gating on flow phase. We further maximize throughput through an \textit{async pipeline} that decouples visual encoding from action generation, combined with \textit{operator fusion} that reduces kernel overhead. On LIBERO and Kinetix benchmarks, Reflex achieves a 2.58$\times$ inference speedup and 50Hz stable streaming, reducing reaction latency by up to 54\% and enabling efficient deployment without performance degradation.

</details>

*Xinzhe Zhou, Xuyang Wang, Xiaoming Duan, Jianping He*

TL;DR: BridgeFlow 提出了一种快速且严格 SE(2)-等变的生成式运动规划框架，通过轻量级任务中心规范化模块实现精确空间等变性，结合布朗桥先验与上下文感知小批量最优传输加速推理，在密集2D环境和7-DoF Franka机械臂上相比扩散基线实现最高15倍推理加速和2倍有效轨迹率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于学习的运动规划器面临两难：要么缺乏内在等变性（将变换后的任务视为新场景），要么通过计算昂贵的专用架构强制等变性（瓶颈实时推理）。作者希望打破这一权衡，实现快速且严格等变的运动规划。

Method: 提出BridgeFlow框架：(1) 轻量级任务中心规范化模块实现精确SE(2)空间等变性，允许使用标准架构；(2) 布朗桥信息先验 + 上下文感知小批量最优传输，构建拉直的向量场，最小化传输成本并稳定训练；(3) 通过无分类器引导显式嵌入环境感知。

Result: 在密集2D环境和7-DoF Franka机械臂上，BridgeFlow相比SOTA扩散基线实现最高15倍推理加速和2倍有效轨迹率提升，并对完全未见的环境和任意空间变换展现出鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: BridgeFlow成功打破了等变性与推理效率之间的权衡，证明了通过轻量级规范化模块实现严格等变性并结合flow matching加速推理的有效性，为实时机器人运动规划提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于flow matching的SE(2)-等变运动规划框架BridgeFlow，面向机器人运动规划（7-DoF机械臂操控），属于具身智能中的规划与控制，因此映射到[具身世界模型与规划控制]。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic motion planning with SE(2)-equivariance, which is outside the user's priority subtopics of continuous language/multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: SE(2)-equivariant motion planning, flow matching for robotics, Brownian bridge prior, 15x inference speedup, 7-DoF Franka manipulator

Abstract: In robotic motion planning, equivariance to rigid body transformations is crucial for robust spatial generalization. However, current learning-based planners face a critical dilemma: they either lack inherent equivariance, treating transformed tasks as novel scenarios, or enforce it via computationally expensive specialized architectures that bottleneck real-time inference. To break this trade-off, we propose BridgeFlow, a fast and strictly SE(2)-equivariant generative motion planning framework. Rather than relying on heavy equivariant networks, BridgeFlow achieves exact spatial equivariance via a lightweight task-centric canonicalization module, enabling generalization using standard architectures. To further accelerate inference, we pair a Brownian bridge informative prior with context-aware mini-batch optimal transport. This constructs a straightened vector field that minimizes transport costs and stabilizes training. Furthermore, environmental awareness is explicitly embedded via Classifier-Free Guidance. Evaluations in dense 2D environments and on a 7-DoF Franka manipulator demonstrate that BridgeFlow achieves up to a 15x inference speedup and a 2x higher valid trajectory rate over state-of-the-art diffusion baselines, alongside robust generalization to entirely unseen environments and arbitrary spatial transformations.

</details>


### [16] [Towards Human-like Physical Intelligence: LifelongVision-Language-Action Learning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14852)
*Yao He, Gan Sun, Wenqi Liang, Fazeng Li, Yang Cong*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (59.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 LifelongVLA，一种面向机器人操作的缓存高效终身视觉-语言-动作学习框架，通过双时间尺度 LoRA 门控模块（短期适配器用于可塑性、长期适配器用于稳定性）和缓存高效随机回放策略，缓解可塑性-稳定性权衡问题，在 xArm 机器人上实现高效技能扩展与旧任务保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在开放世界部署时缺乏终身学习能力，无法顺序学习新任务；而现有终身学习方法在机器人操作中未能有效解决可塑性（学习新任务）与稳定性（保持旧任务精度）之间的权衡问题。

Method: 提出 LifelongVLA 框架，包含：(1) 双时间尺度 LoRA 门控模块，将 VLA 适配分解为短期适配器（可塑性）和长期适配器（稳定巩固），通过任务感知门控显式控制权衡；(2) 缓存高效随机回放策略，无需存储完整轨迹即可保留更均衡的保留信号。

Result: 实验表明 LifelongVLA 在多个基线方法上取得更优性能，实现了高效的技能扩展、对已学操作行为的鲁棒保持，并在 xArm 机器人上降低了实际部署中对重训练的依赖。

Conclusion: LifelongVLA 通过双时间尺度适配与缓存高效回放有效缓解了机器人操作中的可塑性-稳定性权衡，为 VLA 模型的终身学习提供了一种轻量级、可部署的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 LifelongVLA，面向机器人操作（robotic manipulation）的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）终身学习框架，属于具身智能与机器人规划控制范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on lifelong learning for robotic manipulation, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Lifelong learning, VLA, robotic manipulation, plasticity-stability trade-off, LoRA gating

Abstract: Similar to the natural capabilities of humans to sequentially learn new tasks, robots with Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models should possess lifelong learning ability to learn a new task when deployed in open-world environments. However, most recently proposed lifelong learning models aim to effectively learn the current task (plasticity) or maintain high accuracy on previous tasks (stability), while the plasticity-stability trade-off remains largely unsolved in robotic manipulation models. To address this fundamental challenge, we propose a cache-efficient lifelong Vision-Language-Action learning framework for robotic manipulation (i.e., LifelongVLA), which alleviates the plasticity-stability trade-off with a dual-timescale adaptation mechanism while achieving low-cost robotic deployment with a cache-efficient replay strategy. More concretely, we propose a dual-timescale LoRA gating module to decompose VLA adaptation into two lightweight pathways: a short-term adapter for plasticity and a long-term adapter for stable consolidation. These pathways are integrated via a task-aware gate, enabling explicit control of the plasticity-stability trade-off. In the skill replay phase, a cache-efficient stochastic replay strategy is proposed to preserve more balanced retention signals without full-trajectory storage. Finally, experiments show that LifelongVLA outperforms existing baselines, demonstrating efficient skill expansion, robust retention of learned manipulation behaviors, and reduced reliance on retraining for real-world deployment on an xArm robot.

</details>


### [17] [Steering Robustness into World Action Models via Mechanistic Interpretability and Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14943)
*Jihoon Hong, Julian Skifstad, Qiyue Dai, Alice Chan, Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.LG, eess.SY, math.OC, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (71.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文利用机械可解释性分析世界动作模型（WAM）在分布偏移下的鲁棒性表征，发现部分 WAM 架构在激活空间中具有低维线性可分的鲁棒性特征，据此提出无训练的对比激活方向干预方法，并进一步利用 WAM 激活动力学的局部线性性，设计基于模型最优控制的 WA-LQR 控制器，在 Cosmos-Policy 和 DiT4DiT 模型上显著提升了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAM）在语义和物理层面支持机器人控制，但在分布偏移下表现脆弱。现有方法缺乏对 WAM 内部表征如何编码鲁棒性相关信息的理解，也缺少轻量级、无需重新训练即可提升鲁棒性的干预手段。

Method: 1) 通过机械可解释性分析，对比成功与失败 rollout 的激活模式，发现部分 WAM 架构（如 Cosmos-Policy, DiT4DiT）在激活空间中存在低维线性可分的鲁棒性特征，而另一些（如 LingBot-VA）则不具备。2) 基于此提出对比激活方向（contrastive activation directions）进行无训练干预。3) 进一步利用 WAM 激活动力学的局部线性性，设计 World-Action Linear Quadratic Regulator (WA-LQR)，一种最小侵入性的降阶 LQR 控制器，通过模型预测控制进行反馈 steering。

Result: 机械可解释性评估预测 Cosmos-Policy 和 DiT4DiT 具有强可干预性，LingBot-VA 弱可干预性，与后续 steering 实验结果一致。WA-LQR 在 Cosmos-Policy 和 DiT4DiT 上泛化对比方向到新任务，并在相机、夹爪和视觉噪声扰动下显著优于未干预和 prompt steering 基线。

Conclusion: 机械可解释性可以有效预测 WAM 的可干预性，基于最优控制的 WA-LQR 是一种高效、泛化性强的 WAM 鲁棒性提升方法，为将可解释性分析与控制理论结合提升世界模型鲁棒性提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 论文研究 World Action Models (WAMs) 在分布偏移下的鲁棒性控制，使用机械可解释性进行激活空间分析，并提出基于最优控制的 WA-LQR 控制器。这属于面向机器人控制的世界模型研究，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model robustness via mechanistic interpretability and optimal control, aligning with top user priorities. Signals: world model robustness, mechanistic interpretability, optimal control steering, distribution shift, Cosmos-Policy

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) enable semantically- and physically-informed control but are brittle under distribution shift. In this work, we use mechanistic interpretability to study how robustness-relevant perturbations are represented in WAM activation space. Comparing activations across successful and unsuccessful rollouts, we find some WAM architectures exhibit low-dimensional linear separability for robustness-critical features, while others do not. This motivates the use of contrastive activation directions for training-free WAM steering. We also show that local linearity in WAM activation dynamics enables efficient feedback steering via model-based optimal control, yielding World-Action Linear Quadratic Regulator (WA-LQR), a minimally-invasive reduced-order LQR controller. Via mechanistic evaluations, we predict strong steerability in the Cosmos-Policy and DiT4DiT models but weak steerability in LingBot-VA, consistent with steering intervention results. On Cosmos-Policy and DiT4DiT, WA-LQR generalizes contrastive directions to new tasks and improves robustness to camera, gripper, and visual-noise perturbations over unsteered and prompt steering baselines.

</details>


### [18] [AeroAct: Action-Centered World-Action Models for Language-Conditioned Quadrotor Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14997)
*Xinhong Zhang, Qiyuan Zhu, Yubo Huang, Haolin Chen, Runqing Wang, Yuhao Mo, Zhongxin Chen, Yu Hu, Xinjiang Wang, Jian Sun, Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: AeroAct 是首个在真实四旋翼飞行中实例化的世界-动作模型（WAM），它基于预训练视频扩散 Transformer，从第一人称视觉历史、本体感知和语言指令预测局部轨迹-动作块，利用未来帧作为密集后果监督进行训练，部署时直接解码动作，无需生成未来视频。论文还构建了 DiffAero 数据管线、低成本手持采集设备和自引导过程，在闭环仿真和真实实验中验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言条件四旋翼飞行方法通常使用离散动作、高层航点或瞬时速度指令，缺乏对飞行动作如何改变未来观测的密集监督。作者希望构建一个能预测动作对未来观测影响的世界模型，同时保持动作平滑且可动态执行。

Method: 将预训练视频扩散 Transformer 适配为世界-动作模型（WAM），输入第一人称视觉历史、本体感知和语言指令，输出局部轨迹-动作块。训练时使用未来第一人称帧作为密集后果监督，推理时直接解码动作。构建 DiffAero 数据管线（结合 Isaac Lab 和 3DGS 渲染器），并引入低成本手持采集设备和自引导过程提升时序一致性。

Result: 闭环仿真和真实实验表明，时序视觉上下文提升了目标跟踪和物体搜索性能，基于 WAM 的策略可在物理四旋翼上执行。

Conclusion: AeroAct 首次将世界-动作模型成功应用于真实四旋翼飞行，验证了利用未来帧作为密集监督训练动作预测的有效性，为语言条件无人机导航提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 AeroAct，一个面向四旋翼无人机导航的动作中心世界-动作模型（WAM），将预训练视频扩散 Transformer 适配为从第一人称视觉历史、本体感知和语言指令预测局部轨迹-动作块，属于机器人/具身智能中的世界模型用于规划控制，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Strong world model application in robotics but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world model, video diffusion transformer, trajectory-action chunks, real-world quadrotor, language-conditioned control

Abstract: Language-conditioned quadrotor flight requires a policy to ground semantic goals, anticipate the visual consequences of ego-motion, and output control references that remain smooth and dynamically executable under rapidly changing first-person views. Existing aerial vision-language navigation and vision-language-action methods commonly use discrete actions, high-level waypoints, or instantaneous velocity commands, which provide limited supervision about how flight actions change future observations. We present AeroAct, an action-centered world-action model (WAM) for quadrotor navigation. To the best of our knowledge, AeroAct is the first WAM instantiated and demonstrated for real-world aerial flight. The model adapts a pretrained video diffusion Transformer to predict local trajectory-action chunks from egocentric visual history, proprioception, and language. Future first-person frames are used during training as dense consequence supervision, while deployment directly decodes actions without generating future video. To obtain aligned visual, state, language, and dynamically feasible action data, we build a DiffAero-based pipeline with complementary Isaac Lab and 3D Gaussian splatting renderers. We further introduce a low-cost handheld collection device that couples camera observations with motion estimates to recreate flight-like egocentric trajectories, and a self-guidance procedure that improves temporal consistency across overlapping trajectory chunks. Closed-loop simulation and real-world experiments show that temporal visual context improves target tracking and object-search performance, and that WAM-based policies can be executed on a physical quadrotor.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略在无人机跟踪中，当目标被建筑物、植被等遮挡时性能严重下降，持续遮挡会导致目标丢失且难以重新捕获。需要一种具备空间感知和遮挡恢复能力的 VLA 模型。

Result: 相比 OpenVLA，CosFly-VLA-0.8B 在开环 ADE 上降低 34.1%（seen-test）和 35.3%（unseen-test）；闭环优化使 SR 分别提升 29.8% 和 2.5%。

Conclusion: CosFly-VLA 展示了从可见帧模仿到空间接地动作闭环控制的进步，在无人机遮挡跟踪任务上取得了显著提升。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向无人机（UAV）动态目标跟踪的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，涉及具身智能中的动作规划与控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on UAV tracking with VLA, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: UAV tracking, VLA model, spatial awareness, occlusion handling, curriculum learning

Abstract: Dynamic target tracking is essential for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) operating in complex urban environments, where both the target and the camera viewpoint change continuously. Existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies can track visible targets effectively, but their performance often degrades when buildings, vegetation, or roadside objects block the line of sight. During sustained occlusion, a policy may lose the target state, execute actions toward an incorrect region, and amplify this error through subsequent observations until re-acquisition becomes impossible. To this end, we present CosFly-VLA, a spatially aware VLA model that jointly grounds the target, estimates its visibility, and generates continuous flight actions through a structured prediction interface. To train this policy, we use a large-scale recipe over diverse data sources. Spatially Grounded Continued Pretraining (CPT) on a 500k mixed pool injects UAV-view depth, distance, and 3-D spatial reasoning. A three-stage Curriculum-based Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) process then specializes the tracker through multi-head warm-up followed by two-stage curriculum learning over natural and hard / long-occlusion data. Chain-of-Thought (CoT) training subsequently teaches recovery-oriented reasoning traces before structured answers. Finally, a closed-loop Reinforcement Learning (RL) stage optimizes tracking behavior with a multi-component reward covering stand-off tracking, grounding quality, collision avoidance, and task success. Relative to OpenVLA, CosFly-VLA-0.8B reduces open-loop Average Displacement Error (ADE) by 34.1% on seen-test and 35.3% on unseen-test. Closed-loop optimization improves Success Rate (SR) by 29.8% and 2.5%, respectively. These results demonstrate progress from visible-frame imitation toward spatially grounded action-closed-loop control, evaluated under a shared oracle state history.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机之间的动态物体交换由于感知、时序和接触丰富的交互中的不确定性仍然是一个挑战性问题。人机抛接球作为该问题的极端实例，需要精确的实时协调、带反馈控制的预测性运动规划以及对人类运动变化的鲁棒性。实现此类技能对于推进物理人机交互和共享自主性具有重要意义。

Method: 提出了一种实时规划与控制架构，包含三个核心组件：(1) 预测性球追踪模块，用于估计球的轨迹；(2) 自适应在线轨迹优化，采用多段打靶法（multiple-shooting formulation）进行轨迹优化；(3) 基于状态机的协调逻辑，实现人机同步的多球抛接模式。系统在真实机器人平台上进行了用户研究验证。

Result: 在8名不同技能水平（从初学者到专家）的参与者用户研究中，系统实现了三球级联抛接。所有参与者在10分钟测试内均超过了此前报告的最佳结果。一名参与者将三球级联抛接的记录延长了5倍（20次连续接球），另一名参与者在单球接抛中实现了40次连续接球、100%成功率。

Conclusion: 本文证明了所提出的实时规划与控制架构能够使机器人可靠地与人类伙伴进行多球抛接协作，显著超越了此前的最佳结果。该工作为动态人机交互和共享自主性提供了有效的技术方案。

Abstract: Dynamic object exchange between humans and robots remains a challenging problem due to uncertainty in perception, timing, and contact-rich interaction. Human-robot juggling represents a particularly demanding instance of this problem, requiring precise real-time coordination, predictive motion planning with feedback control, and robustness to variability in human motion. Enabling such skills is of interest for advancing physical human-robot interaction and shared autonomy. We present a real-time planning and control architecture for human-robot partner juggling that enables a robot to reliably catch and throw balls in synchronized multi-ball patterns with a human partner. The system integrates predictive ball tracking, adaptive online trajectory optimization using a multiple-shooting formulation, and a state-machine-based coordination logic to enable synchronized multi-ball human-robot partner juggling. In a user study with 8 participants of varying juggling skill from beginner to expert, we demonstrate that our system can achieve three-ball cascades shared between the robot and the human. All participants exceeded previously reported best-case results within a 10-minute test session, with one participant extending the previous record for shared three-ball cascade juggling fivefold to 20 consecutive robot catches, and another participant achieving a 100% success rate with 40 consecutive catches in a single-ball catch-and-return setting. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基础模型通常仅使用单步或短历史视觉运动上下文，无法有效利用长时间跨度的交互历史。作者希望探索上下文长度作为机器人基础模型的新缩放轴线，通过扩展上下文来解锁新能力，如从人类视频演示中进行一次性上下文模仿、在线策略改进、对扰动的鲁棒性以及长程多阶段任务。

Method: RoboTTT将测试时训练（TTT）集成到VLA策略中，构建一个序列模型，其循环状态由快速权重（fast weights）组成——这些参数在训练和推理过程中通过梯度下降更新，将历史信息压缩到权重空间中，并通过检索上下文信息实现长上下文条件化。为了扩展训练上下文长度，该方法结合了序列动作强制（sequence action forcing）与截断式时间反向传播（truncated BPTT）。

Result: 在真实机器人操作任务上，RoboTTT相比单步上下文基线整体性能提升87%，并完整完成了一个五分钟、十阶段的组装任务（所有基线均无法完成）。8K时间步上下文训练的模型比1K时间步训练的相同模型性能提升62%，验证了上下文长度作为新缩放轴线的有效性。

Conclusion: RoboTTT证明了将测试时训练引入机器人策略可以有效扩展上下文长度，解锁了长程任务执行、人类视频模仿等新能力，并首次展示了上下文长度作为机器人基础模型新缩放轴线的潜力，为未来机器人策略的上下文缩放研究提供了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出RoboTTT，将测试时训练（TTT）集成到机器人基础模型（VLA策略）中，通过梯度下降将历史信息压缩到权重空间，实现长达8K时间步的上下文缩放，用于机器人操作任务。这属于面向机器人、具身智能、动作规划和控制的世界模型，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Abstract: Recent robot foundation models operate with single-step or short-history visuomotor context. We introduce Test-Time-Training Robot Policies (RoboTTT), a robot model and training recipe that scale visuomotor context to 8K timesteps, three orders of magnitude beyond state-of-the-art policies, without growing inference latency. At this context length, we unlock new robot capabilities: one-shot in-context imitation from human video demonstrations, on-the-fly policy improvement, robustness to perturbations, and stronger performance on multi-stage, long-horizon tasks. We also observe, for the first time, steady gains in closed-loop performance as pretraining context length scales. At its core, RoboTTT integrates Test-Time Training into robot foundation models such as Vision-Language-Action policies, yielding a sequence model whose recurrent state consists of fast weights, parameters updated by gradient descent during both training and inference, compressing histories into weight space and retrieving contextual information for long-context conditioning. To scale training context length, the recipe combines sequence action forcing with truncated backpropagation through time. On challenging real-robot manipulation tasks, RoboTTT improves overall performance by 87% over the single-step context baseline and fully completes a five-minute, ten-stage assembly task, which no baseline ever does. RoboTTT trained with 8K-timestep context outperforms the same model pretrained with 1K timesteps by 62%, suggesting context length as a new scaling axis for robot foundation models. Videos are available at

TL;DR: RxBrain 是一个具身认知基础模型，采用统一多模态 Mixture-of-Transformers 架构，将语言推理与视觉想象联合编码为单一规划序列，支持具身任务规划、世界状态预测和连续机器人动作生成，无需大规模动作数据预训练即可实现真实机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能方法中，视觉语言模型侧重场景理解和文本决策，生成式世界模型主要预测未来视觉状态，两者分离。作者希望将语言的高层抽象规划能力与视觉的世界状态预测能力联合起来，构建统一的具身认知基础模型。

Method: 提出 RxBrain，采用统一多模态 Mixture-of-Transformers 架构，支持语言、图像和视频的理解与生成。构建自动流水线将具身视频分解为规划步骤并与视觉状态转换对齐，生成文本-视觉联合规划监督。引入 RxBrain-Bench 评测框架，评估模型通过联合文本和视觉组件表示具身规划的能力。

Result: RxBrain 在保持具身理解和生成能力的同时，能生成耦合了文本推理、世界状态预测和联合子目标规划的规划序列。在连续机器人动作生成任务中，无需大规模动作数据预训练即展现出有前景的真实机器人性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为具身认知基础模型迈出了初步一步，证明了语言推理与视觉想象联合建模的有效性，为未来具身智能的统一模型设计提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出具身认知基础模型 RxBrain，将语言推理与视觉想象结合用于具身任务规划，并扩展到连续机器人动作生成。这与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配，属于该方向的核心主题。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and unified multimodal priorities, using continuous visual tokens and joint language-visual planning for embodied cognition. Signals: world model, unified multimodal, continuous visual token, embodied cognition, joint language-visual reasoning

Abstract: Embodied cognition requires agents to connect high-level task reasoning with the physical states to be achieved. We introduce Hy-Embodied-RxBrain, an embodied cognition foundation model with joint language-visual reasoning and imagination. Unlike vision-language models that emphasize scene understanding and textual decision making, or generative world models that mainly predict future visual states, RxBrain represents embodied plans in a single planning sequence where language and visual imagination play complementary roles. Language provides the abstract structure of a plan, including task decomposition, planning primitives, constraints, temporal order, and decision logic, while visual imagination grounds this structure through world state prediction and joint subgoal planning, associating each planning step with intermediate and final physical states. RxBrain adopts a unified multimodal Mixture-of-Transformers architecture that supports language, image, and video understanding and generation within one model. To train this capability, we build an automatic pipeline that converts embodied videos into joint text-visual planning supervision by decomposing videos into planning steps and aligning them with visual state transitions. We further introduce RxBrain-Bench to evaluate whether models can represent embodied plans through joint textual and visual components rather than separate understanding or generation. Experiments show that RxBrain maintains embodied understanding and generation abilities, and produces plans with coupled textual reasoning, world state prediction, and joint subgoal planning. We also extend RxBrain to continuous robot action generation, where it shows promising real-robot performance without large-scale action-data pretraining. These results provide an initial step toward foundation models for embodied cognition.

</details>


### [23] [Action QFormer: Structured Representation Shaping under Action Supervision in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14635)
*Yufeng Ji, Wenhao Tang, Haoyi Niu, Koushil Sreenath, Yi Wu, Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (68.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Action QFormer，一种基于查询的动作接口模块，通过指令条件查询重组VLA模型中的多模态信息为动作兼容表征，在零样本仿真到真实导航任务中显著提升成功率（18.8%→56.3%），并揭示了动作监督对多模态表征的双重影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将动作监督视为下游目标，但忽略了动作监督对继承的多模态表征的塑造作用。作者发现这种塑造具有双重效应：既必要地形成动作兼容表征，又可能破坏语言侧处理和物体定位的表征稳定性。因此需要一种更好的方式来组织和选择多模态信息，在动作监督下控制表征的塑造方式。

Method: 提出Action QFormer，一个查询驱动的动作接口模块。它使用指令条件查询从继承的多模态表征中重新组织信息，生成动作兼容的表征，再送入下游动作生成模块。通过这种解耦设计，动作监督主要作用于动作接口而非直接修改整个多模态通路，从而在保持语言侧表征稳定的同时实现有效的动作学习。

Result: 在零样本仿真到真实导航任务中，Action QFormer将平均闭环任务成功率从18.8%提升至56.3%，固定指令动作生成正确率从22.5%提升至75.5%，几乎消除了分布外指令生成。分析表明Action QFormer减少了广泛的表征重写，同时保留了有针对性的动作监督适应。

Conclusion: 提升VLA性能不仅需要更强的预训练骨干网络，还需要更好的方式来选择和组织继承的多模态信息，并控制其在动作监督下的塑造方式。Action QFormer提供了一种有效的解决方案，通过解耦动作接口来平衡动作兼容性与语言侧表征稳定性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型中的动作监督如何塑造多模态表征，并提出Action QFormer用于机器人导航任务。这属于具身智能中机器人动作规划与控制的范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model and multimodal representation shaping under action supervision, aligning with user's high-priority continuous multimodal and world model directions. Signals: world_model, continuous multimodal representation, action supervision shaping, zero-shot sim-to-real navigation, query-based interface

Abstract: Action supervision in vision-language-action (VLA) models is often treated as a downstream objective for learning action prediction. In this paper, we study it instead as a force that shapes inherited multimodal representations. We show that this shaping has a dual effect: it is necessary for forming action-compatible representations, but when action supervision is applied too directly to the inherited multimodal pathway, it can also destabilize representations that support language-side processing and object grounding. To address this tension, we introduce Action QFormer, a query-based action-facing interface that uses instruction-conditioned queries to reorganize inherited multimodal information into action-facing representations before downstream action generation. In zero-shot sim-to-real navigation, Action QFormer improves average closed-loop task success from 18.8% to 56.3%, raises fixed-instruction action-generation correctness from 22.5% to 75.5%, and nearly eliminates out-of-distribution instruction generations. Further analyses show that Action QFormer changes how action supervision shapes inherited multimodal representations, reducing broad upstream rewriting while preserving targeted and sometimes constructive action-supervised adaptation. These results suggest that improving VLA performance requires not only stronger pretrained backbones, but also better ways of selecting and organizing inherited multimodal information while controlling how it is shaped under action supervision.

</details>

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习中的世界模型在数据覆盖稀疏的区域容易产生模型利用（model exploitation）问题，即动力学幻觉。现有方法要么依赖昂贵的专家演示，要么采用保守算法限制泛化能力。本文希望利用人类直觉物理能力来直接修复世界模型的动力学错误。

Method: 提出Dynamics Learning from Human Feedback (DLHF)，将人类偏好建模为Bradley-Terry偏好损失，作用于轨迹对数似然。进一步提出RENEW，利用认知不确定性（epistemic uncertainty）引导偏好标注，聚焦于模型最易被利用的区域进行微调，提升样本效率并防止灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在Jumanji和经典控制环境中，RENEW相比朴素DLHF显著提升了样本效率，减少了灾难性遗忘，并有效降低了预训练世界模型中的模型利用问题。

Conclusion: 人类偏好可以直接监督世界模型的动力学学习，为离线模型基RL中的模型利用问题提供了新的解决思路。RENEW使该框架在实践中可行，但仍有改进空间。

Classification reason: 该论文研究离线强化学习中世界模型的模型利用（model exploitation）问题，提出通过人类偏好反馈（DLHF）来修复世界模型的动力学幻觉。核心主题是世界模型在离线RL中的训练与改进，属于世界模型方向下的具身世界模型与规划控制（embodied_world_model）这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on offline RL world model exploitation repair, not directly on generative world models or continuous multimodal models. Signals: world model, offline RL, human preference feedback, model exploitation, dynamics learning

Abstract: World models are widely used in offline reinforcement learning (RL) to improve sample efficiency and generate experience beyond a fixed dataset. However, they are vulnerable to model exploitation where data coverage is thin. Prior work addresses this either by collecting more expert demonstrations, which is often expensive, unsafe, or unavailable, or by conservative algorithms that avoid uncertain regions, which limits generalization. We propose instead to repair exploitation directly using human preferences over imagined rollouts, leveraging the strong intuitive physics that allows humans to easily spot egregious dynamics hallucinations. We formalize this as Dynamics Learning from Human Feedback (DLHF), a Bradley-Terry preference loss over trajectory log-likelihoods under a learned dynamics model. Unfortunately, naive DLHF is sample inefficient, so we introduce RENEW, which uses epistemic uncertainty to focus finetuning where the model is most exploitable. We evaluate on several Jumanji and classic control environments and find that while naive DLHF requires an outsize preference budget, RENEW makes the framework practical by improving sample efficiency, limiting catastrophic forgetting, and reducing exploitation in pretrained world models. Taken together, our results provide initial evidence that preferences can supervise world model dynamics directly, offering a new approach to addressing exploitation in offline model-based RL.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboCasa GR-1 Tabletop 和 LIBERO-Plus 三个基准上取得 SOTA 性能，并展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 未来特征预测与稀疏点跟踪是互补的，联合学习二者能够有效增强 VLA 模型的时空推理能力，提升机器人操作策略的性能和泛化性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 FoMoVLA，通过联合学习未来特征预测（visual foresight）和稀疏点跟踪（motion guidance）来增强 VLA 模型的连续动作策略，属于具身智能中的世界模型与动作规划方向，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and continuous multimodal reasoning for embodied VLA, aligning with top priority subtopics. Signals: world model, visual foresight, motion guidance, VLA, continuous representation

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have achieved impressive results in visuomotor policy learning, yet remain fundamentally reactive, mapping current observations and language to actions without explicit forward prediction of world dynamics. Existing visual foresight methods predict future visual states but lack explicit motion guidance: they show where to go but not how to get there. We argue that future feature prediction and sparse point tracking are naturally complementary: the former provides the goal state, while the latter captures the continuous motion path toward it. We propose FoMoVLA, a framework that augments VLA representations with explicit spatio-temporal supervision by jointly learning future feature foresight and sparse 2D point tracking, enhancing the continuous action policy. FoMoVLA introduces compact foresight tokens to decode future feature states, decodes sparse temporal 2D point trajectories to model compact geometric motion, and couples both through a lightweight future-conditioned cross-attention module that enables consistent reasoning between anticipated states and point dynamics. Extensive experiments on LIBERO, RoboCasa GR-1 Tabletop, and LIBERO-Plus demonstrate state-of-the-art performance and strong zero-shot generalization. Project page is available at .

### [26] [When a Verified World Model Still Loses: Play-Adequacy vs Prediction-Accuracy in LLM-Synthesized Code World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14169)
*Javier Aguilar Martín*

Direction: 世界模型

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常以采样轨迹上的高转移准确率来验证LLM合成的世界模型，但本文质疑这种验证标准对规划任务是否充分，旨在揭示预测准确率与规划充分性之间的根本性差距。

Method: 通过端到端合成流水线实验，构建LLM合成的代码世界模型（CWM），使用经典规划器进行搜索，对比转移准确率与游戏实际表现；提出危险定量定律 danger=play_cost×(1−rarity)^N；在不完美信息CWM上证明覆盖界；构造Beacon反例。

Result: CWM可通过100%转移准确率验证且规划器搜索分布上≥98%状态准确，但系统性输掉游戏；遗漏规则的游戏代价为0.091；GPT-5.x系列模型及DAgger等数据增强均无法修复该问题；不完美信息场景下覆盖界为N≳b^{d_max}。

Conclusion: 面向规划的世界模型充分性应基于搜索分布或直接通过游戏表现衡量，而非采样转移的预测准确率。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM合成的代码世界模型（Code World Model）在规划任务中的充分性问题，属于世界模型方向。其核心关注点在于世界模型的可交互性与规划适用性，而非单纯的预测精度，与[可交互与物理世界建模]（interactive_physical_world）这一规范子主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Directly challenges the validity of prediction accuracy as a metric for world models, a core concern for your world_model direction. Signals: world model verification gap, planning failure despite 100% accuracy, quantitative danger law, LLM rule translation vs inference, play-adequacy metric

Abstract: Large language models can synthesize a game's rules as executable code - a Code World Model (CWM) - which a classical planner then searches over. Such models are typically accepted when they reach high transition accuracy on sampled trajectories. We argue this is the wrong notion of adequacy for planning. We show four things. (1) An LLM-synthesized CWM can pass a sampling gate at 100% transition accuracy and be $\geq 98\%$ state-accurate on the planner's own search distribution, yet lose systematically at play, because the $<1\%$ it gets wrong is exactly the pivotal dynamics; the play cost of the omitted rule is $0.091$ (seed-clustered 95% CI $[0.065,0.117]$, $n=4800$). We call this the verified-vs-correct gap, and confirm it end-to-end through the synthesis pipeline. (2) The harm follows a quantitative law, $\mathrm{danger}=\mathrm{play\_cost}\times(1-\mathrm{rarity})^N$, whose $(1-\mathrm{rarity})^N$ gate-miss factor is proven exact and whose play cost is empirically bounded. (3) The failure is not repaired by more data: LLM synthesis behaves as rule translation, not rule inference, and did not infer the omitted rule across models (GPT-5.x) and data regimes (including DAgger and targeted examples). (4) The same mechanism recurs on the belief-inference function of imperfect-information CWMs: we prove a coverage bound (a size-$N$ gate is identifying when $N\gtrsim b^{d_{\max}}$), explaining why shallow games such as Kuhn poker show no gap, and hand-construct Beacon, a verified-but-wrong inference function that passes the gate yet loses every game. These results suggest adequacy for planning-oriented world models should be measured on the search distribution or by play directly, not by prediction accuracy on sampled transitions.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在 Bridge-V2、RT-1、Language Table、Push-T、Robomimic 等机器人操作基准上，DriftWorld 达到最先进的决策性能，推理速度比扩散基线平均快17倍（30+ fps）。作为离线模拟器时，基于 rollout 的评分与真实策略表现相关性高达 0.99。

Conclusion: 漂移生成模型非常适合机器人世界建模，其快速、高质量的未来帧生成能力可直接支持规划与策略评估，为世界模型的高效推理提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出动作条件世界模型 DriftWorld，通过漂移生成模型实现单步前向预测未来帧，属于视频世界模型范畴，与 [CANONICAL] video_world_model 高度匹配。

Importance reason: DriftWorld achieves 17x faster world model rollouts via single-step drifting, directly enabling real-time planning for robotics and aligning with your high-priority world model and efficient inference subtopics. Signals: world model, single-step generation, 30+ fps, 17x faster than diffusion, state-of-the-art decision-making

Abstract: Predictive world models enable robots to plan by imagining the outcomes of their actions, but their value for control hinges on generating many rollouts quickly. This creates a bottleneck for diffusion-based world models: multistep sampling makes each rollout expensive, limiting large-scale action search at inference time. We introduce DriftWorld, an action-conditioned world model based on drifting generative models. Rather than denoising iteratively at inference, DriftWorld learns an action-conditioned drift during training, allowing it to generate future frames from the current observation and a candidate action sequence in a single forward pass at 30+ fps, which is 17x faster on average than diffusion based baselines. We evaluate DriftWorld on standard vision-based robotic manipulation benchmarks, including Bridge-V2, RT-1, Language Table, Push-T, and Robomimic. By producing rollouts that are both accurate and fast, DriftWorld achieves state-of-the-art decision-making performance with far less inference time than diffusion-based world model baselines. Beyond online control, DriftWorld can also serve as an offline simulator for ranking real-world robot policies, with rollout-based scores correlating with ground truth at up to 0.99. These results show that drifting models are a strong fit for robot world modeling, where fast, high-quality imagination directly supports planning and policy evaluation.

</details>

*Zezhong Qian, Xiaowei Chi, Chak-Wing Mak, Tianze Zhou, Ruibin Yuan, Yuhan Rui, Hengzhe Sun, Zhuoqun Wu, Yuming Li, Siyuan Qian, Sirui Han, Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

TL;DR: 本文提出HDR（Hierarchical Denoising for Visual Reasoning），一种将层次化潜变量集成到因果视频生成中的统一框架，用于多步视觉推理。HDR将视频潜变量组织成树状层次结构，实现从粗到细的推理过程：粗去噪层保留不确定假设用于全局规划，细层逐步精化为具体视觉状态。引入稀疏层次注意力模式（SHAP）降低时序注意力成本。在迷宫导航、汉诺塔等六个多步推理任务上，相比流式自回归扩散基线，成功率从34.22提升至60.29（相对提升76.2%），推理速度比双向扩散快54.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式自回归扩散模型效率高但推理能力有限，而双向扩散虽能全局修正但推理成本高。两者都难以在复杂推理任务中同时实现逻辑一致性和低延迟流式输出。因此需要一种既能进行多步推理又能保持高效流式生成的统一框架。

Method: 提出HDR框架，核心创新包括：(1) 层次化潜变量结构——将视频潜变量组织成树状层次，粗层保留多个不确定假设用于全局规划，细层逐步精化为具体视觉状态；(2) 稀疏层次注意力模式（SHAP）——减少时序注意力计算成本；(3) 粗到细的去噪过程——先进行全局推理规划，再逐步细化生成具体帧。同时构建了包含六个多步推理任务（迷宫导航、汉诺塔、一笔画、滑动拼图、推箱子、倒水）的层次化多步视频推理基准。

Result: 在六个多步推理任务上，HDR相比流式自回归扩散基线，成功率从34.22提升至60.29（相对提升76.2%），平均进度从76.00提升至89.56。推理延迟仅0.70秒/潜变量，比双向扩散快54.2倍。仅用2%训练数据即可保留82.9%的全数据性能（双向扩散仅保留52.0%）。真实机器人实验进一步验证了其在物理交互和世界建模中的潜力。

Conclusion: HDR通过层次化潜变量结构成功实现了高效的多步视觉推理，在推理质量和推理速度之间取得了良好平衡，为视频世界模型在复杂推理任务中的应用提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 论文提出HDR框架，将层次化潜变量集成到因果视频生成中实现多步推理，涉及动作条件视频生成、未来预测和世界建模，与[视频世界模型]（video_world_model）高度匹配。同时论文也涉及视频扩散生成，因此也匹配multimodal_generation方向。

Importance reason: Integrates hierarchical latent variables into causal video generation for multi-step visual reasoning, directly advancing world model and continuous multimodal reasoning priorities. Signals: hierarchical denoising for visual reasoning, world model for multi-step reasoning, coarse-to-fine video generation, sparse hierarchical attention, 76.2% relative success gain

Abstract: Video models are evolving into vision foundation models, yet they still lack human-like multi-step reasoning. Streaming autoregressive diffusion models are efficient but limited in reasoning, while bidirectional diffusion enables global revision with high inference costs due to dense frame-level denoising. Both paradigms struggle to achieve logical consistency and low-latency streaming for complex reasoning tasks. We propose HDR (Hierarchical Denoising for Visual Reasoning), a unified framework that integrates hierarchical latents into causal video generation for multi-step reasoning. HDR organizes video latents into a tree-structured hierarchy, enabling coarse-to-fine reasoning before streaming output. Coarse denoising layers preserve uncertain hypotheses for global planning, while finer layers progressively refine them into concrete visual states. A sparse hierarchical attention pattern (SHAP) further reduces temporal attention costs. We introduce a level-stratified multi-step video reasoning benchmark with out-of-distribution cases, covering six tasks: maze navigation, Tower of Hanoi, one-line drawing, sliding puzzle, Sokoban, and water pouring. Compared with streaming autoregressive diffusion baselines, HDR improves success from 34.22 to 60.29 (76.2% relative gain) and increases average progress from 76.00 to 89.56, demonstrating more consistent reasoning trajectories. HDR maintains low-latency streaming at 0.70 seconds per latent, achieving 54.2 times faster inference than bidirectional diffusion. It also retains 82.9% of full-data performance with only 2% training data, compared with 52.0% for bidirectional diffusion. Real-world robot experiments further demonstrate HDR's potential for physical interaction and world modeling. Project demo: .

</details>


### [29] [Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization for World Models in Atari Pong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15142)
*Yukuan Lu, Zaishuo Xia, Weyl Lu, Yubei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (88.71) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了 Atari Pong 中五个代表性世界模型（DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, STORM）的闭环 rollout 质量和像素空间零样本 MBRL 性能，发现所有模型均存在球消失、运动错误等视觉和动力学失败。作者提出概念引导空间正则化（CGSReg），通过对分割的概念区域施加辅助像素重建损失来改进世界模型，在 DreamerV3、DIAMOND 和 TWISTER 上取得了一定效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型通常作为基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）系统的组件被评估，而世界模型本身很少被独立研究。作者希望独立评估世界模型的视觉和动力学质量，并探索其失败原因及改进方法。

Method: 1) 复现五个世界模型（DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, STORM）在 Atari Pong 上的训练，冻结学习到的世界模型。2) 提出闭环 rollout 诊断：用独立训练的 policy 与冻结模型交互，检查生成视频轨迹的视觉和动力学错误。3) 提出像素空间零样本 MBRL：在冻结世界模型内训练新 policy，再在真实环境中评估。4) 提出概念引导空间正则化（CGSReg）：对分割出的概念区域（如球）施加辅助像素重建损失，增强对任务关键概念的建模。

Result: 所有五个世界模型的闭环 rollout 均存在球消失、球运动错误、球拍交互无效等失败。像素空间零样本 MBRL 中，所有模型训练的 policy 性能显著低于原始 MBRL 训练管线，DreamerV3 的平均回报从 -5.5 降至 -20.9（接近最低 -21）。CGSReg 改进了 DreamerV3、DIAMOND 和 TWISTER 的闭环 rollout 和零样本 MBRL 性能，但对其他模型和指标效果不一。

Conclusion: 当前世界模型在 Atari Pong 中存在系统性的视觉和动力学建模缺陷，对任务关键概念（如球）的建模不足是重要原因。CGSReg 作为一种辅助损失可以部分缓解这些问题，但无法解决所有世界模型的瓶颈，需要更全面的改进方案。

Classification reason: 论文研究 Atari Pong 中多个世界模型（DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER 等）的闭环 rollout 诊断与像素空间零样本 MBRL 评估，并提出概念引导空间正则化（CGSReg）改进世界模型。这属于视频世界模型的评估与改进，因此映射到 canonical 子主题"视频世界模型"（video_world_model）。

Importance reason: Directly evaluates world model failures in Atari Pong, aligning with user's priority on world models and video generation, but limited to a simple game environment. Signals: World model evaluation, closed-loop rollout diagnostic, concept-guided spatial regularization, Atari Pong, MBRL performance gap

Abstract: World models are usually evaluated as components of model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) systems, while the world models themselves are rarely studied in isolation. We examine five representative visual world-model agents in Atari Pong: DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, and STORM. After reproducing their training pipelines and matching the reported agent performance, we freeze the learned world models and evaluate them with a closed-loop rollout diagnostic: a policy trained separately from the corresponding MBRL agent interacts with each frozen model, and the generated video trajectories are inspected for visual and dynamical errors. Across all five models, the rollouts contain clear failures, including ball disappearance, incorrect ball motion, and invalid ball-paddle interactions. Beyond visual trajectories, we further evaluate them with pixel-space zero-shot MBRL, where a new policy is trained entirely inside a frozen world model and then evaluated in the real environment. Across all five models, the resulting policies substantially underperform those produced by the corresponding original MBRL training pipelines. The gap is particularly large for DreamerV3, whose mean return drops from -5.5 to -20.9, near the minimum Pong return of -21. We hypothesize that insufficient modeling of task-critical concepts, such as the ball in Pong, may contribute to these failures. We therefore propose Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization (CGSReg), an auxiliary pixel reconstruction loss applied to segmented concept regions. Experiments show that CGSReg improves both closed-loop rollouts and pixel-space zero-shot MBRL in DreamerV3, DIAMOND, and TWISTER. Its effects vary across the remaining models and evaluation metrics, indicating that CGSReg alone does not address all world-model bottlenecks.

</details>

TL;DR: G²SR 提出一种结合轻量级神经网络前端与分析几何后端的混合方法，通过检测和跟踪2D高斯泼溅的跨视图对应关系，利用多视图几何三角化得到3D高斯泼溅，实现快速、内存高效且几何准确的少视图表面重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人平台需要从少量视角的RGB图像快速重建场景表面，以支持在线探索和交互。现有方法中，3DGS从少视图构建存在病态问题（易产生"漂浮物"伪影），而端到端方法依赖大型Transformer网络，计算和内存开销大且泛化性差。因此需要一种既快速又内存高效且几何准确的少视图表面重建方法。

Method: G²SR 将任务分解为两个阶段：1）轻量级神经网络前端，在图像平面上检测和跟踪2D高斯泼溅，建立跨视图的2D对应关系；2）解析几何后端，利用多视图几何原理将2D对应三角化为度量尺度的3D高斯泼溅。前端负责解决对应关系这一"良态"子问题，后端则通过解析计算完成3D重建，避免了端到端方法中大量可学习参数的需求。

Result: 在ScanNet、Replica和DTU数据集上，G²SR 在几何精度上达到或超过了最先进的端到端方法。在384×512分辨率下处理2-3视图输入时，每秒可重建69-89帧，GPU内存占用仅203 MB，相比现有方法内存减少5-107倍。

Conclusion: G²SR 通过将少视图表面重建分解为轻量级神经对应检测与解析几何三角化两个子任务，在保持高几何精度的同时实现了极快的重建速度和极低的内存占用，为移动平台上实时的基于高斯的表面重建提供了实用路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的少视图表面重建方法，属于3D场景重建与3DGS相关研究，因此映射到多模态生成方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Novel hybrid geometry+learning approach for fast, memory-efficient 3D Gaussian surface reconstruction, but not directly aligned with top priority subtopics. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, surface reconstruction, few-view reconstruction, memory-efficient, geometric accuracy

Abstract: Few-view surface reconstruction recovers the visible surfaces of a scene from a few posed RGB images, providing the 3D models that robots need to explore and interact online. On mobile platforms, the reconstruction must be fast and geometrically accurate while keeping a small memory footprint to ensure safe and efficient operation. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) offers a high-fidelity scene representation, but building it from a few views is ill-posed, as many distinct surfaces reproduce the same images, making traditional photometric methods prone to "floater" artifacts. End-to-end methods resolve the ambiguity by regressing splats with large, usually Transformer-based, networks that require heavy compute and memory while generalizing poorly to new scenes. We propose G2SR, which exploits a well-posed core of the task: given cross-view 2D splat correspondences, 3D splats follow analytically from multi-view geometry. G2SR employs a lightweight neural frontend to detect and track 2D Gaussian splats on the image plane and an analytic backend to triangulate each into a metric-scale 3D splat. On ScanNet, Replica, and DTU, G2SR matches or exceeds the geometric accuracy of state-of-the-art end-to-end methods while running at 69-89 reconstructions per second within 203 MB of GPU memory (5-107x less) for 2- and 3-view inputs at 384 x 512 resolution, offering a practical path to online Gaussian-based surface reconstruction.

</details>
