<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3DGS 重建通常需要连续图像序列或依赖 SfM 进行全局重建，计算成本高且无法提供即时反馈；而增量式 SLAM 方法无法处理无序输入。本文旨在实现无序输入下的即时 3DGS 重建，同时保证全局一致性。

Method: 1) 利用视觉位置识别模型（如 NetVLAD）和共视图进行无序序列的快速匹配；2) 通过共视图高效选择高度连接的关键帧以提升重建质量；3) 结合 GPU 优化和精心的高斯原语放置实现快速局部重建；4) 提出基于共视图的聚类方法实现无需顺序输入的闭环校正；5) 引入渐进式层次结构以扩展到包含数千张图像的大规模场景。

Result: 在多个数据集上展示了良好的视觉质量和即时反馈的 3DGS 重建效果，支持多达数千张输入图像，在重建质量和效率上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了无序输入下的即时 3DGS 重建，通过共视图驱动的匹配、关键帧选择、闭环校正和渐进式层次结构，在保证全局一致性的同时实现了高效重建，为实际场景捕获提供了实用解决方案。

Classification reason: 论文提出了一种无序输入图像的即时 3D 高斯泼溅（3DGS）重建方法，属于 3D 场景重建与渲染方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel immediate 3DGS reconstruction from unordered input with global consistency, but not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, immediate feedback, unordered input, global consistency, covisibility graph

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become the method of choice for reconstructing and real-time rendering of captured scenes. To capture a scene with good visual quality, continuous image sequences are usually combined with out-of-order shots for better scene coverage. Structure from motion can reconstruct such captures, but only after they are all available and often with high computational cost. Incremental reconstruction methods -- often derived from SLAM solutions -- provide immediate feedback, but cannot handle the out-of-order capture we require. We provide the first immediate feedback solution for such radiance field capture that provides global consistency. We first introduce a method for fast matching in out-of-order sequences, by repurposing visual place recognition models and a covisibility graph, and provide an efficient way to find highly connected keyframes, improving quality even for ordered sequences. We show how these steps -- together with GPU optimization and careful Gaussian primitive placement -- provide fast local reconstruction, in our challenging radiance field reconstruction case. We then introduce a novel cluster-based method, again using the covisibility graph, to provide efficient loop closure that does not require sequential input. Finally, to handle large scenes in our context, we introduce a progressive hierarchy that allows our method to scale to large environments, without compromising efficiency. Our results show we provide immediate feedback 3DGS reconstruction with good visual quality in several datasets, with up to thousands of input images.

</details>

*Amir Said, Randall Rauwendaal*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 使用大量球谐系数建模视角相关颜色，导致存储需求巨大。现有压缩方法未充分利用现代 GPU 的并行解码与渲染能力，因此需要一种既能高效压缩又能利用 GPU 硬件加速的方案。

Method: 利用 GPU 专用的纹理压缩格式（BC1/BC7）对 3DGS 的球谐颜色系数进行压缩。通过将图元按颜色局部分组并重排序，使纹理压缩更有效；同时设计比特率控制策略，保持随机访问能力以支持大规模并行化。

Result: 实验表明，使用 BC1 和 BC7 纹理压缩格式，GPU 解压缩可在渲染 3DGS 场景时实现可忽略或不可察觉的视觉质量下降。

Conclusion: 基于 GPU 硬件加速的纹理压缩方案可以有效压缩 3DGS 数据，在保持高质量渲染的同时大幅降低存储需求，且支持高效的并行解码。

Classification reason: 论文研究 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 数据的压缩方法，属于 3D 场景表示与渲染的范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on GPU texture compression for 3DGS, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's key directions like continuous models or video generation. Signals: 3DGS compression, GPU texture coding, BC1/BC7, spherical harmonics, not autoregressive

Abstract: Techniques for modeling 3D scenes from image collections, such as 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), are capable of generating high-quality novel views by leveraging graphics primitives with view-dependent appearance. In 3DGS, spherical harmonic (SH) are employed to model view-dependent color, resulting in a large number of SH coefficients per primitive and large memory requirements. While compression approaches have been proposed to mitigate this problem, they do not exploit the capabilities of modern Graphics Processing Units (GPUs) for parallel decoding and rendering. In this paper, we propose a method for compressing SH color coefficients using texture compression schemes specifically designed for efficient parallel GPU decoding and supported by dedicated hardware acceleration. It is shown that those methods can compress color coefficients more effectively than 2D textures by exploiting the fact that primitives can be locally grouped and reordered according to color. Furthermore, we introduce a bit-rate control strategy that preserves random access, enabling large-scale parallelization without compromising rendering performance. Experimental results using BC1 and BC7 texture compression formats show that GPU-based decompression can be achieved with negligible or imperceptible degradation in the visual quality of rendered 3DGS scenes.

</details>

*NVIDIA, Jiahui Huang, Jiawei Ren, Michal Tyszkiewicz, Bjoern Haefner, Michael Shelley, Xin Kang, Seung Wook Kim, Ning Xu, Qi Wu, Janick Martinez Esturo, Shengyu Huang, Nick Schneider, Laura Leal-Taixe, Zan Gojcic, Sanja Fidler*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出前馈式神经重建模型，接受来自标定相机阵列的多视角输入，一次性输出包含静态和动态3DGS层、天空立方体贴图以及逐相机ISP校正的分层结果，并通过3DGUT原生支持非针孔相机模型。

Conclusion: Instant NuRec 通过前馈式架构大幅提升了驾驶场景3D重建与仿真的效率，为大规模自动驾驶仿真提供了可行的解决方案。

Importance reason: Focuses on driving simulation reconstruction, not directly aligned with priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: 3D Gaussian reconstruction, driving scene simulation, feed-forward reconstruction, NuRec integration, autonomous driving

Abstract: 3D simulation platforms are critical for autonomous driving because they enable end-to-end policy evaluation, thereby reducing development costs and improving safety. In recent years, neural simulation has become predominant, with methods such as NuRec playing a central role; however, these methods remain relatively slow and typically require per-scene tuning. In this work, we present Instant NuRec, a feed-forward neural reconstruction model that turns a short multi-view driving log into a fully simulatable 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) world in a single forward pass. The model accepts multi-view input from a calibrated camera rig and emits a layered output consisting of static and dynamic 3DGS layers, a sky cubemap, and per-camera ISP corrections, while providing native support for non-pinhole camera models via 3DGUT. It reconstructs a 10-20-second multi-camera scene in roughly 1.5 seconds and achieves a PSNR on the Waymo Open Dataset that is 2.01 dB above the strongest evaluated baseline. Instant NuRec is deeply integrated into NuRec and is compatible with AlpaSim for closed-loop simulation.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出 CI-Diff，一种基于反事实推理的扩散模型方法，用于罕见概念生成。通过阻断模型固有常识偏见的干扰，利用自然直接效应解耦罕见概念中的非典型属性，并重新表述无分类器引导机制以突出非典型属性，在 RareBench 上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在生成罕见概念（如"紫色香蕉"）时，由于训练数据中物体与常见属性存在强关联（如香蕉通常是黄色），模型难以打破这种关联，导致属性渲染错误或概念组合不一致。作者希望解决这一常识偏见问题。

Method: 提出 CI-Diff（Counterfactual Inference-based Diffusion），将因果推理引入扩散模型。具体地，利用反事实推理中的自然直接效应（Natural Direct Effect）来阻断模型固有常识偏见的干扰，从而捕获文本提示中罕见概念的独立影响。同时重新表述无分类器引导（CFG）机制，以突出非典型属性。

Result: 在 RareBench 基准上进行了大量实验，CI-Diff 在罕见概念生成任务上显著优于现有最先进的扩散模型，能够正确渲染非典型属性并保持概念组合的一致性。

Conclusion: 本文首次将因果推理引入罕见概念生成任务，证明了反事实推理可以有效缓解扩散模型中的常识偏见问题，为定制化生成提供了新的思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究罕见概念生成（rare concept generation），即根据描述不常见属性组合的文本提示生成定制化图像，属于主体与概念驱动生成（customization_generation）范畴。该 canonical 子主题涵盖概念定制、个性化生成等方向，与本论文高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on rare concept generation via counterfactual inference, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with user's key interests. Signals: diffusion models, counterfactual inference, rare concept generation, classifier-free guidance, causal inference

Abstract: Rare concept generation focuses on synthesizing customized images conditioned on text prompts that describe objects with unusual attributes. Previous works failed to align the generated images with rare concepts, resulting in incorrect attribute rendering or inconsistent composition of concepts. Such failures, as we observed, stem from the inherent common knowledge bias in the training stage of diffusion models, where objects are strongly associated with their common attributes, making it difficult to break these associations when generating rare concepts. To address such challenges, in this paper, we propose a novel Counterfactual Inference-based Diffusion approach, dubbed CI-Diff. CI-Diff blocks the interference of the model's inherent common knowledge bias and utilizes the Natural Direct Effect to capture the independent influence of the text prompt of rare concepts on image generation so that decoupling the unusual attributes from the rare concepts. To this end, we reformulate the classifier-free guidance mechanism to highlight the atypical attributes. To the best of our knowledge, we are the first to introduce causal inference into the rare concept generation task. Extensive experiments on the RareBench benchmark validate the superiority of CI-Diff over state-of-the-art diffusion models. Our code can be accessed from .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散视频生成模型在多参考图像条件视频编辑中难以准确协调多个视觉源的信息，编辑指令缺乏显式的参考关系，且大多数多模态大语言模型无法可靠地生成此类指令。

Method: 提出 ReBind 框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) ReBind-Instruct，一个专门训练的 MLLM，通过两阶段渐进式方案学习建立视觉属性与参考源之间的显式绑定；(2) ReBind-Edit，通过轻量级适配将文生视频模型改造为多参考条件视频编辑模型，将视觉属性绑定到指定参考源。

Result: 实验表明 ReBind 在指令质量上显著优于通用 MLLM，并在参考图像条件视频编辑任务上达到开源方法中的最优性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入嵌入参考标记的语义指令作为中间表示，可以有效解决多参考图像条件视频编辑中的多源信息协调问题，为多参考视频编辑提供了一种系统性的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent diffusion-based video generation models have made significant progress in multi-reference image-conditioned video editing. However, existing methods still struggle to coordinate information from multiple visual sources accurately. We identify a critical deficiency in existing approaches. Existing editing instructions lack explicit reference relationships, and most multimodal large language models (MLLMs) cannot generate them reliably. To address this problem, we propose ReBind, a systematic framework that introduces semantic instructions with embedded reference tokens as the intermediate representation for multi-reference image-conditioned video editing. Our key insight is embedding reference tokens at semantic positions to eliminate ambiguity and establish precise bindings between visual attributes and their sources. We develop ReBind-Instruct, a specialized MLLM that learns to establish explicit bindings between visual attributes and their reference sources through a two-stage progressive scheme for precise reference relationships. We further develop ReBind-Edit, which enables lightweight adaptation of text-to-video models to coordinate multiple references by binding visual attributes to their designated sources. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReBind substantially outperforms general-purpose MLLMs in instruction quality and achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source methods on reference image conditioned video editing. Our project webpage: .

Conclusion: 自然论文修订历史为指令驱动的科学图表编辑提供了有效的训练信号，智能体技能演化方法能够持续提升编辑性能，为自动化科研图表编辑开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Editing the figures in a research paper is a routine and time-consuming part of everyday research practice: authors relabel components, rearrange panels, and restyle visuals as they revise their manuscripts. Automating this editing workflow under a natural-language instruction, however, is challenging, because a scientific figure is a dense infographic in which heterogeneous visual elements such as schematics, plots, photos, captions, and arrows are composed under a tight visual grammar to advance a specific argument. To address this, we present SciDiagramEdit, a benchmark and skill-evolution framework that learns from natural paper revisions and operates on the figure's editable vector source, where users can inspect and co-edit individual primitives alongside the agent. Our benchmark mines before/after figure pairs from arXiv version histories, each grounded in the authors' own revision intent. To accommodate the diversity of editing instructions, we adopt agentic learning via skill evolution: an agentic proposer continually refines the agent's skill specification from execution traces over multiple epochs. The resulting skill progressively lifts edit accuracy on a held-out validation set, providing evidence that natural paper revisions are an effective training signal for instruction-driven figure editing.

Abstract: Recent diffusion-based virtual try-on (VTON) methods remain limited by their reliance on segmentation masks, insufficient preservation of fine-grained textures, and limited support for arbitrary multi-garment compositions. Consequently, existing approaches still face significant challenges in real-world e-commerce deployment. We present TAMF-VTON, a texture-aware, mask-free framework that enables high-fidelity image synthesis under practical unconstrained conditions. Our method requires no human parsing or inpainting masks at inference time and supports diverse garment styles, categories, and quantities, enabling the simultaneous transfer of multiple items while preserving body structure and intricate texture details. This is achieved through a unified generative pipeline with three key components: (1) a lightweight Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) adaptation scheme that enables efficient fine-tuning without compromising the base model's general editing capabilities; (2) a frequency-domain supervision mechanism that explicitly optimizes high-frequency spectral consistency to preserve high-fidelity textures; and (3) a robust data curation pipeline employing an adaptive inpainting strategy to simulate the inverse VTON process for high-quality training pair generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both quantitative metrics and perceptual quality. Optimized for efficiency, the model achieves inference in under 15 seconds per image on an NVIDIA RTX 4090 with INT4 quantization. By combining mask-free operation, flexible multi-garment composition, faithful texture preservation, and efficient inference on consumer hardware, TAMF-VTON demonstrates a commercially viable solution for scalable deployment in real-world digital fashion scenarios. The project is available at .

</details>

Abstract: While recent flow-matching 3D generative models (e.g., VecSet) adopt structured representations, their tokens share global context, causing conventional training-free editing to suffer from semantic artifacts such as collapsed preserved regions or incomplete transformations. To address this, we propose TanGO, a training-free framework that enables adaptive per-token steering in the tangent space of generative dynamics. To realize this selective control, we formulate a one-step optimal control rule and determine the strength of each token's control signal using a von Mises-Fisher inspired directional discrepancy derived from the source and target velocity fields. Experiments show that TanGO substantially reduces structural artifacts and achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing 3D editing baselines. The code is publicly available at .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Signals: Video object removal, Privileged distillation, One-step generation, Diffusion model acceleration, Temporal masked transformer

Abstract: Video object removal is a fundamental yet challenging task in video editing. Despite recent progress, existing methods typically fall into two categories. Traditional approaches based on optical flow or attention mechanisms often introduce noticeable artifacts and yield unnatural results. In contrast, diffusion-based methods improve visual realism but demand multiple denoising steps, limiting their practicality. To address these issues, we propose From-Draft-to-Draft-Free (D2DF), a framework that distills the ability of transforming coarse drafts into refined videos into a one-step video generation model. Within D2DF, a teacher model is trained to refine low-quality removal results ("drafts") into high-fidelity videos by multiple steps. Then, through Prior-Privileged Consistency Distillation (PPCD), we distill this capability into a student model that performs one-step removal conditioned on the draft. To eliminate draft dependency, we introduce a Self-Guided Fast Planting (SGFP) module based on our Temporal Masked Transformer that autonomously generates scene-consistent pseudo-drafts in latent space, enabling a fully draft-free one-step model. Extensive experiments show that both draft-conditioned and draft-free versions achieve state-of-the-art performance on multiple metrics, surpassing traditional and multi-step generative methods in both quality and efficiency. The denoising process for a single video takes only about 1 second.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机快速运动时，曝光期间的模糊会破坏3D模型恢复清晰场景所需的信息。事件相机能以微秒级分辨率捕捉运动信号，但现有方法存在两个问题：(1) 物理先验（事件积分）保持边缘但累积漂移，学习先验恢复纹理但扭曲边界；(2) 现有流水线单向运行，事件噪声或固定2D伪标签的偏差会直接传递到3D几何中。

Method: 提出JADE-GS框架：(1) 像素自适应路由门（pixel-adaptive routing gate）融合物理事件积分先验与学习先验；(2) 将2D去恢复器与3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）学生模型耦合为双向循环，利用分离的多视图一致渲染和基于物理的重模糊约束来正则化恢复器，将固定的预处理器转变为几何感知预测器。

Result: 在合成和真实基准上，JADE-GS在LPIPS和CLIP-IQA感知指标上达到最优，PSNR和SSIM也具有竞争力。训练时间约1小时，显存低于5GB，可在单张消费级GPU上运行并保持实时渲染。

Conclusion: 通过事件引导与3DGS的双向耦合，JADE-GS有效解决了运动模糊场景下的3D重建问题，证明了事件相机与3DGS协同工作的潜力，为高质量动态场景重建提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于事件相机引导的3D高斯泼溅去模糊方法，属于图像/视频质量恢复与编辑范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）中的去模糊、质量增强任务高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on event-guided deblurring for 3DGS, which is not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Event camera, 3D Gaussian Splatting, deblurring, pixel-adaptive routing gate, bidirectional coupling

Abstract: When a camera moves fast during exposure, blur destroys the intra-exposure motion a 3D model needs to recover the sharp scene, while event cameras capture exactly this signal at microsecond resolution. Turning them into reliable 3D supervision faces two obstacles. First, the two restoration priors fail in opposite ways: physics-based event-integration priors preserve edges but accumulate drift; learned networks recover texture but distort boundaries. Second, existing pipelines run in one direction only, so raw event noise or the biases of fixed 2D pseudo-labels pass uncorrected into the geometry. JADE-GS addresses both: a pixel-adaptive routing gate fuses the complementary priors, and the resulting 2D restorer is coupled to a 3D Gaussian Splatting student in a bidirectional loop, where detached, multi-view-consistent renders and a physics-based reblurring constraint regularize the restorer, turning a fixed preprocessor into a geometry-aware predictor. Across synthetic and real benchmarks, JADE-GS attains the best perceptual quality, leading LPIPS and CLIP-IQA on both benchmarks with competitive PSNR and SSIM, and trainsin about one hour under 5 GB on a single consumer GPU while preserving real-time rendering.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用Transformer编码器-解码器架构，解码器通过自适应零初始化层归一化（AdaLN-zero）条件化扩散时间步，并与gloss表示进行交叉注意力。引入可微几何模块，在生成过程中强制骨骼长度一致性和生物有效关节角度。训练结合人体测量学、运动学、角度和手指关节约束，以及对比gloss-姿态对齐损失和分类器自由引导。

Result: 在PHOENIX14T和CSL-Daily基准上，相比强扩散基线，在姿态精度、关节角度正确性、分布真实性和反向翻译质量上均取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 物理信息扩散模型能够同时提升手语生成的动作真实性和语义保真度，为生物力学约束在生成任务中的应用提供了有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从文本（gloss序列）生成连续3D手语骨骼动作，属于人体动作生成范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "数字人与动作生成"（avatar_motion_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on sign language generation with biomechanical constraints, which is niche and not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: physics-informed diffusion, sign language generation, biomechanical plausibility, gloss-to-pose, anatomical constraints

Abstract: Sign language production, which generates continuous 3D skeletal motion from spoken language input, must simultaneously satisfy two constraints: semantic fidelity, so that a deaf viewer can recognize the intended sequence of glosses, and biomechanical plausibility, so that the generated skeleton respects anatomical constraints. Existing approaches optimize semantic reconstruction through coordinate-based objectives that treat the skeleton as an unstructured vector, thus allowing for bone length drift, joint angle violations, and temporarily locked fingers. We introduce PIDiffSign, a physics-informed diffusion model for gloss-to-pose translation that incorporates anatomical constraints into both the architecture and training objective. The model uses a Transformer encoder-decoder, where the decoder is conditioned on the diffusion time step through adaptive zero-initialized layer normalization and cross-attends to gloss representations. A differentiable geometry module enforces bone length consistency and biologically valid joint angles throughout generation. Training combines anthropomorphic, kinematic, angular, and finger-joint constraints with a contrastive gloss-pose alignment loss and classifier-free guidance for semantically conditioned sampling. Experiments on the PHOENIX14T and CSL-Daily benchmarks show consistent improvements over a strong diffusion baseline in pose accuracy, joint-angle correctness, distributional realism, and back-translation quality. These results demonstrate that physics-informed diffusion improves both motion realism and semantic fidelity for sign language generation.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: FilmGPT 是一个长上下文自回归 Transformer，在大规模电影语料库上训练，以自回归方式预测下一个镜头。训练时，模型学习电影中镜头序列的统计模式。推理时，提出 footage-constrained decoding 算法：给定一组原始素材片段，算法根据模型学到的统计规律，从素材中选择最佳的下一个镜头，逐步构建完整的电影序列。模型不生成新视频帧，仅从已有素材中选择和排序。

Result: 在镜头序列排序标准基准上，FilmGPT 的下一镜头预测任务超越此前 SOTA。用户研究评估完整电影编辑任务，FilmGPT 的编辑效果显著优于先前方法。此外，模型还展示了在自动视频片段修剪和人在回路电影编辑等多种视频蒙太奇应用中的适用性。

Conclusion: FilmGPT 证明了自回归建模可以有效地捕捉电影编辑的"语法"，为视频蒙太奇任务提供了一种数据驱动的自动化解决方案。该方法无需生成新帧，仅通过智能选择与排序即可生成连贯的电影序列，在多个应用场景中展现出实用价值。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视频蒙太奇（video montage）任务，即从原始视频片段中选择并排序生成连贯的电影序列，属于视频生成与编辑范畴。虽然不生成新帧，但涉及视频序列的自回归建模与选择，与文生视频/视频扩散方向中的视频序列建模相关。映射到 canonical 子主题"文生视频与视频扩散"（text_to_video_diffusion），因为该子主题涵盖视频生成方法，而本文的 footage-constrained decoding 本质上是一种基于自回归模型的视频序列生成/编辑方法。

Importance reason: Strong on autoregressive video modeling but lacks direct connection to continuous latent or world model priorities. Signals: autoregressive video modeling, long-context transformer, footage-constrained decoding, video montage, no new frame generation

Abstract: This work introduces FilmGPT, an autoregressive transformer designed to address the challenge of video montage--turning a collection of raw, "unwatchable" footage into coherent cinematic sequences. Inspired by language learning in modern LLMs, we train a long-context autoregressive transformer on a large corpus of movies. The aim is to implicitly capture the "grammar" of film directly from data rather than from hand-coded rules. Unlike other generative models, FilmGPT does not generate any new video frames. Instead, at inference time, we introduce a footage-constrained decoding algorithm to select the best next shot from the input raw footage according to the statistical patterns learned from films. We first evaluate these learned statistics directly by using the FilmGPT autoregressive model for next shot prediction on a standard benchmark of shot sequence ordering, outperforming the previous state of the art. We then evaluate our footage-constrained decoding algorithm on the full film editing task via a user study, and find that our FilmGPT-based editing significantly outperforms previous approaches. Finally, we demonstrate the applicability of FilmGPT to a wide range of applications in video montage, from automatic video segment trimming to human-in-the-loop film editing.

</details>

*Yinhan Zhang, Dinwei Tan, Xianghao Kong, Yue Ma, Yeying Jin, Anyi Rao*

TL;DR: MagicPrompt 提出了一种超轻量级的提示微调框架，用于视频扩散模型的下游任务适配。它通过注意力嵌入提示调优（Attention-Embedded Prompt Tuning）大幅减少可训练参数，并引入双空间奖励反馈优化（Dual-Space Reward Feedback Optimization）来稳定条件引导任务中的奖励训练。实验表明，该方法在不到1%的可训练参数下达到竞争性能，显著降低了训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视频扩散模型在下游任务中全量微调的计算成本过高，而现有的参数高效微调方法在十亿级模型上仍需要大量可训练参数，且基于奖励的训练在条件引导任务中面临噪声导致的优化不稳定问题。

Method: MagicPrompt 包含两个核心组件：(1) 注意力嵌入提示调优（Attention-Embedded Prompt Tuning），通过轻量级软提示（soft prompts）引导生成，参数量比全量微调少数个数量级，同时保留预训练知识；(2) 双空间奖励反馈优化（Dual-Space Reward Feedback Optimization），利用自监督潜空间目标来改善条件引导的奖励训练稳定性。

Result: 在不到1%的可训练参数下，MagicPrompt 达到了与全量微调相竞争的性能，并显著降低了训练成本。

Conclusion: MagicPrompt 证明了通过极致的参数效率和稳定的奖励优化，可以在视频扩散模型上实现高效的下游任务适配，为大规模生成模型的轻量化微调提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MagicPrompt，一种用于视频扩散模型的超轻量级提示微调方法，核心贡献在于参数高效微调（PEFT）和奖励优化稳定性。这属于生成模型效率优化的范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient video generation fine-tuning with extreme parameter reduction, aligning with user's priority on model efficiency and video generation. Signals: video diffusion, parameter-efficient fine-tuning, prompt tuning, reward optimization, less than 1% trainable parameters

Abstract: Large-scale video diffusion models (VDMs) deliver strong generation performance, but full fine-tuning for downstream tasks incurs prohibitive computational costs. Existing parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods have two critical flaws on billion-scale models: they still require substantial trainable parameters, and reward-based training suffers from noise-induced optimization instability in condition-guided tasks. We propose MagicPrompt, a lightweight framework that achieves extreme parameter efficiency and stable reward optimization. It first adopts Attention-Embedded Prompt Tuning, which steers generation via lightweight soft prompts with orders of magnitude fewer parameters while preserving pre-trained knowledge. It further introduces Dual-Space Reward Feedback Optimization, which uses self-supervised latent objectives to improve condition-guided reward training. Experiments show MagicPrompt reaches competitive performance with less than 1\% trainable parameters and notably reduces training costs.

</details>


### [44] [FlashDecoder: Real-Time Latent-to-Pixel Streaming Decoder with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14898)
*Minguk Kang, Suha Kwak*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

TL;DR: FlashDecoder 是一种快速、内存高效的纯 Transformer 视频解码器，通过逐帧解码和滚动 KV 缓存机制，在保持重建质量的同时实现 3.6x-4.7x 的解码加速和最高 11x 的显存节省，支持恒定延迟的流式解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前潜空间视频扩散模型依赖 3D 卷积解码器，在高分辨率或长视频场景下解码速度慢且显存消耗大，成为实时视频生成的瓶颈。作者希望设计一种更快速、内存高效的解码器来替代传统卷积解码器。

Method: FlashDecoder 采用纯 Transformer 架构，逐帧解码潜变量为像素。每帧解码时，当前帧仅通过滚动 KV 缓存（rolling KV cache）关注固定大小的历史帧窗口。固定时间窗口确保解码速度和显存消耗不随视频长度增长，实现恒定延迟流式解码。由于帧按顺序处理，时序因果性无需显式注意力掩码即可保证。该方法可在高达 1080p 分辨率下训练。

Result: 在 Wan2.1 和 Wan2.2 潜空间上，FlashDecoder 在 1080p 分辨率下重建质量与卷积解码器相当（PSNR 41.55dB vs 41.49dB），但解码速度快 3.6x-4.7x，显存消耗降低最多 11x（单张 H100 GPU）。结合架构感知推理优化后，加速比可达 12x。

Conclusion: FlashDecoder 证明了纯 Transformer 架构可以替代 3D 卷积解码器，在保持重建质量的同时大幅提升解码效率和降低显存消耗，为实时视频生成提供了可行的解码方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出FlashDecoder，一种高效的纯Transformer视频解码器，用于加速潜空间视频扩散模型的解码过程（latent-to-pixel），显著提升解码速度并降低显存消耗。这属于生成模型加速与高效化（efficient_generation）范畴，该[CANONICAL] subtopic涵盖扩散加速、高效采样等效率优化方法。

Importance reason: Directly addresses model efficiency for video generation, a high-priority subtopic, with a practical streaming decoder that significantly accelerates latent-to-pixel decoding. Signals: efficient inference, video generation acceleration, transformer decoder, streaming decoding, memory-efficient

Abstract: Real-time video generation demands fast decoding as much as fast denoising, yet current latent video diffusion models rely on 3D convolutional decoders that are slow and memory-intensive at high resolutions or for long video. We introduce FlashDecoder, a fast, memory-efficient pure-Transformer video decoder that decodes latents to pixels frame by frame. At each step, the current frame attends only to a fixed-size window of past frames through a rolling KV cache. The fixed temporal window keeps decoding fast and memory bounded regardless of video length, enabling constant-latency streaming. Because frames are processed sequentially, temporal causality is enforced without explicit attention masks, enabling training at resolutions up to 1080p and matching the reconstruction quality of convolutional decoders. On the Wan2.1 and Wan2.2 latent spaces, FlashDecoder matches each convolutional decoder in reconstruction quality (e.g., 41.55dB vs. 41.49dB PSNR at 1080p) while decoding 3.6x-4.7x faster with up to 11x less memory on a single H100 GPU. With architecture-aware inference optimizations, the speedup widens to 12x.

</details>

*Wenxuan Chen, Wenjie Feng*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SIRUS，一种无需训练的推理时概念擦除框架，用于文生视频模型。通过定位目标概念的提示证据并在采样过程中抑制其表达，SIRUS 能在不更新模型参数的情况下移除特定概念。同时提出了面向视频的概念擦除评测框架，在 CogVideoX 和 Wan2.2 上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生视频模型能生成逼真视频，但难以可控地移除特定概念（如不安全内容、特定物体或风格）。现有图像概念擦除方法无法直接迁移到视频领域，因为视频中目标概念可能跨帧持续存在，且需保持非目标主体、动作、场景和时间结构不变。

Method: SIRUS 是一个训练无关的推理时框架。给定目标概念的文本别名，SIRUS 在采样过程中定位与目标相关的提示证据（prompt evidence），并在去噪过程中抑制目标表达。无需更新文本编码器或去噪网络。同时提出视频导向的评测框架，分别衡量目标遗忘、非目标保持、视频质量、越狱鲁棒性和效率。

Result: 在 CogVideoX 上，SIRUS 达到 70.4% 平均遗忘成功率和 25.7% 平均帧命中率，优于 VideoEraser 的 44.4%/47.2%；VBench 质量下降从 -0.043 降至 -0.016，实现了最佳的遗忘-质量权衡。在 Wan2.2 上的迁移实验表明 SIRUS 可泛化到不同 T2V 骨干网络。

Conclusion: SIRUS 是一种有效的训练无关推理时概念擦除方法，在遗忘效果和视频质量保持之间取得了更好的平衡，且能跨模型泛化。视频级评测框架为 T2V 概念擦除提供了更全面的评估标准。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文生视频模型的概念擦除（concept erasure/unlearning），属于生成模型的可控性与对齐问题。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）涵盖生成模型的对齐、可控性、guidance 等，概念擦除是其中一种可控性/对齐技术，因此映射至此。

Importance reason: Focuses on inference-time concept suppression for T2V, which is a niche safety/alignment topic not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous models or autoregressive generation. Signals: Training-free inference-time concept erasure, Video-centric evaluation framework, Forgetting-quality trade-off improvement, Transfer to modern T2V backbones, Not in priority subtopics or boost keywords

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) generators can synthesize realistic and temporally coherent videos, but controllably removing a target concept from a generator remains difficult. Unlike text-to-image concept erasure, T2V unlearning must suppress a target concept that may persist across frames while preserving non-target subjects, actions, scenes, and temporal structure. We propose \textbf{SIRUS}, a training-free inference-time framework for concept-level T2V unlearning. Given textual aliases of a target concept, SIRUS localizes target-related prompt evidence and suppresses target expression during sampling, without updating the text encoder or denoising network. We further introduce a video-oriented evaluation framework for T2V unlearning that separately measures target forgetting, non-target preservation, video quality, jailbreak robustness, and efficiency, using video-level failure criteria, frame-level residue statistics, paired preservation analysis, VBench-based quality diagnostics, and deployment overhead measurement. Across five safety, object, and style concepts on CogVideoX, SIRUS reaches 70.4\% average forgetting success and 25.7\% average frame hit, compared with 44.4\% / 47.2\% for VideoEraser, while reducing the average VBench quality drop from -0.043 to -0.016, yielding the strongest forgetting-quality trade-off among fully evaluated baselines. Transfer experiments on Wan2.2 further suggest that SIRUS generalizes across modern T2V backbones.

</details>

*Qifan Zhou, Yuan Wang, Yanbin Hao, Xiang Wang, Kuien Liu, Richang Hong, Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成模型从非精选数据中不可避免地学习到不良概念（如暴力、色情等），现有概念擦除方法通常依赖特定架构，且难以在移除目标概念的同时保留非目标内容和生成先验。

Result: 在 U-Net、DiT、自回归图像生成器以及文生视频模型上均取得优异效果，在单概念和多概念擦除任务中表现强劲，同时有效保留非目标先验。

Conclusion: Uni-AdaVD 提供了一种高效且可适配的推理时安全机制，适用于现代视觉生成模型，无需重新训练即可实现概念擦除。

Classification reason: 该论文提出概念擦除（concept erasure）方法，属于生成模型的安全对齐与可控性范畴，与 canonical 子主题「生成模型对齐与奖励」中的 guidance 与可控性高度匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses safety in visual generation with a universal inference-time method, but lacks direct alignment with top priority subtopics like continuous models or autoregressive video generation. Signals: concept erasure, inference-time, orthogonal value decomposition, multimodal attention, U-Net/DiT/autoregressive

Abstract: Visual generative models inevitably absorb undesirable concepts from uncurated pretraining data, making concept erasure essential for safe deployment. Existing erasure methods, however, are often architecture-specific and struggle to remove target concepts while preserving non-target content and generative priors. We present Uni-AdaVD, a universal inference-time concept erasure framework for visual generation. Uni-AdaVD treats the value space of multimodal attention as a unified intervention space and introduces encoder-aware target representation construction to localize target semantics across heterogeneous text encoders. It further combines orthogonal value decomposition with an adaptive erasing shift to suppress target semantic directions without updating the original model weights. Extensive experiments on U-Net-, DiT-, and autoregressive image generators, as well as text-to-video models, demonstrate strong performance on single- and multi-concept erasure while preserving non-target priors. These results suggest that Uni-AdaVD provides an efficient and adaptable safety mechanism for modern visual generative models. Our code is available at .

</details>

*Vaddi Charan Sai Nandan Reddy, Harini B, Chandana M S*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个双引导框架，结合微调序列到序列LLM的负提示优化和CNN-RNN混合分类器的潜空间引导，在Stable Diffusion生成过程中回滚低质量潜变量更新，从而减少伪影并提升语义保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型生成的图像常存在伪影或语义偏差，手动编写负提示需要大量试错且效果不稳定，现有方法缺乏自动化的质量引导机制。

Result: 实验表明，该双引导框架相比基线扩散模型减少了伪影，提升了语义保真度，在多个评测指标上取得改进。

Conclusion: 负提示优化与潜空间分类器引导相结合能有效提升扩散模型的生成质量，为扩散模型的可控生成提供了一种自动化引导方案。

Abstract: We present a novel system that integrates negative prompt optimization via a fine-tuned sequence-to-sequence LLM and latent-space classifier guidance to improve the quality of images generated by Stable Diffusion. Our approach automatically generates optimized negative prompts, and employs a CNN-RNN hybrid classifier to evaluate and guide diffusion steps, rolling back low-quality latent updates. Experimental results demonstrate that our dual-guidance framework reduces artifacts and improves semantic fidelity compared to baseline diffusion.

### [48] [The Steering Budget: Examples beat Knobs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14246)
*Raj Kumar Rajendran*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

TL;DR: 本文提出"steering budget"概念，指出生成模型的可控引导存在一个由训练数据决定的预算上限：knob（提示词、引导尺度等）只能触及一部分，而 examples（具体实例）可以触及更大的剩余部分。作者在图像生成和晶体结构生成两个领域验证了这一发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型的可控引导（如提示词、引导尺度）存在明显的性能天花板，超过某个点后无法进一步改变目标属性。作者认为这个天花板并非模型本身的缺陷，而是由训练数据预先设定的"预算"，需要理解其本质并找到突破方法。

Method: 作者将属性的可移动范围分为两部分：knob 可触及的部分和仅能通过 examples 触及的部分。提出一种低成本审计训练数据的方法来测量 steering budget，并给出构建示例集（example set）的配方——从模型已学到的内容中组合示例而非添加新训练数据。在图像生成和晶体结构生成两个不相关领域进行验证。

Result: 实验表明，knob 只能触及属性可移动范围的一部分，而 examples 可以覆盖整个 budget。示例引导相比 knob 具有更大的可达范围（reach）和更强的表现力（expressiveness），能够引导模型朝向难以用语言描述的目标。

Conclusion: 生成模型的引导存在由训练数据预设的 budget，knob 只能触及部分范围，而 examples 可以覆盖全部。这一发现为生成模型的可控性提供了新的理论视角和实用方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究生成模型的可控引导（steering），提出"steering budget"概念，分析 knob（提示词、引导尺度等）与 examples（示例）两种引导方式的可达范围。这与生成模型对齐与奖励（generation_alignment）中的 guidance 与可控性高度相关，属于该 canonical 子主题的范畴。

Importance reason: Novel concept of steering budget but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: generative model controllability, steering budget concept, examples vs knobs, image generation, crystal structure generation

Abstract: Generative models are steered with knobs -- prompts, guidance scales, property tags. Turn one as hard as you like and, past a point, it stops moving the property you care about. We find that ceiling is not a shortcoming of the model but a budget, set by the training data before the model is trained: a property's movable range splits in two -- the part a knob can reach, and a second, significant part that only examples -- concrete instances of what you want more of -- can reach. That second part is usually much larger, but not always, and the same budget says so in advance. Reaching that second part takes a different move: instead of turning a knob, you show the model examples, composed from what it already learned rather than added to its training. A cheap audit of the training data measures the budget; we give a recipe for building the example set that reaches all of it. This buys two things a knob can't. Reach: it moves a property across the whole budget, not just the part a knob reaches. Expressiveness: it steers toward targets you can only specify by example -- including ones you can't put into words. We turn these into a handful of falsifiable claims and verify them in two unrelated domains, image and crystal-structure generation -- marking where a knob is enough, and where only examples will do.

</details>

Abstract: State-of-the-art flow based text-to-image (T2I) models exhibit remarkable generative abilities but remain vulnerable to producing unsafe content. Prior safety efforts range from concept erasure and prompt filtering to classifier-based gating. However, simple techniques like parameter efficient adaptations of the models easily bypass such guardrails. We introduce a unique principled approach that achieves safety by regulating the model's attention dynamics through inference-time introspection, exhibiting intrinsic robustness. Our method analyzes and rebalances attention activations throughout image synthesis, steering generations away from unsafe concepts while preserving semantic alignment. This introspective control ensures safety of deployed models. Across standard and adversarial safety benchmarks, our approach achieves remarkable safety scores while maintaining or even improving alignment and perceptual quality. Our results reveal that attention-space regulation offers a considerably more promising path to safer diffusion transformer based image generation than the existing concept erasing code can be accessed at

*Yushi Huang, Xiangxin Zhou, Jun Zhang, Liefeng Bo, Tianyu Pang*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

TL;DR: MeanFlowNFT 将前向过程强化学习框架 DiffusionNFT 扩展到 MeanFlow 生成器上，通过构造瞬时速度预测器桥接平均速度与瞬时速度，实现无需反向轨迹的奖励优化，在图像和视频生成上取得优异效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MeanFlow 生成器通过预测时间区间内的平均速度实现快速少步采样，但现有的 RL 对齐方法（如 DiffusionNFT）针对的是瞬时速度的扩散模型，无法直接应用于 MeanFlow。作者希望将前向过程 RL 的优势引入 MeanFlow，使其在保持少步高效采样的同时也能通过奖励优化提升生成质量。

Method: 利用 MeanFlow identity 桥接平均速度与瞬时速度的关系，构造一个诱导的瞬时速度预测器，将 DiffusionNFT 的目标函数应用于该预测器进行奖励优化，而实际采样仍使用平均速度以保持 MeanFlow 的少步生成优势。同时证明了该方法继承了 DiffusionNFT 的严格策略改进保证。

Result: 在图像生成（SD3.5-M）上，MeanFlowNFT 在 8 项指标中的 6 项上超越了先前最先进的 RL 调优少步生成器；在视频生成（Wan 2.1）上，4 步 MeanFlowNFT 达到 VBench 84.33 分，超过了 50 步 LongCat-Video RL 的 82.57 分。

Conclusion: MeanFlowNFT 成功将前向过程 RL 扩展到 MeanFlow 生成器，在保持少步高效采样的同时显著提升了生成质量，证明了该方法的有效性和通用性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MeanFlowNFT，将前向过程强化学习（DiffusionNFT）扩展到 MeanFlow 生成器上，用于对齐生成模型与人类偏好/任务奖励。这属于生成模型对齐与奖励优化的范畴，与 canonical subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous multimodal generation by applying forward-process RL to MeanFlow, a key technique for efficient few-step generation. Signals: continuous language model, flow matching, video generation, efficient inference, RL alignment

Abstract: MeanFlow generators achieve fast few-step sampling by predicting average velocities over time intervals, making them attractive for efficient generation. Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a powerful way to align diffusion and flow models with human preferences and task-specific objectives. In particular, DiffusionNFT offers an efficient forward-process RL framework that does not require reverse-process trajectories or likelihood estimation. However, applying such RL methods to MeanFlow remains underexplored. DiffusionNFT optimizes instantaneous velocities, whereas MeanFlow samples with average velocities. To bridge this gap, we introduce MeanFlowNFT. Inspired by the MeanFlow identity, which bridges average and instantaneous velocities, we construct an induced instantaneous-velocity predictor. We apply the DiffusionNFT objective to this predictor, making reward optimization well-defined for MeanFlow. Sampling remains based on the average velocity, preserving MeanFlow's fast few-step generation. We further prove that MeanFlowNFT inherits DiffusionNFT's strict policy-improvement guarantee. Experiments on image and video generation show that MeanFlowNFT consistently improves baselines. Moreover, it outperforms prior state-of-the-art RL-tuned few-step generators on most metrics ($6$ of $8$ on SD3.5-M), and can even surpass multi-step RL-tuned diffusion while using only a few sampling steps. For instance, on Wan 2.1, $4$-step MeanFlowNFT reaches a VBench score of $84.33$, surpassing $50$-step LongCat-Video RL ($82.57$).

</details>


### [51] [Multi-Axis Max@K Reinforcement Learning for Representative Diversity in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14962)
*Ku Onoda, Paavo Parmas, Hiroki Furuta, Soichiro Nishimori, Yuta Oshima, Shohei Taniguchi, Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CV

Importance: 值得关注 (73.21) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 multi-axis max@K，一种基于组的强化学习目标，通过跨样本的类别级最大分数聚合与信用分配，提升扩散文生图模型在多个语义维度上的生成多样性，并在公平性评测中取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图模型虽然能生成高质量、对齐提示的图像，但同一提示下生成的样本往往只覆盖少数视觉模式，缺乏多样性。对于人物相关的提示，这种多样性缺失还可能放大人口统计偏差。因此需要一种方法显式提升生成结果在多个语义维度上的覆盖度。

Method: 提出 multi-axis max@K 目标：对一组样本，先对每个目标类别取组内最大分数，再对所有类别的最大值求和。信用分配上，一个样本仅在它提升某个类别的组内最大值时才获得该类别上的正权重，从而鼓励不同样本贡献不同类别。将该目标作为强化学习奖励，对扩散模型进行微调。

Result: 在合成颜色任务和 SD3.5-M 上验证了信用分配机制的有效性。在感知外观公平性评测中，multi-axis max@K 在三个自动评估器上相比基线模型将 Fairness Score 提升了 0.23-0.36，同时保持了图像质量和文本对齐度。

Conclusion: Multi-axis max@K 是一种有效的组级强化学习目标，能够显著提升文生图模型在多个语义维度上的生成多样性，有助于缓解人口统计偏差，且不牺牲图像质量与提示对齐。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 multi-axis max@K 强化学习目标，用于提升文生图模型的多样性（覆盖多种语义模式），属于生成模型的对齐与奖励优化范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on diversity in text-to-image generation, which is relevant but not a top priority subtopic. Signals: Text-to-image generation, Reinforcement learning for diversity, Fairness improvement, Not in priority subtopics, No boost keywords matched

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models can synthesize realistic, prompt-aligned images, yet samples generated for the same prompt often cover only a small subset of visually distinct modes. This limits the diversity of images, and for person-centric prompts, can reflect or amplify demographic skew. We formalize this problem as coverage of a predefined set of semantically specified modes, which we call target-mode coverage. We then propose multi-axis max@K, a group-based reinforcement learning objective for improving such coverage in diffusion-based T2I models. Given a group of samples and one score per target category, multi-axis max@K first takes the maximum score across samples for each category and then sums these category-wise maxima. The resulting credit assignment gives a sample positive weight on a category only when it increases that category's group-wise maximum, allowing different samples to contribute to different categories. We first validate the credit-assignment mechanism on a synthetic mixture and on SD3.5-M using deterministic pixel-based color rewards. We then evaluate the same objective on perceived-appearance fairness. Across three automatic evaluators on held-out prompts, multi-axis max@K improves the Fairness Score by 0.23-0.36 relative to the base model, while maintaining image quality and text alignment.

</details>

*Saptarshi Roy, Debepsita Mukherjee, Pratik Patil*

Main category: stat.ML, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

TL;DR: 本文理论分析了 Rectified Flow 中的最优自蒸馏问题：给定一个次优的教师速度场，学生模型在真实速度与教师速度的混合信号上训练能否严格改进教师？在线性 Rectified Flow 加岭回归的设定下，证明了精确的仿射路径恒等式，推导出最优混合系数的闭式解，并证明只要教师风险沿正则化路径非平稳，最优自蒸馏就能严格改进教师。最优系数遵循符号规则：正混合纠正欠正则化教师，负混合纠正过正则化教师。实验在 Gaussian 模型、混合 Gaussian 和图像数据上验证了该方法在速度风险、模态恢复和有限步生成上的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成模型越来越多地使用模型生成的信号进行训练，这既带来了自我改进的机会，也带来了崩溃的风险。本文旨在研究 Rectified Flow 中自蒸馏的理论基础，探索学生模型能否通过混合真实信号和教师信号来严格改进次优教师模型。

Method: 在线性 Rectified Flow 框架下，对固定插值对施加岭回归，证明精确的仿射路径恒等式，推导最优混合系数的闭式解。提出单次广义交叉验证（GCV）和验证调优程序，避免网格搜索和重复重拟合。结合 RF Wasserstein 收敛界，证明最优自蒸馏改进了控制连续时间和有限步生成误差的速度估计项。

Result: 理论证明：只要教师风险沿正则化路径非平稳，最优自蒸馏就能严格改进教师。最优系数遵循符号规则（正混合纠正欠正则化，负混合纠正过正则化）。实验表明，在 Gaussian 模型、混合 Gaussian 和图像数据上，最优自蒸馏在速度风险、模态恢复和有限步生成方面均优于教师和纯蒸馏。

Conclusion: 本文为 Rectified Flow 的自蒸馏提供了严格的理论基础，证明了最优自蒸馏可以严格改进次优教师，并给出了实用的调优方法。该工作为生成模型中的自我改进机制提供了理论支撑，有助于理解自蒸馏何时有效以及如何最优地利用模型生成信号。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 Rectified Flow 的最优自蒸馏（self-distillation）方法，属于生成模型训练中的自我改进与对齐优化问题，与 canonical 子主题「生成模型对齐与奖励」中的对齐、蒸馏、优化等方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Strong theoretical analysis of self-distillation for rectified flow, but tangential to user's priority on continuous language/video generation models. Signals: rectified flow, self-distillation, linear probing, theoretical analysis, velocity risk improvement

Abstract: Modern generative models are increasingly trained using model-generated signals, creating both opportunities for self-improvement and risks of collapse. We study optimal self-distillation (SD) for rectified flow (RF): given a suboptimal teacher velocity field, can a student trained on a mixture of true RF velocities and teacher velocities provably improve the teacher? For linear RF with ridge regularization on fixed interpolation pairs, we prove an exact affine path identity, derive the optimal mixing coefficient in closed form, and show strict improvement in integrated velocity risk whenever the teacher risk is nonstationary along the regularization path. The optimal coefficient obeys a sign rule: positive mixing corrects under-regularized teachers, while negative mixing corrects over-regularized teachers. We also give one-shot generalized cross-validation (GCV) and validation tuning procedure that avoids grid search over mixing weights and repeated refitting. Combining this theorem with RF Wasserstein convergence bounds, we show that optimal self-distillation improves the velocity estimation terms controlling continuous-time and finite-step generation error. Experiments with Gaussian models, Gaussian mixtures, and image data show that optimal self-distillation improves velocity risk, mode recovery, and finite-step generation relative to both the teacher and pure distillation.

</details>

Conclusion: MultiRef-Compass填补了MR2AV评测的空白，为未来MR2AV研究提供了标准化评测基础，揭示了当前方法在参考保持、多实体绑定和音视频一致性方面的显著不足。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了MultiRef-Compass，一个面向多参考到音视频生成（MR2AV）的统一评测基准，包含350个样本、14个子指标的评估协议以及MLLM-as-a-Judge框架。这属于生成模型评测与数据集方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "generation_benchmark"（生成模型评测与数据集）高度匹配。

Signals: Evaluation benchmark, Multi-reference generation, Audio-video generation, Not a priority subtopic, No strong boost keywords

Abstract: Multi-reference-to-audio-video (MR2AV) generation aims to generate coherent audio-video content conditioned on multiple references and textual instructions. Existing benchmarks mainly focus on text-driven generation, single-reference subject preservation, or isolated audio-video alignment, leaving the emerging MR2AV setting largely unexplored. Compared with these settings, MR2AV requires models to jointly reason over multiple references while generating synchronized visual and audio content. Models must not only preserve each reference faithfully but also correctly bind and compose multiple referenced entities into coherent audio-visual events. To address this gap, we introduce MultiRef-Compass, a unified benchmark for MR2AV generation. It comprises $350$ carefully curated samples constructed through a scalable and controllable asset-composition pipeline, covering multi-view subject preservation, multi-entity binding, and human-object-scene composition. To provide interpretable assessment, MultiRef-Compass defines an evaluation protocol with four dimensions: Basic Quality, Reference Consistency, Audio-Visual Consistency, and Instruction Following, using 14 sub-metrics. MultiRef-Compass integrates automatic metrics with a rejudging-enhanced MLLM-as-a-Judge framework, enabling scalable and auditable evaluation of both perceptual fidelity and reference-conditioned composition. Extensive experiments on eight representative MR2AV systems reveal substantial room for improvement across multiple evaluation dimensions, underscoring the need for a comprehensive benchmark and positioning MultiRef-Compass as a foundation for future MR2AV research.

</details>

*Yuqi Tang, Tengfei Liu, Yizheng Lai, Yuran Wang, Yang Shi, Wanshun Su, Zhuoran Zhang, Qixun Wang, Xiaohan Zhang, Xinlei Yu, Xuehai Bai, Xuanyu Zhu, Bohan Zeng, Bozhou Li, Shujie Li, Yifan Dai, Yujie Wei, Shixuan Liu, Haotian Wang, Jialu Chen, Yuanxing Zhang*

TL;DR: KeyFrame-Compass 是首个针对关键帧条件视频生成的综合评测基准，包含386个精心筛选的样本，覆盖三个应用领域、两种视频结构、两种提示粒度、两种条件格式和四种关键帧密度。该工作提出了自动化评估框架，将关键帧执行分解为六个互补指标（存在性、保真度、时序顺序、定位、持久性和唯一性），并通过基于证据的 MLLM 判断和专用感知模型评估整体视频质量。对9个代表性视频生成系统的实验揭示了当前模型在关键帧忠实执行与自然视频合成之间存在明显权衡，且随着关键帧约束密度增加性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有视频生成模型支持多关键帧条件输入，但缺乏系统性的评测方法来衡量模型是否能忠实再现给定的关键帧序列，同时保持整体视频质量。现有基准主要关注文本到视频或单帧条件生成，无法评估多关键帧条件下的执行能力。

Method: 构建了包含386个样本的基准数据集，覆盖三个应用领域（电影/广告/动画）、两种视频结构（故事板/过渡）、两种提示粒度、两种条件格式（独立帧/故事板网格）和四种关键帧密度（1/2/4/8帧）。提出自动化评估框架：关键帧执行评估包含六个指标（存在性、保真度、时序顺序、定位、持久性、唯一性）；整体视频质量评估采用基于证据的 MLLM 判断，结合专用感知模型（如 IQA、动作质量评估）。

Result: 对9个代表性视频生成系统的实验表明：1）当前模型在关键帧忠实执行与自然视频合成之间存在明显权衡；2）随着关键帧约束密度增加，模型性能显著下降；3）大多数开源模型无法正确将故事板网格输入解释为时序有序的关键帧序列。

Conclusion: KeyFrame-Compass 填补了关键帧条件视频生成评测的空白，揭示了现有模型在多关键帧约束下的根本局限性，为未来研究提供了标准化评测平台和改进方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 KeyFrame-Compass，首个针对关键帧条件视频生成的综合评测基准，包含386个样本、多维度评估框架和9个视频生成系统的实验分析。这属于生成模型评测基准，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "generation_benchmark（生成模型评测与数据集）" 完全匹配。

Importance reason: Provides a needed benchmark for keyframe-conditioned video generation, but the topic is not a top priority for your configured interests. Signals: video generation evaluation, keyframe conditioning, benchmark, automated metrics, trade-off analysis

Abstract: Video generation increasingly relies on keyframe-based workflows, where creators specify a sequence of reference images to guide generation. Although recent models support multi-keyframe conditioning, it remains unclear whether they can faithfully reproduce the prescribed keyframes while maintaining overall video quality. We present KeyFrame-Compass, the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating keyframe-conditioned video generation. The benchmark contains 386 carefully curated samples spanning three application domains, two video structures, two prompt granularities, two conditioning formats, and four keyframe densities, enabling controlled analysis under diverse generation settings. We further introduce an automated evaluation framework that jointly measures keyframe execution and overall video quality. Specifically, we decompose keyframe execution into six complementary metrics covering presence, fidelity, temporal ordering, localization, persistence, and uniqueness, while assessing overall video quality through evidence-grounded MLLM judgments augmented with specialized perception models. Experiments on nine representative video generation systems reveal several fundamental limitations. Current models exhibit a clear trade-off between faithful keyframe execution and natural video synthesis. Their performance further degrades as keyframe constraints become denser and most open-source models also fail to interpret storyboard-grid inputs as temporally ordered keyframe sequences.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出使用人口统计学条件化的合成医学图像生成器（基于Stable Diffusion 2.1），同时用于疾病分类器的偏差缓解（训练侧）和偏差检测（评估侧）。在COVID-19胸部CT分类任务上，合成数据作为预训练先验（而非联合增强）显著提升分类器性能，且合成评估集能准确复现真实大样本测试集的子群排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类器的公平性审计面临少数子群样本量不足的问题，导致性能评估的置信区间过宽，无法可靠检测偏差。现有方法缺乏同时解决训练侧偏差缓解和评估侧偏差检测的统一方案。

Method: 使用端到端微调的Stable Diffusion 2.1作为人口统计学条件化合成生成器。在训练侧，将合成数据作为预训练先验（sequential pretraining），再在真实数据上微调；在评估侧，生成合成少数子群测试集用于偏差检测。对比了联合增强（joint augmentation）和顺序预训练两种范式。

Result: 顺序预训练+微调显著优于联合增强，在相同固定数据量下超越全真实数据基线，实现约100倍真实数据效率。在偏差检测方面，合成评估集在MCC和Recall指标上完美复现真实大样本测试集的子群排名（Spearman ρ=1.00），在真实小样本测试集失效的单元格上提供更可靠的估计。

Conclusion: 人口统计学条件化的合成生成器可以同时作为子群偏差的修复工具和测量工具，在公平性审计最关心的少数子群单元格中发挥最大价值。

Classification reason: 该论文使用条件生成模型（Stable Diffusion）生成合成医学图像，用于疾病分类器的偏差缓解和偏差检测。核心贡献在于利用生成模型构建合成数据集进行公平性审计和训练增强，属于生成模型在评测/数据集构建方面的应用，因此映射到多模态生成方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical bias mitigation, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: medical imaging, bias mitigation, synthetic data, stable diffusion, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Per-subgroup fairness audits of medical image classifiers face a sample-size problem: minority subgroups in held-out test sets have so few samples that the resulting confidence intervals on per-subgroup performance are wider than the bias the audit is meant to detect. We argue that a demographically-conditioned synthetic generator can do both: mitigate bias on the training side and detect bias on the evaluation side. Working on COVID-19 chest CT classification with an end-to-end fine-tuned Stable Diffusion 2.1 generator, we make two findings. For bias mitigation (training), a demographically-balanced synthetic cohort is most useful as a pretraining prior, not as joint augmentation: with the same fixed data, sequential pretraining followed by fine-tuning substantially outperforms joint augmentation, and the resulting classifier surpasses the full-real baseline at $\sim$$100\times$ real-data efficiency. For bias detection (evaluation), across five synthetic minority cohorts and five classifier seeds, the synthetic estimator reproduces the subgroup ranking of a well-powered real oracle (Spearman $\rho = 1.00$ on MCC and Recall) and gives the more reliable per-cell estimate where the small real test set runs out of samples. The synthetic cohort is therefore most useful in exactly the cells that fairness audits care about, as both a fix for and a measure of subgroup bias.

</details>

*David Ayllon, Alice Baird, Jeffrey Brooks, Franc Camps-Febrer, Jakub Piotr Cłapa, Theo Lebryk, Jens Madsen, Olya Ossipova, Sharath Rao, Hoon Shin, Tigran Soghbatyan, Georg Streich, Rashish Tandon, Panagiotis Tzirakis*

TL;DR: 本文提出 RW-Voice-EQ Bench，一个面向真实世界语音 AI 系统的多维评测基准，涵盖 TTS、STS、语音理解（SU）和 ASR 四个维度。评测发现：TTS 的自然度、表现力、身份稳定性和可靠性是独立维度；STS 中部分智能体仍主要依赖文本转录而非利用声学信息；SU 在副语言任务上表现不均衡；ASR 在真实口音、情感、噪声和对话条件下暴露出传统基准无法捕捉的失败。结论是语音 AI 应作为声学、表达、交互和鲁棒性能力的剖面进行评估，而非单一总分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音 AI 基准通常只评估孤立能力（如词错误率、文本对话质量），很少测试系统是否利用了区分口语与文本的声学信息。缺乏一个多维度的、面向真实世界条件的综合评测基准。

Method: 构建 RW-Voice-EQ Bench 多维基准，从 TTS（自然度、表现力、身份稳定性、可靠性）、STS（声学信息利用程度）、SU（副语言任务）、ASR（真实口音、情感、噪声、对话条件）四个维度进行系统评测，分析各维度间的独立性与相关性。

Result: TTS 各维度（自然度、表现力、身份稳定性、可靠性）高度独立；STS 中部分系统仍以文本转录为主，未充分利用声学信息；SU 在副语言任务上表现不均衡；ASR 在真实世界条件下（口音、情感、噪声、对话）显著下降，传统干净语音基准无法反映这些失败。

Conclusion: 语音 AI 系统应作为声学、表达、交互和鲁棒性能力的多维剖面进行评估，而非依赖单一聚合分数。RW-Voice-EQ Bench 为更全面的语音 AI 评测提供了框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向语音AI的多维评测基准（RW-Voice-EQ Bench），涵盖TTS、STS、SU、ASR等任务，属于生成模型评测与数据集方向。该论文与 canonical subtopic "generation_benchmark"（生成模型评测与数据集）高度匹配，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Focuses on voice AI benchmarking, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Voice AI benchmark, TTS/STS/SU/ASR evaluation, real-world conditions, no continuous language or video generation focus, no autoregressive or diffusion generation

Abstract: Current voice AI benchmarks typically evaluate isolated capabilities such as speech intelligibility, word error rate, or text-based dialogue quality, but they rarely test whether systems harness the acoustic information that distinguishes spoken language from its textual representation. To this end, we introduce the Real World Voice EQ Bench, a multidimensional benchmark for evaluating voice AI across text-to-speech (TTS), speech-to-speech (STS), speech understanding (SU), and automatic speech recognition (ASR). Our evaluations indicate that performance is highly dimension-specific. For TTS, naturalness, expressiveness, identity stability, and reliability are largely independent evaluation dimensions. For STS, access to audio does not guarantee use of vocal affect, and some agents remain largely transcript-driven. For SU, models perform unevenly across paralinguistic tasks. For ASR, real world accent, emotion, noise, and conversational conditions expose failures that are not captured by established clean-speech benchmarks. Together, these results show that voice AI should be evaluated as a profile of acoustic, expressive, interactional, and robustness capabilities rather than by a single aggregate score.

</details>

TL;DR: WanSong 是一个纯扩散模型驱动的歌曲生成基础模型，无需自回归阶段，可直接生成长达5分钟的高保真多语言歌曲，并同时输出人声和背景音乐双音轨，支持步蒸馏加速和下游编辑微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音乐生成方法多采用自回归+扩散级联的多阶段流水线，效率低且难以生成长时长、高保真的歌曲。同时，支持可控性和双音轨（人声+伴奏）输出的端到端方案仍缺乏。

Method: WanSong 采用纯扩散架构，直接对音频潜空间进行扩散建模，无需自回归阶段。模型通过步蒸馏（step-distillation）加速推理，并支持微调以适应下游编辑任务。单次前向传播即可生成最长5分钟的多语言歌曲，同时输出人声和背景音乐双音轨。

Result: WanSong 能够生成长达5分钟的高保真多语言歌曲，支持双音轨输出，推理速度通过步蒸馏得到显著提升，且具备良好的可控性和下游编辑微调能力。

Conclusion: WanSong 证明了纯扩散模型在长时长、高质量歌曲生成任务上的可行性，避免了复杂的级联流水线，为商业级音乐生成提供了高效、可控的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出纯扩散式歌曲生成模型 WanSong，支持长达5分钟的多语言歌曲生成并输出人声和背景音乐双音轨，属于音乐/音频生成范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 音频与多模态联合生成 (audio_av_generation)。

Importance reason: Pure diffusion song generation is novel but far from user's priority subtopics in continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: pure diffusion song generation, dual-stem output, step-distillation, no autoregressive stage, multilingual long-form audio

Abstract: Music generation foundation models have recently attracted significant industry attention. However, achieving efficient generation and high-fidelity long-form audio while supporting controllability remains challenging. To address these needs, we present \textbf{WanSong}, a simple yet powerful approach for long-form, commercial-grade song generation. Unlike autoregressive (AR) and cascaded multi-stage pipelines (\eg, AR followed by diffusion), \textbf{WanSong} is a pure diffusion-based model that directly generates high-fidelity, multilingual songs up to 5 minutes and outputs dual stems (vocals and background music) in a single run. In addition, our diffusion framework enables faster inference through step-distillation, and offers an efficient pathway for fine-tuning and customization to support downstream editing tasks.

</details>

### [58] [GeoDetect: Geometric Adversarial Detection for VLPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14737)
*Afsaneh Hasanebrahimi, Hanxun Huang, Christopher Leckie, James Bailey, Sarah Erfani*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoDetect，一种利用VLP嵌入空间几何结构（各向异性）来检测对抗样本的方法。理论分析表明对抗样本在嵌入空间中偏离数据流形，通过几何分数可有效检测，在多种VLP架构和攻击设置下表现鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言预训练模型（VLP）易受对抗攻击，但现有的对抗检测方法主要针对单模态（纯视觉或纯语言），在多模态VLP中的有效性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 首先分析VLP嵌入空间的几何结构，发现其存在与单模态视觉模型不同的结构化各向异性。理论证明在此各向异性结构下，对抗攻击会增加干净样本与对抗样本之间的期望几何距离。基于此，提出GeoDetect方法，利用几何分数（geometric scores）衡量样本偏离数据流形的程度，从而检测对抗样本。

Result: 在多种VLP架构和威胁设置（包括单模态攻击、多模态攻击和自适应攻击）下，GeoDetect均能可靠地检测对抗样本，展现出鲁棒性和实用性。

Conclusion: GeoDetect利用VLP嵌入空间的几何特性提供了一种有效的对抗样本检测方法，有助于提升多模态模型的安全性和可靠性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLP多模态模型的对抗攻击检测方法，属于多模态安全与攻防方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防" (multimodal_safety_attack)。

Importance reason: Focuses on adversarial detection in VLPs, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in generation, world models, or continuous representations. Signals: VLP adversarial detection, geometric embedding analysis, anisotropic structure, off-manifold detection, robust detection method

Abstract: Vision-language pre-trained models (VLPs) are widely used in real-world applications. However, they remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Although adversarial detection methods have demonstrated success in single-modality settings (either vision or language), their effectiveness and reliability in multimodal models such as VLPs remain largely unexplored. In this work, we study the geometry of VLP embedding spaces and observe structured anisotropy that differs from unimodal vision models. Our theoretical analysis shows that under this anisotropic structure, adversarial attacks increase the expected geometric separation between clean and adversarial examples (AEs). Specifically, we demonstrate that AEs consistently exhibit greater expected distances to randomly sampled points than their clean counterparts, indicating that AEs tend to push representations out of manifold regions. Building on these insights, we propose GeoDetect, which leverages these off-manifold deviations via geometric scores to identify AEs. Through comprehensive evaluations, we show that our approach reliably detects AEs across diverse VLP architectures and threat settings, covering unimodal and multimodal attacks as well as adaptive attacks, thereby providing a robust and practical approach to improving the safety and reliability of these models.

</details>

*Genglin Liu, Muye Zhang, Krishnamurthy Viswanathan, Nichole J. Hansen, Blaž Bratanič, Nathan L Clement, Shalini Ghosh, Ariel Fuxman*

TL;DR: 本文提出一个自动化的多智能体红队框架，通过迭代假设生成与变异策略系统性地合成对抗性难例，并利用多级验证委员会筛选高质量样本，最终通过测试时检索将这些样本作为上下文示例来提升目标MLLM的鲁棒性，在图像安全基准上将假阴性率从41.2%降至24.5%，无需任何人工标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在内容安全审核等任务中仍易受对抗攻击和分布外边缘案例的影响，传统主动学习和人工标注难以规模化应对新型多模态威胁，亟需自动化、可扩展的红队测试方法。

Method: 提出一个多智能体红队框架：(1) 高推理能力的Architect智能体负责提出新假设并对历史尝试进行变异，生成对抗性文本描述；(2) 高级图像生成器根据描述合成对抗性图像；(3) 多级验证委员会（多个LLM评分器）对生成的样本进行严格筛选；(4) 筛选后的高质量对抗样本通过测试时检索作为上下文示例，提升目标模型的鲁棒性。

Result: 在公开图像安全基准上，将目标模型的假阴性率（FNR）从41.2%降低至24.5%，且完全无需人工标注。

Conclusion: 自动化多智能体红队框架能够有效发现边界违规和模糊策略边缘案例，通过合成对抗样本作为上下文示例显著提升MLLM的鲁棒性，为大规模多模态安全审核提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出自动化红队框架，通过多智能体架构合成对抗性难例来攻击和防御多模态大模型，属于多模态安全与攻防方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防"。

Importance reason: Strong automated red-teaming framework for MLLM safety, but only tangentially touches your priority on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Multi-agent red-teaming, adversarial example synthesis, MLLM robustness, test-time retrieval, no human annotation

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed for nuanced content safety and moderation tasks, yet they remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks and out-of-distribution edge cases. Traditional active learning and manual annotation fail to scale against the complexity and volume of novel multimodal threats. In this paper, we propose an automated, agentic red-teaming framework that systematically synthesizes difficult examples using an iterative strategy that proposes novel hypotheses as well as mutating on past attempts. Leveraging a multi-agent architecture that consists of a high-reasoning Architect agent, an advanced image generator, and a multi-level verification committee of LLM raters, our system autonomously uncovers boundary-pushing violations and ambiguous policy edge cases without any human intervention. By employing these carefully synthesized adversarial examples as in-context demonstrations via test-time Retrieval, we substantially improve the target model's robustness, reducing the False Negative Rate (FNR) from 41.2% to 24.5% in a public image safety benchmark without relying on any human labeling.

</details>


### [60] [On Success and Simplicity: A Second Look at Transferable Vision-Language Attack Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14974)
*Yuchen Ren, Zhengyu Zhao, Chenhao Lin, Bo Yang, Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 SimVLA，一个简单但更有效的视觉语言对抗攻击流水线，通过解决跨模态交互不当和操作冗余问题，在可迁移性和效率上显著超越现有复杂方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型的可迁移对抗攻击方法普遍采用复杂的损失函数或多阶段文本/图像攻击流水线，但本文发现这些复杂设计反而引入了三个被忽视的问题（跨模态交互不当和操作冗余），因此希望设计一个更简单但更成功的攻击流水线。

Method: 提出 SimVLA 流水线，核心包括：(1) 跨模态词识别（Cross-modal Word Identification），利用领域知识识别与图像内容最相关的文本词进行攻击；(2) 简化损失函数，避免不必要的复杂交互；(3) 去除冗余的多阶段设计，实现单阶段高效攻击。

Result: 在 Flickr30k 图文检索任务上，SimVLA 在 R@1 可迁移性上超越 SOTA 基线 8.01%-14.71%，同时仅消耗约 35.73% 的时间和 46.26% 的最大显存。在四个数据集和三个下游任务上均验证了优越性。

Conclusion: 本文表明，在视觉语言对抗攻击中，利用领域知识（如跨模态词识别）比盲目追求复杂操作更为有效，SimVLA 可作为未来扩展的简单而有效的基线。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉语言预训练模型的可迁移对抗攻击，属于多模态安全与攻防方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防" (multimodal_safety_attack)。

Importance reason: Focuses on adversarial attack pipeline for VLPMs, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's key interests in generation, world models, or efficiency. Signals: adversarial attack, vision-language attack, transferability, efficiency improvement, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Vision-Language Pre-training Models (VLPMs) are known to be vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Recent transferable attacks on VLPMs have followed a common pipeline with complicated loss functions or multi-stage text/image attacks. However, in this paper, we demonstrate that such a sophisticated attack pipeline can be simpler yet more successful. Specifically, we identify three previously overlooked issues caused by inappropriate cross-modal interactions and excessive operations. To address them, we propose the Simple Vision-Language Attack (SimVLA) pipeline, which observably improves transferability and efficiency. Experiments on four datasets and three downstream tasks validate the superiority of our pipeline. For instance, on Flickr30k text-image retrieval dataset, our SimVLA outperforms the SOTA baseline in R@1 transferability by 8.01\%-14.71\%, while consuming only about 35.73\% of the time and 46.26\% of the max VRAM. Overall, the superiority of our SimVLA highlights the importance of leveraging domain knowledge (e.g., our proposed cross-modal word identification), while blindly pursuing intricate operations (e.g, complex loss functions and redundant multi-stage designs) may even be harmful. We hope our SimVLA can serve as a simple yet effective backbone for future extensions. Code is available at .

</details>


### [61] [When Words Are Safe But Actions Kill: Probing Physical Danger Beyond Text Safety in Hidden-State Risk Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15218)
*Weimeng Wang, Ziqiang Wang, Zihang Zhan, Chuanpu Fu, Qi Li, Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CR

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM中内容危险（CD）与物理危险（PD）在隐状态空间中是可分离的信号，并提出PRISM——一种基于全隐状态的单层L2正则化逻辑回归探针，用于检测具身智能体规划中的物理安全风险。在SafeAgentBench和PSB-1K上，PRISM以低误报率实现了高精度，优于同规模LLM法官。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用作具身智能体的高层规划器，但语言上安全的指令在物理世界中执行时可能产生危险后果。现有的文本级安全对齐方法无法区分"内容危险"和"物理危险"，导致要么漏检物理风险，要么过度拦截安全任务。因此需要一种能检测物理层面危险而非仅文本层面危险的方法。

Method: 通过隐状态方向分析和随机分割零假设检验，证明CD和PD在LLM表征中是可分离的。基于此提出PRISM：在完整隐状态上训练单层L2正则化逻辑回归探针，用于检测物理危险。同时构建了PhysicalSafetyBench-1K（PSB-1K）对比基准，包含1000对无直接伤害关键词的物理风险样本。

Result: PRISM在SafeAgentBench上达到86.2-87.7%准确率、11.7-13.7%误报率，而同规模LLM法官误报率为24.7-39.0%。在PSB-1K上PRISM达到99.6%准确率和0.7%误报率，而Qwen2.5-3B法官拒绝了67.8%的安全任务。PRISM在SafeText和EARBench上也验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 内容危险和物理危险在LLM表征中是可分离的，隐状态探针是一种有效的表征级物理安全检测方法，优于传统的文本级安全对齐方法。这为具身智能体的安全规划提供了新的思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM作为具身智能体规划器时的物理安全风险检测，属于多模态/具身场景下的安全对齐问题，与多模态安全与攻防（multimodal_safety_attack）这一CANONICAL子主题高度相关。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on LLM safety for embodied agents, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: safety probing, hidden-state analysis, physical danger detection, low priority subtopic, no video/image generation

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly serve as high-level planners for embodied agents, where linguistically benign instructions can become unsafe once grounded in the physical world. We study whether this physically grounded danger is the same safety problem as ordinary text-level content danger. Through hidden-state direction analysis and random-split null tests, we show that content danger (CD) and physical danger (PD) form separable signals in LLM representations across Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B/32B, Phi-3.5 and SmolLM2. Building on the CD/PD separability, we propose PRISM, a single-layer L2-regularized logistic probe over full hidden states. PRISM achieves 86.2--87.7\% accuracy on SafeAgentBench with 11.7--13.7\% FPR, while same-scale LLM judges over-block safe tasks at 24.7--39.0\% FPR. We further introduce PhysicalSafetyBench-1K (PSB-1K), a contrastive benchmark of 1{,}000 physical-risk pairs without direct harm keywords, to test whether methods detect physically grounded danger rather than explicit unsafe wording. On PSB-1K, PRISM reaches 99.6\% accuracy and 0.7\% FPR, whereas a Qwen2.5-3B judge rejects 67.8\% of safe tasks. PRISM also replicates on SafeText and EARBench, supporting hidden-state probing as a representation-level method for physical safety beyond text moderation.

</details>
