### [62] [CARPRT: Class-Aware Zero-Shot Prompt Reweighting for Black-Box Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14125)
*Ruijiang Dong, Zesheng Ye, Jianzhong Qi, Lei Feng, Feng Liu, Gang Niu, Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 CARPRT，一种类别感知的零样本提示重加权方法，通过为每个类别独立计算提示权重来改进黑盒视觉语言模型的零样本分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示集成方法对所有类别使用相同的权重向量，忽略了不同提示对不同类别的适用性差异（如"aerial view of"适合"airport"但不适合"apple"），导致次优的零样本分类性能。

Method: CARPRT 为每个类别标签和每个可用提示，通过计算在该提示下被预测为该类别的图像与文本的相关性分数平均值，量化提示-类别的特异性相关性，然后归一化得到类别特定的权重向量，整个过程无需训练。

Result: 在标准图像分类基准上，CARPRT 优于现有的类别无关重加权方法，验证了建模提示-类别依赖关系对零样本预测的有效性。

Conclusion: 建模提示与类别之间的依赖关系对于零样本预测至关重要，CARPRT 提供了一种简单有效的训练无关方案，可推广到更广泛的基于提示集成的 VLM 应用场景。

Classification reason: 该论文研究黑盒视觉语言模型的零样本分类提示加权方法，属于多模态对齐与偏好优化范畴，与 canonical subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on prompt ensembling for black-box VLMs, which is not aligned with user's priority on continuous models, video generation, or world models. Signals: Black-box VLM, prompt reweighting, zero-shot classification, class-aware weighting, no training required

Abstract: Pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) enable zero-shot image classification by computing the similarity score between an image and textual descriptions, typically formed by inserting a class label (e.g., "cat") into a prompt (e.g., "a photo of a"). Since the score for a given image-class pair is sensitive to the choice of prompt, existing studies ensemble multiple prompts using a weighting vector to aggregate scores across different prompts. Yet, in current strategies, the weighting vector assigned to each prompt is shared across all classes, implicitly assuming that prompts are conditionally independent of classes, which often does not hold in practice, as a prompt like "an aerial view of" might be apt for "airport" but ill-suited for "apple". To address this, we propose class-aware zero-shot prompt reweighting (CARPRT). This scoring scheme adjusts the weighting vector for each class label by capturing the class-specific relevance of different prompts in a training-free manner. For each class label and every available prompt, we quantify their class-specific relevance by averaging image-text relevance scores over images predicted to that class under the given prompt. These estimates are then normalized to derive class-specific weights. Evaluations on standard image classification benchmarks show that CARPRT outperforms existing class-independent reweighting methods, confirming that modeling prompt-class dependencies is crucial for effective zero-shot prediction and even broader VLM-based application settings that rely on prompt ensembling. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [63] [SEED: Self-Evolving On-Policy Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14777)
*Jinyang Wu, Shuo Yang, Zhengxi Lu, Fan Zhang, Yuhao Shen, Lang Feng, Haoran Luo, Zheng Lian, Shuai Zhang, Zhengqi Wen, Jianhua Tao*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (86.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出SEED框架，一种自演化在线策略蒸馏方法，将agentic RL中的稀疏轨迹奖励转化为密集的token级监督信号，通过让策略模型从已完成轨迹中提取hindsight技能并蒸馏回策略，实现样本高效且泛化能力强的agent训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于结果（outcome）的强化学习在长程agent任务中面临监督鸿沟：轨迹级别的稀疏奖励无法为中间决策步骤提供有效指导，导致token级策略学习缺乏细粒度信号。

Method: SEED框架包含两个核心阶段：(1) 技能提取——策略模型在完成轨迹后，作为分析器从中提取自然语言形式的hindsight技能（如可复用工作流、关键观察、避错规则）；(2) 在线策略蒸馏——在普通和技能增强两种上下文中对采样动作重新评分，利用技能引起的概率偏移构建密集的token级蒸馏信号，与基于结果的RL联合优化。

Result: 在文本和视觉agent任务上的大量实验表明，SEED一致提升了性能和样本效率，并对未见场景展现出鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SEED通过自演化的在线策略蒸馏有效弥合了agentic RL中结果级奖励与token级学习之间的监督鸿沟，为长程交互式agent训练提供了一种高效且可泛化的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SEED框架，利用on-policy强化学习对语言模型进行agentic任务训练，通过自演化蒸馏将轨迹级奖励转化为token级密集监督信号。核心方法属于强化学习与策略优化范畴，与多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）方向高度匹配，且论文在文本和视觉agent任务上均进行了实验验证。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel self-evolving distillation method for agentic RL, but its focus on multi-turn agent tasks and sparse reward conversion is only tangentially related to your core priorities in continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: agentic reinforcement learning, on-policy distillation, hindsight skill extraction, token-level supervision, sample efficiency

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly trained as interactive agents for long-horizon tasks involving multi-turn interaction, tool use, and environment feedback. Outcome-based reinforcement learning (RL) provides a practical optimization paradigm, but its sparse trajectory-level rewards offer limited guidance on intermediate decisions, leaving a supervision gap between episode-level outcomes and token-level policy learning. We propose SEED (SElf-Evolving On-Policy Distillation), a self-evolving framework that converts completed on-policy trajectories into training-time hindsight skills and distills their behavioral effect back into the policy model. SEED first fine-tunes the policy to analyze completed trajectories and generate natural-language skills that capture reusable workflows, decisive observations, or failure-avoidance rules. During RL, the current policy both collects trajectories and serves as the analyzer that extracts hindsight skills from them. Policy updates therefore improve subsequent decision making and skill analysis together, allowing hindsight supervision to evolve with the policy. SEED then re-scores the sampled actions under ordinary and skill-augmented contexts, converting the skill-induced probability shift into a dense token-level on-policy distillation signal. This signal is jointly optimized with outcome-based RL, keeping the auxiliary supervision aligned with the current trajectory distribution. Extensive experiments on text-based and vision-based agentic tasks show that SEED consistently improves performance and sample efficiency, exhibiting robust generalization to unseen scenarios. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [64] [Multimodality as Supervision: Self-Supervised Specialization to the Test Environment via Multimodality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14721)
*Kunal Pratap Singh, Ali Garjani, Rishubh Singh, Muhammad Uzair Khattak, Efe Tarhan, Jason Toskov, Andrei Atanov, Oğuzhan Fatih Kar, Amir Zamir*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (88.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Test-Space Training (TST)，一种在测试环境中收集多模态数据并进行跨模态自监督预训练的方法，使模型特化于特定环境。实验表明，仅利用测试环境的多模态数据进行自监督学习，即可达到与 DINOv2、CLIP 等大规模互联网预训练通用模型相当的性能，为减少对互联网大规模数据集的依赖提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际应用（如家用机器人）在部署环境中配备了丰富的多模态传感器，但现有方法通常依赖大规模互联网数据集进行预训练。受发展心理学启发，生物体通过多模态感知构建环境表征。本文旨在探索能否仅利用测试环境中的多模态数据，通过跨模态自监督学习获得高性能模型，从而减少对互联网大规模数据集的依赖。

Method: 提出 Test-Space Training (TST) 框架：在受限的测试环境中收集多模态数据（如 RGB、深度、红外等），然后在该数据上进行跨模态自监督预训练（如从一种模态预测另一种模态）。预训练后的模型在相同环境的多个下游任务上进行评估。通过控制实验设置，系统研究了多模态数据量、模态组合、预训练策略等因素对模型特化能力与泛化能力的影响。

Result: 实验表明，仅使用测试环境的多模态数据进行跨模态自监督预训练，即可在多个下游任务上达到与 DINOv2、CLIP 等大规模互联网预训练通用模型相当的性能。进一步分析发现，增加预训练数据量可使模型更好地特化于测试环境，但会降低对未见环境的泛化能力，揭示了特化与泛化之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 本文证明了多模态数据本身可以作为有效的自监督信号，在测试环境中进行特化预训练能够达到与大规模互联网预训练相当的效果。这为减少对互联网大规模数据集的依赖提供了可行方案，并揭示了数据量与特化/泛化权衡之间的重要关系。

Classification reason: 该论文研究利用多模态数据（跨模态学习）在测试环境中进行自监督预训练，使模型特化于特定环境。核心是多模态自监督学习与对齐，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）中的"多模态对齐"主题高度相关，且该 subtopic 涵盖多模态自监督学习范式。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel self-supervised specialization paradigm using test-environment multimodality, reducing reliance on internet-scale data. Signals: Self-supervised specialization, Cross-modal learning, Test-space training, Reduces internet data dependency, Multimodal sensing in deployment

Abstract: Cross-modal learning, i.e., learning to predict one modality from another, is a fundamental mechanism for self-supervision via leveraging multimodality. Many practical applications, e.g., deploying a household robot, involve devices that are equipped with a rich set of sensors that enable multimodal sensing in their test environment. This presents an opportunity to apply cross-modal learning to the multimodal data sensed by these devices to learn representations. Findings in developmental psychology also suggest that biological agents leverage it to build an effective representation of their surroundings. To study this, we propose a controlled setup, where we restrict a user device to just a given test environment. It results in a specialization setup where we attempt to develop a performant model for this specific test environment. Under this setup, we develop Test-Space Training (TST), which performs multimodal data collection in the test environment and performs self-supervised pre-training on it. We evaluate these models on various downstream tasks in the same environment. Under this setup, we find various interesting insights, such as collecting rich multimodal data only from the test environment and leveraging cross-modal learning, we can achieve competitive results with generalist models (e.g., DINOv2 and CLIP) pre-trained on large-scale internet datasets. This enables an alternative scenario where the need for external Internet-scale datasets for pre-training models is reduced. We also present a set of analyses and ablations that raise intriguing points on substituting data with (multi)modality, and how varying pre-training data enables a tradeoff between a model's abilities to specialise to a test environment, and generalize to held-out spaces.

</details>


### [65] [AlphaWiSE: Adaptive Weight Interpolation for Continual Multimodal Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15094)
*Sarthak Jain, Qiran Hu, Zhen Zhu, Yaoyao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (56.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AlphaWiSE 提出一种后验权重空间插值方法，通过为每个参数张量学习一个标量插值系数，组合两个冻结的源检查点，在持续多模态表示学习中保持跨模态对齐，无需额外推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型（如 CLIP）在持续适应新数据时，会破坏先前阶段学到的跨模态对齐。传统持续学习方法返回单一检查点，无法为所有检索方向同时优化稳定性-可塑性权衡。

Method: 提出 AlphaWiSE，一种后验权重空间插值方法。对于两个冻结的源检查点，为每个参数张量（按检查点键标识）拟合一个标量插值系数（所有张量条目共享），在小样本记忆集上优化这些系数，然后生成一个插值后的检查点。模型架构和参数量与源检查点相同，不增加推理时间。

Result: 在音频-图像-文本检索任务上，AlphaWiSE 在多个检索方向和评估指标上一致优于强持续学习基线方法。

Conclusion: AlphaWiSE 通过简单的权重空间插值有效缓解了持续多模态学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，保持了跨模态对齐质量，且无需修改模型架构或增加推理开销。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态模型（CLIP）在持续学习场景下的跨模态对齐问题，提出权重插值方法以保持跨模态检索的对齐质量。这与多模态对齐（multimodal alignment）主题高度相关，因此归入 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）子主题。

Importance reason: Proposes a practical post-hoc weight interpolation method for continual multimodal representation learning, but the subtopic is not a priority and lacks direct connection to priority subtopics. Signals: continual learning, multimodal representation, weight interpolation, CLIP, cross-modal retrieval

Abstract: Multimodal models such as CLIP learn a shared embedding space for cross-modal retrieval, but continual adaptation to sequentially arriving data can disrupt the cross-modal alignment acquired from earlier phases. Conventional continual-learning methods return a single checkpoint, which commits every retrieval direction to the same stability-plasticity trade-off. We propose AlphaWiSE, a post-hoc weight-space interpolation method that composes two frozen source checkpoints. For each aligned parameter tensor identified by its checkpoint key, AlphaWiSE fits one scalar interpolation coefficient shared by all tensor entries. The coefficients are fitted on a smaller exemplar memory and used to materialize one interpolated checkpoint. The deployed model has the same architecture and parameter count as either source checkpoint, which does not require additional inference time. Extensive experiments on audio-image-text retrieval show consistent improvements over strong continual-learning baselines across multiple retrieval directions and evaluation metrics.

</details>

### [66] [SD-MAR: Multi-image Analytical Reasoning via Synthetic Data and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14333)
*Shiyu Yuan, Sourav Sanjukta Bhabesh, Zhe Wang, Dmitriy Bespalov, Wesley Rose, Huzefa Rangwala*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (60.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SD-MAR 提出一个合成数据框架，用于训练和评估 VLM 的多图像分析推理能力（多图比较、变化检测、多步视觉推理），并采用 GRPO-lite 与 BDA 强化学习方法进行微调，在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 和 InternVL3-8B 上显著提升推理准确率，同时保持或改善跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在单图像感知任务上表现优异，但在需要跨多张图像进行对比分析、变化检测和多步推理的任务上能力不足，且缺乏相应的评测基准和训练数据。

Method: 提出 SD-MAR 框架：(1) 通过受控扰动构建成对视觉场景，生成语义变化归因和定量比较等推理任务；(2) 提出 GRPO-lite 与 Backward Discounted Allocation (BDA) 强化学习方法，去除 KL 正则化以加强策略优化，并将更多奖励分配给后期推理步骤。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 和 InternVL3-8B 上，域内准确率提升最高达 36.95%；Qwen2.5-VL-7B 在 SD-MAR 基准上超越 GPT-4.1；跨域泛化保持或提升（MME、MMMU-Pro、MathVista 波动在 1% 以内，MMBench 提升 4%）；LLM-as-judge 评估显示逻辑连贯性和解释质量一致提升。

Conclusion: SD-MAR 框架有效提升了 VLM 的多图像分析推理能力，合成数据与强化学习结合的方法在保持泛化性的同时显著提升了推理性能，为多图像推理这一重要但未被充分探索的能力提供了可行的训练与评估方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦多图像分析推理（multi-image analytical reasoning），涉及多步视觉推理、变化检测与比较推理，属于多模态推理范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot）涵盖视觉推理、多步推理等内容，与该论文高度匹配。

Importance reason: Solid work on multi-image reasoning but lacks direct connection to your priority continuous multimodal or world model directions. Signals: synthetic data for multi-image reasoning, GRPO-lite with BDA, Qwen2.5-VL-7B outperforms GPT-4.1, out-of-domain generalization preserved, no continuous token or world model focus

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate strong perceptual abilities but remain limited in tasks requiring analytical reasoning across multiple visual states, such as multi-image comparison, change detection, and multi-step visual inference. These capabilities are critical for real-world multimodal applications where reasoning must be grounded in systematic differences between visual contexts. However, existing benchmarks rarely require both explicit visual comparison and analytical reasoning, leaving this capability underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce SD-MAR (Synthetic Data for Multi-image Analytical Reasoning), a framework for training and evaluating VLMs on multi-image analytical reasoning. SD-MAR constructs paired visual scenarios through controlled perturbations and generates reasoning tasks spanning semantic change attribution and quantitative comparison. We further train VLMs using GRPO-lite with Backward Discounted Allocation (BDA), a reinforcement learning approach that removes KL regularization to encourage stronger policy optimization while allocating greater credit to the later reasoning steps where analytical conclusions are formed. Experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-7B and InternVL3-8B show that GRPO-lite fine-tuning on SD-MAR improves in-domain accuracy by up to 36.95%, with Qwen2.5-VL-7B outperforming GPT-4.1 on the SD-MAR benchmark. Importantly, out-of-domain generalization is preserved or improved: performance remains within 1% on MME, MMMU-Pro, and MathVista, while improving by up to 4% on MMBench. LLM-as-judge evaluation further demonstrates consistent improvements in logical coherence and explanation quality across both models.

</details>


### [67] [AdaTurn: Budget-Aware Test-Time Scaling for Active Visual Perception Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14547)
*Susan Liang, Chao Huang, Filippos Bellos, Jing Bi, Jason J Corso, Chenliang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (60.28) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AdaTurn 提出了一种预算感知的测试时扩展框架，通过条件化智能体于允许的交互轮数并引入 Forced-Answer DAPO 训练过预算边界行为，解决了主动视觉感知智能体在有限预算下因截断而无法给出有效答案的"灾难性截断"问题，显著提升了低预算场景下的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动视觉感知智能体在部署时面临不同的预算限制（交互轮数），现有方法将预算视为隐藏变量，导致当可用预算小于智能体偏好的轨迹长度时，交互被截断而无法产生有效答案（灾难性截断）。需要一种预算感知的方法来适应不同预算场景。

Method: 提出 AdaTurn 框架，核心组件为 Forced-Answer DAPO (FA-DAPO)，将超预算事件从被掩码或惩罚的失败转化为可训练的最后决策步骤，教会模型在无法继续使用工具时综合部分证据。同时，在训练和推理时随机化预算，并引入负载均衡调度器使操作可行。

Result: AdaTurn 显著提升了低预算下的准确率，例如在 VisualProbe-Medium 上，4轮预算下从 36.7% 提升至 47.6%，同时在大预算下保持强扩展性，并能有效迁移到多种骨干网络和通用多模态基准。

Conclusion: AdaTurn 通过预算感知训练和边界行为建模，有效解决了主动视觉感知智能体的灾难性截断问题，实现了不同预算下的自适应推理，为测试时计算扩展提供了新的视角。

Classification reason: 该论文研究主动视觉感知智能体在测试时根据预算（turn budget）进行推理时计算扩展（test-time scaling），属于多模态推理与推理时计算扩展范畴，与 canonical subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses test-time scaling and budget-aware truncation in active visual agents, relevant to efficient inference and multimodal reasoning. Signals: test-time scaling, budget-aware, active visual perception, catastrophic truncation, Forced-Answer DAPO

Abstract: Active visual agents solve fine-grained image tasks by interleaving reasoning with image-grounding actions across multiple turns. However, deployment-time rollout budgets are rarely fixed: some requests permit long rollouts, while others require the agent to act under a tight turn limit. Existing methods train the policy as if the rollout budget were hidden, so when the available budget is smaller than the trajectory the agent prefers, the interaction is often truncated before any valid answer is produced; we term this failure \emph{catastrophic truncation}. To overcome this challenge, we present AdaTurn, a budget-aware framework that conditions the agent on the allowed number of turns and explicitly trains the boundary behavior induced by the budget. Our key component, Forced-Answer DAPO (FA-DAPO), converts the over-budget event from a masked or penalized failure into a trainable final-decision step, teaching the model to synthesize partial evidence when further tool use is no longer possible. We further randomize rollout budgets during both training and inference and introduce a load-balanced scheduler that makes such operations practical. AdaTurn substantially improves low-budget accuracy, for example raising VisualProbe-Medium from 36.7% to 47.6% at four turns, while preserving strong scaling at larger budgets and transferring effectively to multiple backbones and general multimodal benchmarks.

</details>


### [68] [Gold-Guided Programmatic Distillation for Financial Reasoning over Hybrid Tables and Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14709)
*Yun Dong, Erica Zhao, Elana Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (42.79) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于执行验证的程序化蒸馏框架，利用教师模型生成可执行的Python程序而非自然语言推理链，通过金标准推导引导程序合成，并引入迭代恢复阶段，在TAT-QA金融问答数据集上使7B学生模型超越72B教师模型及多种强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融问答涉及混合表格与文本数据，需要多源推理和精确数值计算。大语言模型生成的中间推理步骤（自然语言推理链）容易出现算术错误，作为蒸馏的监督信号不可靠。因此需要一种更可靠的蒸馏方法，将数值推理能力从大模型迁移到小模型。

Method: 基于程序化蒸馏框架，使用执行验证的Python程序替代自由形式的文本推理链。具体包括：(1) 利用金标准推导（gold derivations）引导教师侧程序合成；(2) 仅保留执行正确且产生金标准答案的程序作为高质量监督；(3) 引入迭代恢复阶段，重新处理教师失败的样本，让学生模型从中恢复并纳入新验证的程序进行训练。

Result: 在TAT-QA测试集上，最佳7B学生模型达到87.00 EM / 87.18 F1，显著优于72B教师模型（78.46 EM）以及TAGOP、TAT-LLM等强基线。

Conclusion: 执行验证的程序化蒸馏为训练小模型进行可靠数值推理提供了一种有效且可扩展的框架，证明了程序化监督信号相比自然语言推理链在数值推理任务中的优越性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究混合表格与文本的金融问答推理，属于多模态推理（表格+文本）与数值推理范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链 (multimodal_reasoning_cot) 高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on financial reasoning distillation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword domain. Signals: financial reasoning, hybrid tables and text, programmatic distillation, execution verification, TAT-QA dataset

Abstract: Financial question answering over hybrid tabular and textual data may require multi-source reasoning and precise numerical computation. While large language models (LLMs) can generate intermediate reasoning steps, natural-language rationales remain prone to arithmetic errors, making them an unreliable supervision source for distillation. Building on programmatic distillation, we develop an approach that transfers reliable numerical reasoning from a large teacher model to a compact student using execution-verified Python programs instead of free-form textual rationales. It leverages gold derivations to guide teacher-side program synthesis and retains only programs that execute correctly and produce the gold answer, ensuring high-quality supervision. We further introduce an iterative recovery stage that revisits teacher-failed examples, enabling the student to recover and incorporate newly verified programs into training. Experiments on TAT-QA show that our framework is highly effective for hybrid financial reasoning. Our best 7B student achieves 87.00 EM / 87.18 F1 on the test set, substantially outperforming the 72B teacher (78.46 EM) as well as traditional and strong LLM-based baselines, including TAGOP and TAT-LLM. These results demonstrate that execution-verified programmatic distillation provides an effective and extensible framework for training smaller models to perform reliable numerical reasoning.

</details>


### [69] [WorkDrive: Roadwork Chain of Causation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14727)
*Tianyi Jiang, Wen Zhang, Sihan Yang, Ming Lu, Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: WorkDrive 提出面向自动驾驶施工区域的因果推理链框架，通过自动化多任务感知提取场景结构化事实，构建 Chain-of-Causation 标注用于监督微调，再结合基于横向元动作与轨迹一致性的 GRPO 强化学习，在 ROADWork 数据集上将轨迹 ADE 降低 12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶 VLM 在施工区域表现不佳，因为车道线、永久标志等熟悉视觉线索被改变或缺失，而 VLM 缺乏显式引导，无法将施工区域观测与正确的规划决策联系起来。

Method: 提出 WorkDrive 框架：(1) 自动化多任务感知管线提取结构化场景事实；(2) 将场景事实注入 Chain-of-Causation (CoC) 标注管线，引导标注器关注施工区域特有元素；(3) 用 CoC 推理标签进行监督微调；(4) 基于横向元动作与预测轨迹之间一致性的 GRPO 强化学习进一步优化。

Result: 在 ROADWork 数据集上，CoC 推理将轨迹平均位移误差 (ADE) 降低 9.0%，一致性 GRPO 进一步降低 3.0%，累计降低 12%。

Conclusion: WorkDrive 通过感知驱动的因果推理链有效提升了自动驾驶 VLM 在施工区域的轨迹预测能力，验证了结构化因果推理与强化学习结合的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 WorkDrive 框架，通过 Chain-of-Causation (CoC) 因果推理链将施工场景感知与轨迹预测对齐，属于多模态视觉推理与因果推理范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链 (multimodal_reasoning_cot) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on autonomous driving causal reasoning, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Chain-of-Causation, GRPO reinforcement learning, trajectory ADE reduction, ROADWork dataset, autonomous driving VLM

Abstract: Autonomous driving vision-language models (VLMs) struggle in roadwork zones, where familiar visual cues such as lane markings and permanent signs are altered or absent, and temporary devices such as cones and barriers redefine the drivable corridor. VLMs can detect these objects, but without explicit guidance they anchor their reasoning on familiar elements from pre-training and fail to connect work-zone observations to correct planning decisions. We propose WorkDrive, a framework that constructs perception-grounded causal reasoning for work zones and aligns it with trajectory prediction. An automated multitask perception pipeline extracts structured scene facts and injects them into a Chain-of-Causation (CoC) annotation pipeline, redirecting the annotator's attention to domain-specific elements. The resulting reasoning labels are used for supervised fine-tuning, followed by reinforcement learning with a single reward: consistency between lateral meta-actions and the predicted trajectory. On ROADWork, the largest public work-zone dataset, the proposed roadwork CoC reduces trajectory average displacement error (ADE) by 9.0\%, and consistency-based GRPO yields a further 3.0\%, achieving progressive improvement over the trajectory-only baseline. Code and data will be publicly released.

</details>


### [70] [TopoAgent: A Self-Evolving Topological Agent for Multimodal Scientific Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14658)
*Mingze Xu, Yinghui Li, Jiayi Kuang, Zhanhui Kang, Di Yin, Ying Shen, Xing Sun, Yuxing Han*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TopoAgent 提出了一种自演化的拓扑智能体框架，用动态有向无环图（DAG）替代传统线性推理流程，通过前端分解器将复杂科学问题拆解为视觉化原子，基于依赖关系组织成 DAG 实现严格上下文隔离，并引入自适应原子裂变机制在运行时动态拆分瓶颈节点。在数学、物理、化学基准上显著优于现有线性智能体框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在多模态科学推理中采用单一线性规划，存在视觉-语义错配、长上下文幻觉以及固定任务粒度下执行脆弱等问题，亟需一种更鲁棒、抗噪声、可自纠错的推理范式。

Method: TopoAgent 包含三个核心组件：(1) 前端分解器将复杂查询拆解为视觉化原子；(2) 基于依赖关系将原子组织为有向无环图（DAG），实现严格上下文隔离；(3) 自适应原子裂变机制，在运行时当工具能力边界被超出时动态将瓶颈节点拆分为更细粒度的子原子。

Result: 在数学、物理、化学等多个科学推理基准上的实验表明，TopoAgent 显著优于最先进的线性智能体框架，展现出更强的鲁棒性、抗噪声能力和自纠错能力。

Conclusion: TopoAgent 通过图结构化的自演化推理框架有效解决了线性规划在多模态科学推理中的局限性，为自主科学推理提供了一种鲁棒、抗噪声、可自纠错的新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 TopoAgent，一个面向多模态科学推理的自演化拓扑智能体框架，涉及数学、物理、化学等科学领域的多模态推理。这与 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链 (multimodal_reasoning_cot) 高度匹配，该子主题涵盖多模态 CoT、视觉推理、数学/科学推理等方向。

Importance reason: Proposes a DAG-based topological agent for scientific reasoning, but is not aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: Multimodal reasoning, DAG-based planning, Scientific benchmarks, Self-evolving agent, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel in general tasks, rigorous scientific reasoning remains challenging due to the limitations of monolithic, linear planning. Such sequential designs often suffer from visual-semantic misalignment, long-context hallucinations, and brittle execution under fixed task granularity. We propose TopoAgent, a self-evolving topological framework that replaces linear trajectories with dynamic, state-isolated graph evolution. TopoAgent first employs a front-end decomposer to fracture complex queries into visually-grounded atoms. These atoms are organized into a Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) based on their dependencies, enabling strict context isolation to shield the reasoning engine from irrelevant historical noise. Furthermore, we introduce adaptive atomic fission, which dynamically splits bottleneck nodes into finer-grained sub-atoms at runtime when tool capability boundaries are exceeded. Extensive experiments across mathematics, physics, and chemistry benchmarks demonstrate that TopoAgent significantly outperforms state-of-the-art linear agent frameworks, providing a robust, noise-resistant, and self-correcting paradigm for autonomous scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [71] [AI vs Human Expert Reasoning: Assessing Agreements in Building Typology Predictions based on Street View Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14756)
*Zahratu Shabrina, Muhammad Asa, Jin Rui, Lu Yin, Stephen Law*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

TL;DR: 本文研究 VLM（GPT-4o、Claude 3.5 Sonnet、Gemini 2.0 Flash）从街景图像推断建筑类型（结构、用途、层数）的能力，与人类专家标注进行对比。通过 Chain-of-Thought 提示和关键词概率分析，发现 VLM 更关注视觉线索，而人类专家更依赖上下文和领域知识。VLM 平均准确率约 70%，展示了在城市分析中辅助自动化分类的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索 VLM 在建筑类型分类任务中能否替代或辅助人类专家，评估其推理过程与人类推理的异同，为城市分析自动化提供依据。

Method: 使用 GPT-4o、Claude 3.5 Sonnet、Gemini 2.0 Flash 三种 VLM，结合不同缩放策略和提示技术（包括 Chain-of-Thought），从 Google 街景图像预测建筑类型（结构、用途、层数）。通过分析 AI 解释中关键词的出现概率，研究 VLM 推理模式，并与人类专家（土木工程师和建筑师）的标注进行对比。

Result: Chain-of-Thought 提示提供更稳定的模型性能；VLM 平均准确率约 70%；AI 更关注视觉指标，人类专家更依赖上下文线索和领域知识。

Conclusion: VLM 能在一定程度上近似人类专家的建筑类型分类能力，可作为城市分析中模式识别和目标检测的辅助工具，具有自动化和规模化应用的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文使用 VLM（GPT-4o、Claude 3.5 Sonnet、Gemini 2.0 Flash）进行建筑类型推断，并采用 Chain-of-Thought 提示策略分析推理过程，属于多模态推理与思维链范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 multimodal_reasoning_cot。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM reasoning comparison for urban classification, not on core continuous multimodal or generation priorities. Signals: VLM reasoning analysis, Chain-of-Thought prompting, street view imagery, building typology, human-AI agreement

Abstract: This research investigates the potential of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to infer building typologies: Construction, Current Use, and Storeys from Google Street View (GSV) images. Predictions generated by VLMs are compared with inference by human experts (civil engineers and architects) as a source of manually labelled ground-truth data. We evaluate several state-of-the-art VLMs, including GPT-4o, Claude 3.5 Sonnet, and Gemini 2.0 Flash. By applying different scaling strategies and prompting techniques, we found that Chain-of-Thought prompts provide an overall more stable model performance. We also investigate the reasoning behind VLMs' building-typology predictions by examining the probabilities of keywords appearing in AI explanations. This enabled us to analyse patterns in these reasonings and identify key themes driving both agreements and disagreements between VLM and expert labels. We find that AI tends to focus on visual indicators, whereas human experts place greater emphasis on broader contextual cues and domain knowledge, in addition to visual cues. Overall, VLM can approximate experts' capability in building-typology classification at scale, with an average accuracy of approximately 70%. The study demonstrates the VLM's potential for AI automation in tasks that require pattern recognition and object identification in an urban context. AI have the potential to serve as complementary and collaborative tools for urban analysis, leveraging their strengths in understanding visual patterns. This study contributes to the exploration of the efficiency and scalability of AI visual prediction and provides insights into the reasoning processes that could support automation processes in urban analysis and prediction.

</details>

### [72] [SceneBind: Binding What and Where Across Vision, Audio and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15265)
*Mingfei Chen, Zijun Cui, Ruoke Zhang, Hyeonggon Ryu, Eli Shlizerman*

Main category: cs.SD, cs.MM, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SceneBind 提出了一种全模态场景表示方法，将语义与3D空间信息结合，支持视觉、音频和语言之间的跨模态场景检索与物体定位，并在真实双耳音视频数据集上取得了SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态编码器擅长实例级语义理解（"有什么"），但缺乏显式的空间结构信息（"在哪里"）。SceneBind 旨在弥补这一空白，构建同时包含语义和3D空间信息的场景表示，支持跨模态场景检索和物体定位。

Method: SceneBind 将每个场景表示为语义-空间实体，包含全局语义嵌入和以物体为中心的语义-空间槽（slots），显式建模物体级语义、空间属性和不确定性。提出 SceneBind Matching 方案，结合全局场景相似度与物体对齐，支持跨模态场景检索和物体定位。同时构建了真实双耳音视频数据集，设计了跨模态语义与空间信号对齐的训练协议。

Result: 在场景检索和空间检索任务上达到SOTA，并在音视频定位等下游任务上展现出强大的零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: SceneBind 通过显式建模语义-空间联合表示，有效弥补了现有全模态编码器缺乏空间结构的不足，为跨模态场景理解与检索提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SceneBind，一种面向视觉、音频和语言的跨模态场景表示与检索方法，核心任务是跨模态场景检索和物体定位（object grounding），属于多模态检索范畴。论文中明确提到 "cross-modal scene retrieval" 和 "object grounding"，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong cross-modal scene retrieval but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: cross-modal retrieval, 3D spatial grounding, audio-visual dataset, object-centric slots, zero-shot localization

Abstract: We present SceneBind, an omni-modal representation of realistic scenes with joint semantic and 3D spatial understanding across vision, audio and language. Existing omni-modal encoders excel at instance-level semantics (i.e., what is present), but often lack explicit spatial structure (i.e., where it is). SceneBind addresses this gap by representing each scene as a semantic-spatial entity, combining a global semantic embedding with object-centric semantic-spatial slots. This representation explicitly captures object-level semantics, spatial attributes, and uncertainty. We further propose SceneBind Matching, a semantic-spatial matching scheme that integrates global scene similarity with object alignment, supporting cross-modal scene retrieval and object grounding. To train and evaluate SceneBind, we curate a novel real-world binaural audio-visual dataset with structured semantic and spatial annotations, and propose a training protocol for aligning semantic and spatial signals across modalities. SceneBind is compatible with large-scale pretrained semantic encoders, adds lightweight spatial modeling with only a few additional tokens. It achieves state-of-the-art scene and spatial retrieval while enabling strong zero-shot transfer to downstream tasks such as audio-visual localization.

</details>


### [73] [SUFLECA: Scaling Up Feature Learning for CAD-to-image Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15058)
*Saad Ejaz, Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas, Javier Civera, Holger Voos, Jose Luis Sanchez-Lopez*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SUFLECA提出了一种弱监督的零样本CAD-图像对齐框架，通过在大规模多源数据集上利用NOC监督学习几何感知特征，并结合几何一致性匹配算法，在ScanNet25k上以更小计算量超越现有零样本方法10.3/12.2个百分点，甚至首次超越全监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本CAD-图像对齐方法依赖外观驱动的视觉基础模型匹配，在遮挡或仿真到真实域迁移场景下性能严重退化。需要一种能够学习几何感知、跨域泛化的特征表示的方法来解决这一问题。

Method: SUFLECA包含两个核心贡献：(1) 在674K张图像（涵盖12个真实与合成数据集）上，通过归一化物体坐标（NOC）监督从预训练视觉表示中缩放几何感知特征学习，学习紧凑的几何感知特征；(2) 提出几何一致性匹配算法，建立可靠的一对一CAD-图像对应关系，无需迭代位姿精化即可实现亚秒级对齐。

Result: 在ScanNet25k基准上，SUFLECA达到33.4%/42.3%的类别/实例准确率，以更小计算量超越最强零样本基线10.3/12.2个百分点，并首次在该基准上超越全监督方法。

Conclusion: SUFLECA通过大规模几何感知特征学习和几何一致性匹配，有效解决了零样本CAD-图像对齐中的域迁移和遮挡问题，证明了弱监督方法在3D-2D跨模态对齐任务上可以超越全监督方法，为机器人操作和增强现实等应用提供了高效解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究CAD模型与RGB图像的对齐（CAD-to-image alignment），本质上是跨模态（3D CAD → 2D图像）的匹配与检索问题，属于多模态检索范畴。论文使用视觉基础模型提取特征并通过NOC监督学习几何感知特征，实现零样本的CAD-图像对应匹配，与多模态检索方向高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on CAD-to-image alignment for robotics, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Weakly-supervised zero-shot, 9D pose estimation, geometry-aware features, outperforms fully supervised, robotics/AR application

Abstract: CAD-to-image alignment aims to estimate an object's 9D pose (rotation, translation, and anisotropic scale) from a single RGB image, enabling applications in robotics and augmented reality. Recent zero-shot methods use visual foundation models to match image regions to CAD models, yet typically their correspondences are appearance-driven and degrade under occlusion or sim-to-real domain shift. To address these limitations, we introduce SUFLECA (Scaling Up Feature LEarning for CAD Alignment), a weakly-supervised framework for zero-shot CAD alignment with two key contributions. First, SUFLECA scales up geometry-grounded feature learning from pretrained visual representations through Normalized Object Coordinates (NOCs) supervision on 674K images spanning 12 real and synthetic datasets, learning compact geometry-aware features that generalize across domains. Second, we propose a geometrically consistent matching algorithm that establishes reliable one-to-one CAD-to-image correspondences. Together, these contributions enable accurate, sub-second alignment per object instance without iterative pose refinement. On ScanNet25k, SUFLECA achieves 33.4%/42.3% category/instance accuracy, outperforming, with a smaller computational footprint, the strongest zero-shot baseline by 10.3/12.2 percentage points and, for the first time on this benchmark, even surpassing fully supervised methods. Code is available at:

</details>

### [74] [SafeRelBench: A Spatial-Relation-Aware Benchmark for Process-Level Safety in VLM-Driven Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14543)
*Huaigang Yang, Ya Li, Min Ren, Bo Dai, Zhenliang Zhang, Zhaofeng He*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SafeRelBench是一个面向VLM驱动具身智能体的空间关系感知过程级安全评测基准，包含507个可执行样本，评估智能体在执行任务过程中是否违反支撑、包含、邻近等空间关系导致的安全约束。实验发现现有VLM智能体在任务成功率和过程安全性之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身安全评测主要关注静态风险识别、不安全指令拒绝或最终状态任务完成，缺乏对动作过程中由空间关系（如支撑、包含、邻近）引发的过程级安全失效的系统研究。

Method: 构建SafeRelBench基准，包含507个可执行评测样本（248个空间关系样本和259个非空间控制样本），系统测试VLM驱动具身智能体在执行风险动作前是否满足安全条件，将空间关系作为具身安全评估的核心维度。

Result: 对7个开源和闭源VLM驱动具身智能体的评测显示，模型在完成任务的同时往往违反过程级安全约束，任务成功与过程安全之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 安全的具身智能不仅需要更强的感知和规划能力，还需要对物体关系如何在交互过程中塑造风险进行可靠的推理。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向VLM驱动具身智能体的空间关系感知安全评测基准（SafeRelBench），属于多模态评测与基准方向。同时涉及具身世界模型中的安全评估。因此映射到[多模态评测与基准]（CANONICAL）和[具身世界模型与规划控制]（CANONICAL）。

Importance reason: Focuses on embodied agent safety evaluation, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: VLM-driven embodied agents, spatial-relation safety benchmark, process-level safety, task success vs. safety gap, household environments

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as the reasoning backbone of embodied agents, enabling robots to interpret visual scenes, follow language instructions, and plan multi-step actions. In household environments, however, safety depends not only on recognizing objects, but also on how actions change the physical scene over time. Existing embodied safety evaluations largely focus on static risk recognition, unsafe instruction refusal, or final-state task completion. As a result, process-level safety failures induced by spatial relations such as support, containment, and proximity remain insufficiently studied. To address this gap, we introduce SAFERELBENCH, a spatial-relation-aware safety benchmark with 507 executable evaluation samples, including 248 spatial-relation samples and 259 non-spatial control samples. Using SAFERELBENCH to evaluate seven open- and closed-source VLM-driven embodied agents, we find a substantial gap between task success and process-level safety compliance: models often complete the requested task while violating process-level safety constraints. Unlike prior benchmarks, SAFERELBENCH explicitly tests whether agents satisfy safety conditions before risk-prone actions, making spatial relations a core dimension in embodied safety assessment. More broadly, our results show that safe embodied intelligence requires not only stronger perception and planning, but also reliable reasoning about how object relations shape risk during interaction.

</details>


### [75] [Just Keep Prompting: Evaluating Repetitive Socratic Prompting in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14099)
*Shayda Moezzi, Bishoy Galoaa, Lorena Genua, Taskin Padir, Sarah Ostadabbas*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (56.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 JKP（Just Keep Prompting），一个多轮对话评测框架，通过对抗性否定、纯苏格拉底式追问和上下文感知苏格拉底式总结三种策略，在最多10轮追问中评估 VLM 的认知稳定性。在 GPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Pro 和 Qwen3-VL-30B 上的实验表明，重复追问总体准确率变化不大，但轨迹层面存在显著不稳定性：正确答案退化、错误答案恢复、频繁答案翻转。重复追问的收益有限，更多是 destabilizer 而非推理辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 评测主要关注单轮或少轮问答的准确性，忽略了真实部署中用户可能反复质疑、追问模型答案的场景。模型在多轮对话压力下的稳定性（epistemic stability）尚未被系统评估。

Method: 提出 JKP 评测框架，包含三种追问策略：(1) Adversarial Negation——反复拒绝模型答案；(2) Pure Socratic Interrogation——反复要求模型重新评估确定性；(3) Context-Aware Socratic Summarization——先总结模型之前的推理再要求重新考虑。在 STAR 基准的子集上对 GPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Qwen3-VL-30B 进行 720 次多轮运行评测。

Result: 从 Turn 0 到 Turn 10 的总体准确率变化不大，但轨迹分析显示显著不稳定性：正确答案会退化，错误答案会恢复，许多运行出现反复答案翻转。Qwen3-VL-30B 最终准确率最高但在直接矛盾下变得自信地错误；Gemini 2.5 Pro 相对稳定但 token 开销大；GPT-4o 最脆弱且振荡最严重。

Conclusion: 重复追问的收益有限，更多是 destabilizer 而非推理辅助。多轮 VLM 评测不仅捕捉额外推理能力，还揭示了模型的压力响应特征——模型在重复挑战下如何权衡视觉基础、校准和对话顺从性。建议未来 VLM 评测纳入多轮稳定性维度。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 JKP，一个多轮对话评测框架，用于评估 VLM 在持续追问下的稳定性（epistemic stability）。这属于多模态评测范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Evaluates VLM stability under repeated prompting, but focuses on general multi-turn evaluation rather than the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: VLM epistemic stability, multi-turn evaluation framework, Socratic prompting strategies, model-dependent instability, GPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Pro, Qwen3-VL-30B

Abstract: Deploying Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in real-world settings requires not only strong visual reasoning but also stability under sustained conversational pressure. We introduce Just Keep Prompting (JKP), a multi-turn evaluation framework that measures VLM epistemic stability when users repeatedly challenge, question, or contradict a model's answer. JKP probes models for up to 10 follow-up turns using three strategies: Adversarial Negation (repeated rejection), Pure Socratic Interrogation (repeated calls to reassess certainty), and Context-Aware Socratic Summarization (reflecting the model's prior rationale back before asking for reconsideration). We evaluate GPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Pro, and Qwen3-VL-30B on a subset of the STAR benchmark across 720 multi-turn runs. Aggregate accuracy changes modestly from Turn 0 to Turn 10, but trajectory-level analysis reveals substantial instability: correct answers regress, wrong answers recover, and many runs exhibit repeated answer flipping. Repeated prompting has bounded upside and often acts as a destabilizer rather than a reasoning aid. The effect is strongly model-dependent: Qwen3-VL-30B achieves the highest final accuracy but becomes confidently wrong under direct contradiction; Gemini 2.5 Pro is comparatively stable but token-expensive; GPT-4o is the most brittle and oscillatory. These findings reveal that multi-turn VLM evaluation captures not just additional reasoning but pressure-response profiles: how models trade off visual grounding, calibration, and conversational compliance under repeated challenge.

</details>


### [76] [OmniaBench: Benchmarking General AI Agents Across Diverse Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14989)
*Chengyu Shen, Yujie Fu, Gangtao Xin, Yanheng Hou, Wenlong Fei, Guojie Zhu, Jiawei Li, Hongcheng Gao, Runming He, Zhen Hao Wong, Meiyi Qiang, Hao Liang, Zhao Cao, Hao Jiang, Chong Chen, Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OmniaBench是一个面向通用AI智能体的综合评测基准，基于层次化场景分类体系（90个一级域、354个二级域）构建了1431个单轮/多轮任务，引入十维能力分类和八种组合难度因子，对当前前沿模型（如Claude-Sonnet-5、GPT-5.6-Sol）进行评测，发现其Overall Pass@1仅约58%，揭示了模型在规划、约束保持和自适应修正方面的持续局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评测基准往往局限于特定场景、工具生态或交互格式，难以系统地表征模型在异构应用场景中的能力边界。作者希望构建一个覆盖广泛场景、支持细粒度能力分析的通用智能体评测基准。

Method: 从应用商店、产品文档、行业资源、网页检索和人工精炼中提取场景知识，构建层次化分类体系（ToC/ToB/ToE，90个一级域、354个二级域）。基于该分类体系构建可执行环境，通过DAG、DAG-S、Solver和Program四种互补路线合成单轮和多轮任务。引入十维能力分类和八种组合原子难度因子，支持细粒度评测分析。最终数据集包含1431个任务，并从中筛选出644个高难度子集以降低评测成本。

Result: 当前最强模型Claude-Sonnet-5和GPT-5.6-Sol的Overall Pass@1分别仅为58.54和57.14。不同领域和能力维度上存在明显差异，模型在规划、约束保持和自适应修正方面存在持续局限。

Conclusion: OmniaBench为通用AI智能体提供了一个广泛且具有诊断能力的评测基准，揭示了当前前沿模型在复杂多场景任务中的能力边界和不足，为未来智能体能力提升提供了方向性指导。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向通用AI智能体的评测基准OmniaBench，覆盖ToC/ToB/ToE多场景、多轮交互任务，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的评测基准。虽然论文主要关注LLM智能体而非传统多模态VLM，但其评测框架、能力分类体系与多模态评测基准高度相关，因此复用[多模态评测与基准]这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Broad agent benchmark but lacks focus on user's priority continuous multimodal and generation subtopics. Signals: General agent benchmark, hierarchical scenario taxonomy, ten-dimensional capability analysis, reveals planning and constraint limitations, not aligned with user's continuous language/video generation focus

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly evolving from text generators into general agents capable of understanding user requests, invoking external tools, and completing complex tasks through interaction. However, existing agent benchmarks often focus on limited scenarios, tool ecosystems, or interaction formats, making it difficult to systematically characterize model capabilities across heterogeneous application settings. We introduce OmniaBench, a benchmark for evaluating general agents across diverse scenarios with explicit state spaces. We derive application-oriented scenario knowledge from app stores, product documents, industry resources, Web retrieval, and human refinement, forming a hierarchical taxonomy that spans ToC, ToB and ToE with 90 level-1 and 354 level-2 domains. Based on this taxonomy, we construct executable environments and synthesize single-turn and multi-turn tasks through four complementary routes: DAG, DAG-S, Solver, and Program. OmniaBench further introduces a ten-dimensional capability taxonomy and eight compositional atomic difficulty factors to support fine-grained evaluation and analysis. The resulting dataset contains 1,431 tasks, together with a challenging subset of 644 tasks designed to reduce evaluation cost and mitigate potential contamination of the full set after public release. The bench presents substantial challenges to current frontier models, with even Claude-Sonnet-5 and GPT-5.6-Sol achieving Overall Pass@1 scores of only 58.54 and 57.14, respectively. Further analyses reveal clear differences across domains and capabilities, as well as persistent limitations in planning, constraint maintenance, and adaptive correction. OmniaBench provides a broad and diagnostic benchmark for characterizing the capability boundaries of general agents.

</details>


### [77] [VIABench: A Comprehensive Video Benchmark Collected from Blind Individuals for Visual Impairment Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14660)
*Yunfeng Liu, Yuandong Yang, Jiarui Han, Zhenpeng Huang, Yuqing Tang, Xiangyu Zeng, Gangshan Wu, Limin Wang*

TL;DR: VIABench 是一个由盲人群体录制的第一人称视频基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在视障辅助场景中的能力，涵盖主动提醒、视觉问答和视觉引导交互三个核心任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视障人士在日常生活中面临视觉信息获取困难，虽然 MLLM 在通用视觉语言任务上表现优异，但其在真实盲人辅助场景中的实用性尚未被充分探索，缺乏专门的评测基准。

Method: 收集盲人群体录制的第一人称视频，定义三个核心任务（主动提醒、VQA、视觉引导交互），设计支持在线（实时）和离线两种设置的严格评测流程。

Result: 实验表明当前 MLLM 在视障辅助场景中仍难以提供全面支持，尤其在需要准确预测和实时响应的主动提醒任务上表现不佳。

Conclusion: VIABench 揭示了现有 MLLM 在真实盲人辅助场景中的不足，有望推动面向实际辅助场景的定制化 MLLM 研究，改善视障人士的导航与交互体验。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 VIABench，一个专门用于评估 MLLM 在视障辅助场景中表现的第一人称视频基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此复用 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Novel benchmark for MLLMs in assistive contexts, but outside user's core priority subtopics and negative keywords apply. Signals: multimodal_understanding_generation, video benchmark, blind assistance, proactive reminder, VQA

Abstract: Visually impaired individuals (VIIs) encounter significant daily challenges due to limited access to visual information. Although Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved impressive results on general vision and language tasks, their practical utility in real-world blind assistance still remains largely underexplored. To fill this gap, we introduce VIABench, a comprehensive video benchmark specifically designed to evaluate MLLMs in Visually Impaired Assistance scenarios using first-person videos recorded or shared by VIIs themselves. VIABench defines three core tasks, each targeting a distinct requirement in visual assistance. Proactive Reminder: Assesses the model's ability to interpret ongoing video content while proactively anticipating and verbally describing upcoming navigation-critical events; Visual Question Answering (VQA): Evaluates the model's capacity to answer user-posed questions about the environment or objects within the video; Vision-Guided Interaction: Tests context-aware reasoning to accomplish intentional interactions between user and environment. To ensure a robust and fair evaluation, we propose a rigorous benchmarking pipeline that supports both online (real-time) and offline settings. Our experiments demonstrate that current MLLMs still struggle to deliver comprehensive support for VIIs, especially in the Proactive Reminder task, which demands accurate anticipation and real-time responsiveness. We hope VIABench will drive future research toward developing customized MLLMs for real-world assistance, ultimately improving navigation and interaction experiences for visually impaired individuals. Code and data will be released at .

</details>


### [78] [Contextualized Evaluation of Vision Language Models through Dynamic, Multi-turn Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14499)
*Yijiang Li, Huiqi Zou, Bingyang Wang, Ziang Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出CEDI框架，将MLLM评测重构为评估模型、自动考官和评分者三方交互，通过基于图的多轮半结构化对话动态评估模型能力。在视觉幻觉任务上的实验表明，这种上下文化交互式评测比传统静态评测能发现更多且更贴近实际使用场景的幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM基准测试在静态、受控环境下评估模型，与真实世界中动态、交互式、上下文化的应用场景存在根本性错位，导致基准分数无法反映模型实际能力。

Method: 提出CEDI框架，将评测重构为三方交互：评估模型（evaluatee model）、自动考官（automated examiner）和评分者（grader）。考官通过基于图的状态空间表示引导多轮半结构化对话，采用多种策略（如澄清请求、对抗性探测）来获取模型性能证据。

Result: 在多个模型、设置、数据集和领域上的实验表明，上下文化交互式评测比传统静态评测发现显著更多的幻觉，且这些幻觉更接近实际使用场景。幻觉会在长上下文中通过自我强化的对话历史累积，模型在需要前提拒绝或拒绝回答的问题上尤其脆弱。

Conclusion: CEDI框架为MLLM能力评估提供了一种更真实、系统且生态效度更高的方法，揭示了静态评测无法捕捉的模型缺陷，是迈向现实世界MLLM评测的重要一步。

Classification reason: 该论文提出CEDI框架，通过动态多轮交互对MLLM进行上下文化评测，属于多模态评测方法，与[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）这一CANONICAL subtopic高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a dynamic evaluation framework for MLLMs, but the focus on visual hallucination benchmarks is not aligned with your priority subtopics on continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: MLLM evaluation, dynamic multi-turn interaction, visual hallucination, benchmark misalignment, graph-based dialogue

Abstract: Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have made substantial advances on benchmarks, yet their real-world effectiveness remains uncertain. This gap stems from the fundamental misalignment between benchmarks in controlled, static settings and the dynamic, interactive, and contextualized nature of real-world applications. To bridge this gap, we propose CEDI (Contextualized Evaluations of MLLMs through Dynamic, multi-round Interactions), a framework that recasts evaluation as a three-party interaction between an evaluatee model, an automated examiner, and a grader. The examiner conducts multi-turn, semi-structured conversation guided by a graph-based representation of the task. By navigating state-space transitions, CEDI deploys diverse strategies, from clarification requests to adversarial probes, to elicit performance evidence. We apply CEDI to visual hallucinations. Empirical results across multiple models, diverse settings, datasets, and domains show that contextualized, interactive evaluations reveal not only significantly more hallucinations than conventional static evaluation but also ones that more closely resemble those arising in practical use cases. We further show that hallucinations often accumulate over long contexts, through self-reinforcing dialogue history, and models are particularly vulnerable to questions requiring premise rejection or refusal. Together, these findings highlight CEDI as a step toward realistic, systematic, and ecologically valid assessments of MLLMs' capabilities. Code is available at .

</details>


### [79] [SportD: Can VLMs Physically Strategize?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14616)
*Jasin Cekinmez, Addison J. Wu, Haotian Xia, Akshaya Bharadhwaj, Anay Putty, Anirudh Ravishankar, Jaewoong Lee, Jinglin Xiao, Kyumin Andrew Shim, Mishika Ahuja, Nisarga Patil, Leo Liu, Zhuohan Liu, Weining Shen*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (56.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 SportD 基准，包含 2022 年世界杯 478 个场上决策事件，用于评测 VLM 在足球场景中的物理策略推理能力。通过基于控球价值的评估模型衡量模型决策质量，发现最佳 VLM 仅 31.4% 选择最优动作（职业球员为 38.9%），且模型倾向于低方差、低回报的动作，表现出对熟悉模式的模仿而非真正的反事实评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 评测多关注视觉理解任务，但缺乏对模型在物理世界中基于视觉信息进行策略性决策能力的量化评估。足球场景提供了可量化的决策价值评估框架，适合研究 VLM 的物理策略推理能力。

Method: 构建 SportD 基准，从 2022 年世界杯比赛中提取 478 个持球决策事件。每个事件包含比赛视频片段，模型需选择射门或传球给特定队友。采用控球价值模型（possession-value model）计算每个可选动作的预期进球概率，以此作为客观评估标准，衡量最优动作准确率和次优决策的遗憾值。

Result: 最佳 VLM（GPT-4o）在 31.4% 的事件中选择最高价值动作，低于职业球员的 38.9%。所有模型均表现出显著更高的遗憾值。VLM 系统性地偏好低方差、低回报动作：射门频率低于最优策略和真实球员，选择的传球也缺乏进攻性。模型在球员选择次优动作时仍能以高于随机水平的概率模仿该动作，表明存在部分模仿而非真正的反事实评估。

Conclusion: 当前前沿 VLM 在物理策略推理方面仍显著落后于职业球员，倾向于保守决策且缺乏对反事实替代方案的系统评估。SportD 为衡量 VLM 的物理策略推理能力提供了一个价值驱动的评测框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 SportD 基准，用于评测 VLM 在足球场景中的物理策略推理能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Evaluates VLM physical reasoning in sports but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: VLM physical strategy benchmark, soccer decision-making, possession-value evaluation, low optimal-action accuracy, imitation vs counterfactual reasoning

Abstract: Vision--language models have become increasingly capable of interpreting visual scenes, but it remains unclear whether they can use information to make strategically effective decisions. We investigate this question in soccer, where models observe the seconds preceding an on-ball decision and must choose whether to shoot or pass to a specific teammate. Unlike conventional visual-understanding tasks, soccer enables decisions to be evaluated quantitatively by estimating the value of every available action. We introduce SportD, a benchmark comprising 478 on-ball decisions from the 2022 FIFA World Cup. Each model choice is evaluated against a possession-value model that estimates the action that most increases the attacking team's probability of scoring, allowing us to measure both optimal-action accuracy and the value forfeited by suboptimal decisions. Across three frontier VLMs, the best selects the highest-valued action on 31.4% of events, compared with 38.9% for the professional players, and all models incur significantly greater regret. Further analysis reveals a systematic preference for lower-variance and lower-reward actions: VLMs shoot less often and select substantially less progressive passes than either the optimal policy or the real players. The models also reproduce the player's specific action above chance even when that action is suboptimal, suggesting partial imitation of familiar play patterns rather than consistent evaluation of counterfactual alternatives. SportD provides a value-grounded testbed for measuring physical strategic reasoning in VLMs.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文对6个多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在科学可视化素养评估测试（SciVis）上进行基准测试，包含18个科学可视化/插图的49个题目，覆盖8种技术和11种任务类型，并与485名人类参与者数据对比。结果显示Gemini最强，超过人类均值，开源模型低于人类基线；模型在科学插图、搜索和空间理解上表现好，但在纹理/集成可视化及定量估计上表现差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM评估主要集中于图表（chart）理解，缺乏对科学可视化（SciVis）素养的系统评估，无法验证模型是否真正理解科学可视化中的复杂视觉编码和物理现象。

Method: 采用标准化的科学可视化素养评估测试（SciVis），包含18个科学可视化和插图的49个题目，覆盖8种可视化技术和11种任务类型。在封闭世界协议下评估3个闭源模型（GPT-4o、Gemini 1.5 Pro、Claude 3.5 Sonnet）和3个开源模型（LLaVA-1.6、InternVL2、Qwen2-VL），并与485名人类参与者数据对比。

Result: Gemini 1.5 Pro表现最强，超过人类均值；开源模型均低于人类基线。模型在科学插图、搜索任务和空间理解上表现较好，但在纹理/集成可视化及定量估计任务上表现差。错误分析揭示模型在细粒度定量估计、流向解释和接地编码解释方面存在系统性失败。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM在科学可视化素养上表现不均衡，SciVis素养应作为多模态AI系统评估的必要基准维度，为未来模型改进提供方向。

Classification reason: 该论文对6个MLLM在科学可视化素养评估测试（SciVis）上进行基准测试，属于多模态评测与基准方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Evaluates MLLMs on scientific visualization literacy, but the topic is not aligned with user's priority subtopics and is penalized by negative keywords. Signals: MLLM benchmark, scientific visualization, human comparison, Gemini outperforms, not chart-centric

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interpret visualizations, yet current evaluations remain largely chart-centric and provide limited evidence of understanding of scientific visualization (SciVis). We benchmark six MLLMs on the scientific visualization literacy assessment test, a standardized SciVis literacy assessment comprising 49 items based on 18 scientific visualizations and illustrations, spanning 8 techniques and 11 task types. We evaluate three closed-source and three open-source models under a closed-world protocol and compare their performance using data from 485 human participants. Results show that current MLLMs do not exhibit uniform SciVis literacy. Gemini is the strongest model overall, exceeding the human mean across the evaluated subsets, whereas the open-source models remain below the human baseline. Performance is highly uneven across techniques and tasks: models perform best on scientific illustration, search, and spatial understanding, but struggle on texture-based and integration-based visualizations and on quantitative estimation. Error analysis reveals recurring failures in fine-grained quantitative estimation, flow-direction interpretation, and grounded encoding interpretation. These findings position SciVis literacy as a necessary benchmark dimension for evaluating multimodal AI systems. Our code and model outputs are publicly available at .

</details>


### [81] [Symbal: Detecting Systematic Misalignments in Model-Generated Captions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15216)
*Maya Varma, Jean-Benoit Delbrouck, Sophie Ostmeier, Akshay Chaudhari, Curtis Langlotz*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Symbal提出了一种利用现成基础模型的双阶段框架，用于检测MLLM生成图像描述中的系统性错位错误（即特定视觉特征与特定描述错误之间的关联），并构建了SymbalBench基准（170万图像-文本对，420个数据集）进行评估，相比基线方法性能提升近4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在生成图像描述时经常引入错误，导致图像-文本对错位。现有方法主要关注单个样本级别的错误检测，但缺乏对系统性错位（即某种视觉特征反复导致某种描述错误）的检测能力。本文旨在填补这一空白，帮助用户审计MLLM生成的描述数据集。

Method: Symbal采用双阶段结构：第一阶段使用现成的基础模型（如视觉模型和语言模型）从图像-文本对中提取候选视觉特征和候选描述错误；第二阶段通过统计关联分析检测视觉特征与描述错误之间的系统性关联，并以自然语言总结结果。SymbalBench包含来自自然图像和医学图像两个领域的170万图像-文本对，组织成420个带有标注的系统性错位数据集。

Result: Symbal在SymbalBench上正确识别了63.8%的数据集中的系统性错位，相比最佳基线方法提升近4倍。在真实场景评估中，Symbal能够准确发现四种不同MLLM生成描述中的系统性错位，并能有效审计现成的图像-描述数据集。

Conclusion: Symbal作为一种无需访问底层MLLM的审计工具，能够有效检测图像描述中的系统性错位错误，为提升多模态数据质量和模型可靠性提供了重要支持。该任务、方法和基准为多模态系统的安全审计和错误分析开辟了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出检测MLLM生成图像描述中的系统性错位（systematic misalignment）这一新任务，并构建了包含170万图像-文本对的SymbalBench基准来评估该方法。核心贡献是任务定义、检测方法和评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on caption error detection, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal evaluation, systematic misalignment detection, benchmark creation, not in priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often introduce errors when generating image captions, resulting in misaligned image-text pairs. Our work focuses on a class of captioning errors that we refer to as systematic misalignments, where a recurring error in MLLM-generated captions is closely associated with the presence of a specific visual feature in the paired image. Given a vision-language dataset with MLLM-generated captions, our aim in this work is to detect such errors, a task we refer to as systematic misalignment detection. As our first key contribution, we present Symbal, which utilizes a structured, dual-stage setup with off-the-shelf foundation models to identify systematic misalignments and summarize results in natural language. As our second key contribution, we introduce SymbalBench, a benchmark designed to evaluate automated methods on our proposed task. SymbalBench consists of 1.7 million image-text pairs from two domains (natural and medical images), organized into 420 vision-language datasets with annotated systematic misalignments. Symbal exhibits strong performance on this benchmark, correctly identifying systematic misalignments in 63.8% of datasets, a nearly 4x improvement over the closest baseline. We supplement our evaluations on SymbalBench with real-world evaluations, showing that (1) Symbal can accurately surface systematic misalignments in captions generated by four MLLMs and (2) Symbal is a powerful tool for auditing off-the-shelf image-caption datasets. Ultimately, our novel task, method, and benchmark can aid users with auditing MLLM-generated captions and identifying critical errors, without requiring access to the underlying MLLM. Code is available at .

</details>


### [82] [HoloGeo: Mitigating Landmark Bias in Geo-localization via Evidence-Driven Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15255)
*Pengcheng Zhou, Xuanyu Liu, Yanchen Yin, Bobo Li, Shengqiong Wu, Mong-Li Lee, Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: HoloGeo 提出证据驱动的推理框架，通过构建 BF-30k 数据集和多维奖励机制，缓解 VLM 在地理定位中的地标偏差问题，在多个基准上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在地理定位中容易受到地标偏差影响，即过度依赖地标特征而忽略其他地理线索，导致定位不准确。目前缺乏对这一问题的系统性量化和缓解方法。

Method: 首先设计 Bias Intensity (BI) 和 Bias Harmfulness (BH) 两个量化指标，构建 LandmarkBias-3K 基准。然后提出 HoloGeo 框架，包含 BF-30k 高质量数据集（标注了结构化多证据无偏推理链），并通过多维奖励机制鼓励模型均衡关注多种视觉线索，实现证据驱动的联合推理。

Result: HoloGeo 在 IM2GPS3K 和 YFCC4k 上保持优异性能，同时在 LandmarkBias-3K 上显著优于现有开源 VLM，验证了其在鲁棒地理空间推理中的有效性。

Conclusion: 证据驱动的推理框架能有效缓解地标偏差，提升地理定位的可靠性和鲁棒性，为多模态推理中的偏差缓解提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了 LandmarkBias-3K 基准用于评估地标偏差，并提出了 BI/BH 量化指标，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on geo-localization bias, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: VLM geo-localization, landmark bias, evidence-driven reasoning, benchmark creation, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have significantly improved image geo-localization, yet existing models remain susceptible to landmark bias, causing them to overlook geographical cues or form spurious correlations, ultimately resulting in inaccurate localization. To systematically investigate this issue, we first design two quantitative metrics, Bias Intensity (BI) and Bias Harmfulness (BH), to characterize the impact of landmarks exerted on model reasoning, and establish a comprehensive benchmark, LandmarkBias-3K. To mitigate landmark bias, we further propose an evidence-driven reasoning framework, HoloGeo, to improve the reliability of geo-localization. HoloGeo is supported by a high-quality dataset, BF-30k, annotated with structured multi-evidence bias-free reasoning chains. By incorporating multi-dimensional rewards, HoloGeo explicitly encourages balanced attention over diverse visual cues and achieves evidence-driven joint reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HoloGeo not only maintains excellent performance on IM2GPS3K and YFCC4k but also significantly outperforms existing open-source VLMs on LandmarkBias-3K, validating its effectiveness for robust geospatial reasoning.

</details>


### [83] [MM-IssueLoc: A Controlled Benchmark for Evaluating Visual Evidence in Multimodal Repository-Level Issue Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15205)
*Shaoxiong Zhan, Shi Hu, Boyu Feng, Hai Lin, Andrew Gong, Zhengda Zhou, Jiaying Zhou, Yunyun Hou, Hao Su, Hai-Tao Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.SE

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MM-IssueLoc，一个受控的多模态仓库级问题定位基准，包含 652 个 issue-PR 实例、7 种图像类别和 4 个相关性等级，用于评估视觉证据在代码仓库问题定位中的作用。实验表明现有系统在多模态问题定位上仍不可靠，最强 agent 的 file Acc@5 仅 38.96。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级问题定位评估主要基于纯文本任务，忽略了实际 issue 中常见的截图、错误对话框、UI 状态等视觉证据。已有的多模态 SE 基准将定位与补丁合成纠缠在一起，无法判断视觉输入是否真正帮助了定位。因此需要一个受控基准来明确评估视觉证据在问题定位中的作用。

Method: 构建了包含 652 个 issue-PR 实例的基准，覆盖 23 种编程语言，标注了 7 种图像类别和 4 个相关性等级。提供文件级和函数级 gold labels，支持纯文本和带图像两种评估模式，并设计了 VCE（Visual Content Extraction）诊断方法将图像转换为结构化文本证据。同时提出了 MM-IssueLoc-VL-Emb 作为受控多模态检索器。

Result: 最强 agent 达到 38.96 file Acc@5 和 22.45 function Acc@10；最强检索器达到 33.86 function Acc@10。跨基准比较显示，在文本主导的 SWE 基准上的高定位分数不能直接迁移到多模态问题定位任务上。

Conclusion: MM-IssueLoc 将视觉证据转化为显式的评估变量，揭示了现有系统在多模态仓库级问题定位上的不足，为未来研究提供了受控的测试平台，使研究者能够测试系统是否真正利用视觉证据进行定位，而非依赖纯文本线索或下游补丁生成效应。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个包含视觉证据的多模态仓库级问题定位基准（MM-IssueLoc），属于多模态评测与基准方向。该基准包含图像类别标注、相关性等级、文件级和函数级标注，并设计了受控评估协议。这与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on software engineering issue localization, not on core multimodal generation or world models. Signals: SE benchmark, multimodal understanding, not generation, no continuous token or flow matching, negative keywords absent but direction weight low

Abstract: Real repository issues routinely include visual evidence such as screenshots, error dialogs, rendered UI states, and logs, yet repository-level issue localization is evaluated mostly as a text-only task. Existing multimodal SE benchmarks evaluate end-to-end repair, entangling localization with patch synthesis and obscuring whether visual input helped, hurt, or was ignored. We introduce \textbf{MM-IssueLoc}, a controlled benchmark and evaluation protocol for repository-level localization with visual evidence. MM-IssueLoc contains 652 issue-PR instances across 23 languages, with annotations for 7 image categories and 4 relevance levels. It provides file-level and function-level gold labels, paired text-only and with-image evaluation, and VCE-based diagnostics that convert images into structured textual evidence. We evaluate LLM-based and retrieval-based systems, including MM-IssueLoc-VL-Emb as a controlled multimodal retriever. Results show that existing systems remain far from reliable multimodal repository localization: the strongest agent reaches 38.96 file Acc@5 and 22.45 function Acc@10, while the strongest retriever reaches 33.86 function Acc@10. Cross-benchmark comparisons show that high localization scores on text-dominant SWE benchmarks do not transfer cleanly to multimodal issue localization. MM-IssueLoc turns visual evidence into an explicit evaluation variable, enabling future work to test whether systems improve by using visual evidence for localization, rather than by relying on text-only cues or downstream patch-generation effects.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-efficient_multimodal'></div>

## 多模态高效化与轻量化 [[Back]](#toc)

高效 MLLM、PEFT、蒸馏、量化、token 压缩、稀疏推理。

### [84] [Seeing the End at Step Zero: Accelerating Diffusion MLLMs via MLP Sparsity-Aware Truncation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14557)
*Qicheng Zhao, Qi Sun, Zheyu Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现扩散多模态大模型（DMLLM）在第一步去噪时，MLP激活稀疏性会隐式揭示有效语义边界。基于此提出Seer，一种无需训练的框架，通过SNR准则检测边界并一次性截断冗余后缀，结合混合执行策略实现动态序列长度加速，吞吐量提升最高约31倍，且保持甚至提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMLLM在推理时因实际输出长度未知，需将序列填充到预定义最大长度，导致大量冗余计算浪费在无用的[EOS] token上。现有方法缺乏对DMLLM特有生成机制的效率优化。

Method: 观察到DMLLM在第一步去噪时MLP激活稀疏性会出现显著变化，指示有效语义边界。提出Seer框架：(1) 基于SNR准则检测该边界；(2) 一次性截断冗余后缀用于后续所有计算；(3) 设计混合执行策略以在批处理中兼容动态序列长度。

Result: 吞吐量提升最高约31倍；在9个基准上保持甚至提升性能（如DocVQA从63.52提升至63.66），有效消除填充浪费并缓解噪声泄露。

Conclusion: Seer是一种高效、即插即用的DMLLM加速方案，无需重新训练即可显著提升推理效率，同时保持模型在多模态推理任务上的性能。

Classification reason: 该论文关注扩散多模态大模型（DMLLM）的推理加速，通过检测MLP激活稀疏性进行序列截断以减少冗余计算，属于多模态模型高效化与轻量化方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化" (efficient_multimodal)。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for diffusion MLLMs, a key priority subtopic, with a novel training-free acceleration method. Signals: efficient inference, diffusion MLLM, MLP sparsity, training-free acceleration, dynamic sequence length

Abstract: Diffusion Multimodal Large Language Models (DMLLMs) are highly effective for multimodal reasoning, yet their inference efficiency is significantly hindered by fixed-length generation constraints. Since the actual output length is unknown, output sequences are padded to a predefined maximum length, resulting in substantial redundant computation over unnecessary [EOS] tokens. In this work, we discover that DMLLMs implicitly reveal their valid semantic boundary at the very first denoising step through a distinct shift in MLP activation sparsity. Leveraging this observation, we propose Seer, a training-free framework that detects this boundary using a Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR)-based criterion and performs one-shot truncation of the redundant suffix for all subsequent computations. To preserve these theoretical gains during batched serving, Seer incorporates a hybrid execution strategy that maximizes throughput while seamlessly accommodating dynamic sequence lengths. Experimental results demonstrate that Seer effectively eliminates padding waste, accelerating throughput by up to $\sim$31$\times$. Across 9 benchmarks, Seer robustly maintains overall performance and even improves accuracy on complex visual tasks by mitigating noise leakage (e.g., DocVQA score increases from 63.52 to 63.66), offering a highly efficient, plug-and-play solution for DMLLM acceleration.

</details>

### [85] [Can We Trust Item Response Theory for AI Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15190)
*Han Jiang, Sunbeom Kwon, Jinwen Luo, Ziang Xiao, Susu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (52.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了项目反应理论（IRT）在AI基准评测中的可靠性。由于AI基准数据（少模型、多题目、能力分布偏斜）与人类测试数据分布存在显著差异，标准IRT估计工具可能失效。通过在6个LLM基准上模拟18000种条件，比较4种估计工具，发现经典估计器在大规模场景下不可行，而可扩展估计器在小规模或非正态分布模型集上产生不可靠的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI基准评测越来越多地使用IRT等项目级统计模型来估计模型能力、排名系统、选择信息性样本和诊断基准质量。然而，AI基准数据与IRT最初设计的人类测试数据在数据分布上存在显著差异（模型少、题目多、能力分布偏斜/聚类/多峰），这种分布不匹配可能威胁IRT建模的可靠性，但缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 从6个广泛使用的LLM基准中提取项目参数和能力分布，在三种常见IRT模型下模拟响应矩阵，比较四种估计工具：边际最大似然估计（MML）、马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）、变分推断（VI）和神经伪孪生估计器（NPSE）。在18000种模拟条件下系统评估计算可行性、可扩展性以及模型排名、预测性能和项目特征推断的可靠性。

Result: 经典估计器（MML、MCMC）在大规模基准场景下计算不可行；可扩展估计器（VI、NPSE）在小规模或非正态分布的模型集上产生不可靠的项目级和排名推断。研究识别了何时潜特质模型可靠地支持或可能扭曲AI基准评测结论，以及可信使用所需的样本量和诊断方法。

Conclusion: IRT在AI评测中的应用需要谨慎对待数据分布不匹配问题。经典估计器难以扩展，可扩展估计器在非理想条件下不可靠。研究为AI基准评测中可信使用IRT提供了样本量建议和诊断指南，强调了在AI评测中应用人类测试工具时需要重新审视其适用性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究项目反应理论（IRT）在AI评测中的可靠性，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的评测方法论研究。现有[multimodal_understanding_generation]方向下的[generation_benchmark]（生成模型评测与数据集）子主题涵盖评测基准、自动评测指标等，该论文系统评估IRT在LLM基准测试中的统计可靠性，完全匹配该子主题。

Importance reason: This paper is a methodological critique of IRT for AI evaluation, not directly advancing the user's priority subtopics in continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: IRT reliability, AI benchmark methodology, simulation study, regime mismatch, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: AI benchmarks increasingly leverage item-level statistical models, particularly item response theory (IRT), to estimate model capabilities, rank systems, select informative examples, and diagnose benchmark quality. However, AI benchmark data often departs from the data regime of human testing, for which standard IRT estimation tools were originally developed: benchmarks typically involve fewer evaluated models, far more items, and capability distributions that may be skewed, clustered, or multimodal. We examine how these regime mismatches challenge the reliability of IRT modeling for AI evaluation. Using item parameters and capability distributions derived from six widely used LLM benchmarks, we simulate response matrices under three common IRT models and compare four estimation tools used in recent benchmark studies: marginal maximum likelihood, Markov chain Monte Carlo, variational inference, and a neural pseudo-Siamese estimator. Across 18,000 simulation conditions, we systematically evaluate computational feasibility, scalability, and the reliability of IRT inferences about model rankings, predicted performance, and item characteristics. Results show that classical estimators can become infeasible in large benchmark settings, whereas scalable estimators can produce unreliable item-level and ranking inferences with small or nonnormally distributed model sets. This study identifies when latent trait models reliably support or risk distorting AI benchmarking claims, and what sample sizes and diagnostics are needed for trustworthy use.

</details>

### [86] [Reinforcing Egocentric Spatial Perception in Multimodal Large Language Models via Ego Scene Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14497)
*Chi Kit Wong, Ye Pan, Yuanhuiyi Lyu, Xu Zheng, Zidong Cao, Lutao Jiang, Zixin Zhang, Huiyu Zhou, Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 值得关注 (77.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Ego Scene Augmentation (ESA) 框架，通过构建 Ego-element Graph 作为中间表征，利用视觉基础模型增强多模态大语言模型在自我中心场景中的空间感知能力，在 EgoTextVQA 基准上室内和室外设置分别提升 8.14% 和 8.72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在复杂自我中心场景中由于空间感知能力有限，难以进行有效的空间推理，亟需增强其从自我视角理解空间关系的能力。

Method: 提出 ESA 框架，核心是构建 Ego-element Graph 作为中间表征，该图封装并整合了视觉基础模型提取的自我中心空间特征，然后利用该图增强 MLLM 对自我视角场景的空间感知能力。

Result: 在 EgoTextVQA 基准上，室内设置提升 8.14%，室外设置提升 8.72%，在室内购物子集上表现最为突出。

Conclusion: ESA 框架通过 Ego-element Graph 有效增强了 MLLM 的自我中心空间感知能力，显著提升了自我中心 VQA 任务性能。

Classification reason: 该论文关注自我中心视角下的空间感知与空间推理，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的[空间与 3D 多模态推理]（spatial_3d_reasoning）规范子主题，该子主题涵盖空间推理、3D 视觉定位等。

Importance reason: Addresses spatial perception in MLLMs but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Egocentric VQA, spatial perception enhancement, Ego-element Graph, visual foundational models, EgoTextVQA benchmark

Abstract: Egocentric Visual Question Answering (VQA) has attracted widespread attention as an important task for enabling Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to interact with the real world. However, existing MLLMs struggle to perform effective spatial reasoning in complex egocentric scenes due to their limited spatial perception capabilities. To this end, we introduce Ego Scene Augmentation (ESA), an egocentric spatial perception framework, which actively enhances the spatial perception capabilities from the egocentric perspective, powered by the proposed Ego-element Graph. Our core insight is leveraging the Ego-element Graph as an intermediary representation to augment the egocentric spatial perception of MLLMs via visual foundational models. Specifically, we 1) construct the Ego-element Graph, which encapsulates and integrates egocentric spatial features enabled by visual foundational models; 2) enhance the spatial perception capabilities of MLLMs via the Ego-element Graph for ego-perspective scenes. Our proposed ESA framework presents significant performance improvement on the EgoTextVQA benchmark. We achieve an 8.14% gain on the indoor setting and an 8.72% gain on the outdoor setting. Furthermore, our ESA shows the most impressive performance improvement in the shopping subset of the indoor setting. The project code is publicly available.

</details>


### [87] [Beyond Single Expert: Harmonizing Diverse Visual Priors in MLLMs for Spatial Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15054)
*Xiao Lin, Xiaohu Huang, Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 高优先级 (85.95) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 ViPS 框架，通过高效先验代理（Efficient Prior Proxy）和动态先验融合（Dynamic Prior Fusion）机制，将多个预训练基础模型（如 Depth Anything、SAM、DINOv2 等）的互补视觉先验和谐地注入 MLLM，显著提升空间理解能力，在多个空间推理和 3D 理解基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常只引入单一预训练模型的先验知识来增强 MLLM 的空间理解能力，但作者发现不同基础模型提供了互补的空间先验，在不同任务上各有优势。如何有效融合多种视觉先验以充分发挥其潜力，是本文要解决的核心问题。

Method: ViPS 包含两个核心组件：(1) Efficient Prior Proxy——设计轻量级先验代理模块，以极小的推理开销从多个预训练模型（如 Depth Anything、SAM、DINOv2 等）中提取互补的视觉先验；(2) Dynamic Prior Fusion——提出上下文感知的动态融合机制，根据输入图像和任务需求自适应地融合和注入来自不同先验代理的信息，实现多种先验的和谐整合。

Result: 在多个复杂空间推理和 3D 空间理解基准上，ViPS 超越了现有方法，取得了新的 SOTA 性能。实验表明，融合多种视觉先验比使用单一先验效果更好，且动态融合机制优于简单的拼接或平均融合。

Conclusion: ViPS 验证了融合多种互补视觉先验能够有效提升 MLLM 的空间理解能力，提出的高效先验代理和动态融合机制为多先验融合提供了可行的技术方案，为未来构建更强空间感知能力的多模态模型奠定了基础。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于通过融合多种视觉先验提升 MLLM 的空间理解能力，在多个空间推理和 3D 空间理解基准上取得 SOTA，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的「空间与 3D 多模态推理」[CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with unified multimodal MLLM and efficient inference priorities, with novel multi-prior fusion for spatial reasoning. Signals: MLLM spatial understanding, multi-model prior fusion, efficient prior proxy, dynamic prior fusion, SOTA on spatial/3D benchmarks

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated substantial promise in spatial understanding. Existing works typically incorporate prior knowledge extracted from a pre-trained foundation model to further enhance the spatial awareness of MLLMs. In this paper, we first reveal that when integrating diverse foundation models into MLLMs, different models provide complementary spatial priors that benefit different tasks. Motivated by this, we propose $\textbf{ViPS}$, a novel multi-model prior framework designed to fully unleash the potential of incorporating multiple $\textbf{Vi}$sual $\textbf{P}$riors from diverse models into MLLMs for $\textbf{S}$patial understanding. Specifically, ViPS introduces an Efficient Prior Proxy to generate multiple foundational priors with minimal inference overhead, and a Dynamic Prior Fusion mechanism to achieve harmonious and context-aware prior fusion and injection from the prior proxies. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ViPS successfully harmonizes diverse visual priors, establishing new state-of-the-art performance across multiple complex spatial reasoning and 3D spatial understanding benchmarks. Project page:

</details>

### [88] [Video = World + Event Stream](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15038)
*Lianghua Huang, Zhi-Fan Wu, Yupeng Shi, Wei Wang, Mengyang Feng, Cheng Yu, Chen Liang, Junjie He, Chen-Wei Xie, Yu Liu, Jingren Zhou, Ang Wang, Bang Zhang, Baole Ai, Chongyang Zhong, Jinwei Qi, Kai Zhu, Pandeng Li, Peng Zhang, Wenyuan Zhang, Xinhua Cheng, Yitong Huang, Yun Zheng, Yuxiang Bao, Yuzheng Wang, Zhiwei Lin, Zoubin Bi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (84.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Wan-Streamer v0.3 提出"视频=世界+事件流"的统一视角，将视频分解为持久上下文（世界）和随时间变化的内容（事件流），构建通用预训练任务，并实例化为实时全双工音视频交互模型，实现低延迟（~200ms 模型侧响应）的多模态理解与生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频交互模型通常将视频视为单一序列，缺乏对持久上下文（场景、环境、主体特征等）与动态变化（行为、语音等）的显式区分，导致实时交互中难以高效建模长期依赖和快速响应变化。作者希望提出一个统一框架，将视频分解为"世界"和"事件流"，从而支持通用的实时多模态交互任务。

Method: 提出"视频=世界+事件流"的分解视角：世界是视频展开的持久上下文（环境、场景、主体、声学条件等），事件流是随时间变化的内容（场景变化、主体行为、语音等）。基于此设计通用预训练任务：给定世界和输入事件，预测世界如何实时移动、变化和响应。将该框架实例化为实时全双工音视频交互模型 Wan-Streamer v0.3，其多模态理解过程为视觉-语言-动作形式，将多模态用户输入映射为语言形式的语音和行为动作。模型支持 640×368 视频 25FPS、160ms 流式单元、~200ms 模型侧响应延迟、~550ms 总交互延迟。

Result: Wan-Streamer v0.3 保持了 v0.2 的操作点：640×368 视频 25FPS，160ms 流式单元，约 200ms 模型侧响应延迟，在 350ms 双向网络预算下总交互延迟约 550ms。论文未提供定量评测结果，主要贡献在于概念框架和系统设计。

Conclusion: "视频=世界+事件流"的统一视角为实时多模态交互提供了通用框架，能够支持全双工音视频交互等广泛下游任务。该视角将视频理解与生成统一在同一个范式下，为构建更自然的实时交互系统奠定了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Wan-Streamer v0.3，将视频建模为"世界+事件流"，实现实时全双工音视频交互，属于理解与生成一体化的统一多模态模型。该工作同时涉及多模态生成（音视频生成），因此匹配 multimodal_generation 方向。现有 subtopic "统一多模态模型"（unified_multimodal_model）在 multimodal_understanding_generation 和 multimodal_generation 下均有收录，是最佳匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a unified video decomposition framework (world + event stream) that directly aligns with the user's priority on unified multimodal models and continuous multimodal representation. Signals: Unified video decomposition framework, Real-time full-duplex audio-visual interaction, Low-latency streaming (~200ms), Vision-language-action mapping, General-purpose pretraining task

Abstract: We present Wan-Streamer v0.3, which reframes our native-streaming interaction model under a single organizing view: a video is a world plus an event stream. The world is the persistent context in which a video unfolds, including the environment, scene, subjects, ambient acoustic conditions, voice characteristics, and other relatively stable conditions. The event stream is everything that changes over time within that world, including scene or environmental changes, subject behavior, speech, and other sounds. This yields a general-purpose pretraining task over large amounts of real video: given a world and incoming input, predict how the world moves, changes, and responds in real time. The resulting competence can be specialized to a broad family of real-time downstream tasks. We instantiate it on real-time full-duplex audio-visual interaction, where the event stream is the agent's speech together with free-form behavior. Functionally, the model's multimodal understanding process is vision-language-action-like: it maps multimodal user input to language-form speech and behavior actions. Wan-Streamer v0.3 preserves the v0.2 operating point: 640x368 video at 25 FPS, a 160 ms streaming unit, approximately 200 ms model-side response latency, and approximately 550 ms total interaction latency under a 350 ms bidirectional network budget.

</details>
