### [89] [An Intelligent-Cloud Edge Multimodal Interaction System for Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14675)
*Zihan Guo, Xiaoqi Li*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (65.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种云边协同的多模态交互框架，将改进的YOLO手势检测器（YOLO-DC，集成CBAM和DIoU损失）与LLM/VLM智能体结合，在资源受限的TonyPi机器人上实现鲁棒的人机交互。实验表明手势检测精度达98.9%，系统任务成功率最高达95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂环境下的人机交互需要准确的手势感知、语义场景理解和可靠的任务规划，但机器人端计算资源有限，现有方法难以兼顾精度与效率。

Method: 提出云边协同框架：边缘端（TonyPi机器人）负责数据采集、通信、动作执行和反馈；云端负责手势检测（改进YOLO-DC，在neck中加入CBAM注意力模块，用DIoU损失替代原边界框回归目标）、场景理解、多模态融合和动作规划（LLM+VLM智能体协同）。

Result: YOLO-DC在公共手势数据集和自定义数据集上分别达到98.9%和95.0%的精度，mAP@0.5为90.7%和92.7%。系统级评估中，单动作、复合动作和视觉依赖任务的成功率分别为95%、88%和82%。30名参与者评估的总体平均满意度为3.69/5。

Conclusion: 将改进的手势检测与多模态智能体结合，可以在资源受限的机器人平台上实现有效的人机交互，验证了云边协同框架的可行性。

Classification reason: 论文提出云边协同的多模态交互框架，集成VLM和LLM智能体进行手势感知、场景理解和任务规划，属于通用视觉语言模型与MLLM在机器人交互中的应用，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与MLLM"。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic interaction with YOLO and cloud-edge, not on core continuous multimodal or autoregressive generation priorities. Signals: Robotics interaction, YOLO-DC gesture detection, Cloud-edge framework, Low relevance to priority subtopics, No continuous language or video generation

Abstract: Robust human-robot interaction in complex environments requires accurate gesture perception, semantic scene understanding, and reliable task planning under limited onboard computing resources. This paper presents a cloud-edge multimodal interaction framework that integrates an enhanced YOLO-based gesture detector with coordinated large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) agents. The proposed detector, incorporates the Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM) into the neck and replaces the baseline bounding-box regression objective with Distance-IoU (DIoU) loss. These modifications improve feature discrimination and localization for small or partially occluded gestures in complex backgrounds. The cloud layer performs gesture detection, scene understanding, multimodal fusion, and action planning, whereas the TonyPi robot locally handles data acquisition, communication, action execution, and feedback. Experiments on a public gesture dataset and a custom dataset show that YOLO-DC achieves precision values of 98.9% and 95.0%, with mAP@0.5 values of 90.7% and 92.7%, respectively. System-level evaluation yields success rates of 95%, 88%, and 82% for single-action, composite-action, and vision-dependent tasks. A 30 participant evaluation yields an overall mean satisfaction score of 3.69 out of 5. These results demonstrate the feasibility of combining refined gesture detection with multimodal agents for resource-constrained robotic interaction.

</details>


### [90] [Multi-Head Latent Control: A Unified Interface for LLM Agent Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14277)
*Amirhosein Ghasemabadi, Ruichen Chen, Bahador Rashidi, Di Niu*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Multi-Head Latent Control（MHLC），一种轻量级层，通过从冻结的 LLM 或 VLM 的隐状态轨迹中读取控制信号，实现部署时的决策（如是否继续推理、是否交给更强模型、是否调用工具等），无需修改原模型。在语言和视觉-语言任务上，MHLC 显著改善了多模型系统的质量-成本权衡，在 AndroidWorld 上将大模型使用率降低 90.7%，工具调用质量提升最高 158%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 作为智能体部署时，需要决定是否继续推理、交给更强模型、请求信息、调用工具或放弃回答。现有方法依赖 prompt 级路由、外部编排或任务特定微调，成本高且难以随模型更新维护。作者希望直接从模型的隐生成过程中推断这些控制决策，实现轻量、可迁移的部署时控制。

Method: 提出 Multi-Head Latent Control（MHLC），在冻结的 LLM/VLM 上附加两个轻量级预测头：(1) Capability Head，预测当前模型能否解决该实例，否则交给更强模型；(2) Resolution Head，预测合适的决策：澄清、工具调用、放弃或直接回答。两个头仅在冻结 LLM 的隐状态轨迹上训练，无需修改原模型。支持在推理过程中早期截断并移交，实现成本-质量权衡优化。

Result: 在路由执行（小+大模型）设置下，AndroidWorld 上大模型使用率降低 90.7%，多个基准平均降低 27-53%，同时保留大部分大模型性能。工具调用决策质量提升最高 +158% 相对得分，遗漏必要工具调用减少 65.5%。

Conclusion: MHLC 提供了一种统一、轻量的接口，通过隐状态轨迹直接推断 LLM/VLM 的部署时控制决策，无需修改模型或依赖外部编排，在成本和质量之间取得了良好的平衡，适用于多模型系统和工具调用场景。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Multi-Head Latent Control，通过从冻结 LLM/VLM 的隐状态轨迹中读取控制信号（能力预测、决策预测），实现模型推理时的路由、工具调用等决策。论文同时涉及语言和视觉-语言设置（VLM），属于通用 VLM/MLLM 架构与推理控制的范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 vlm_mllm_general。

Importance reason: Novel latent control for LLM agents, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: latent control, LLM agent, hidden-state trajectories, model routing, tool use

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as agents, but reliable agentic behavior requires more than next-token prediction. At inference time, it is preferred that an agent can decide whether to proceed with its current reasoning, defer to a stronger model, request additional information, invoke external tools, or abstain under the given setup. Existing approaches address these decisions through prompt-level routing, external orchestration, or task-specific fine-tuning, which primarily rely on input-side signals, and are often costly and difficult to maintain as model backbones evolve. We ask whether such control decisions can be inferred directly from a model's latent generation process. We introduce Multi-Head Latent Control, a lightweight layer that reads hidden-state trajectories from a frozen LLM or VLM to produce deployment-time control signals. A Capability Head predicts whether the current model can solve the instance or should defer to a stronger collaborator, while a Resolution Head predicts appropriate resolution decision Clarification, Tool Use, Abstention, or Direct Answering. Both heads are trained only on latent traces from the same frozen LLM backbone, enabling post hoc adaptation without modifying the model. Across language and vision-language settings, Multi-Head Latent Control consistently improves the quality-cost tradeoff of multi-model systems, enabling early handoff from partial generations and more accurate intervention decisions. In routed execution (small + large model), it reduces large-model usage by up to 90.7 percent on AndroidWorld and 27-53 percent on average across benchmarks, while retaining most of large-model performance. Additionally, the learned control signals improve tool-use decision quality, yielding up to +158 percent relative score gain and 65.5 percent fewer missed-required tool calls.

</details>


### [91] [VTM-Nav: Hierarchical Visual-Topological Memory for Cross-Episode Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14514)
*Xiaoran Xu, Yupeng Wu, Tianyu Xue, Yifan Xu, Xuanran Dong, Xiaoshan Yang, Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 VTM-Nav，一种基于分层视觉-拓扑记忆的训练无关 VLM 导航框架，用于跨场景物体目标导航。通过房间级和物体级的持久化记忆结构，结合粗到细匹配和保守执行守卫，在 HM3D 和 MP3D 基准上取得了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关的 VLM 导航方法在每轮 episode 后重置场景状态，无法跨 episode 复用经验。本文提出跨场景物体目标导航任务，让智能体在同一场景中反复运行并保留自获取经验，以提升导航效率。

Method: 提出 VTM-Nav 框架，包含：(1) 分层视觉-拓扑记忆（VTM），在房间级和物体级组织场景知识；(2) 粗到细匹配机制，检索相关经验并在与当前观测一致时提供软引导；(3) 保守执行守卫，缓解振荡、阻塞和过早停止问题。所有组件均无需训练，使用固定 VLM 骨干网络。

Result: 在 HM3D v0.1、HM3D v0.2 和 MP3D 三个基准上，VTM-Nav 均取得最佳性能，超越了增强跨 episode 文本记忆的 WMNav 基线，证明了结构化视觉-拓扑经验复用的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 分层视觉-拓扑记忆能够有效支持跨 episode 的经验复用，提升 VLM 导航智能体在相同场景中的表现，且无需额外训练。该方法在多个数据集上表现一致且鲁棒。

Classification reason: 该论文使用 VLM 进行开放词汇语义推理，属于视觉语言模型在具身导航中的应用，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on embodied navigation with VLM memory, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: VLM navigation, cross-episode memory, object-goal navigation, training-free, hierarchical visual-topological memory

Abstract: Object-goal navigation requires an embodied agent to locate and reach an instance of a specified object category in an indoor environment. Recent training-free approaches leverage vision-language models (VLMs) for open-vocabulary semantic reasoning, but are typically evaluated under an episodic protocol that resets all scene-specific state after each episode. We introduce Cross-Episode Object-Goal Navigation, in which an agent repeatedly operates in the same scene, retains only self-acquired experience, and keeps its model parameters fixed. To support experience reuse, we present \method, a training-free VLM navigation framework with a persistent hierarchical Visual-Topological Memory (VTM). The VTM organizes scene knowledge at room and object levels and retrieves relevant experience through coarse-to-fine matching, providing memory as soft guidance only when it agrees with current observations. A conservative execution guard further mitigates oscillations, blocked motions, and premature stopping. Under a controlled same-scene protocol, we evaluate \method{} on three benchmarks, HM3D v0.1, HM3D v0.2, and MP3D, and compare it with a strengthened WMNav baseline augmented with cross-episode textual memory, while keeping the VLM backbone and action pipeline identical. \method{} achieves the best performance across all three benchmarks, demonstrating the effectiveness and robustness of structured visual-topological experience reuse across datasets.

</details>


### [92] [U-shaped Multi-granularity Learning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14966)
*Biao Chen, Yunqian Yu, Xiangxu Zhao, Zhongshu Chen, Mengmeng Jing, Lin Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (77.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 UPrompt，一种 U 形多粒度提示学习框架，通过粗到细的级联增强和细到粗的分层监督，在视觉和文本模态中构建并行多粒度表征，解决了 VLM 提示学习中的粒度困境，在 17 个基准上取得 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 提示学习方法存在粒度困境：全局提示缺乏细粒度语义感知，局部提示忽略上下文关联，限制了跨任务泛化能力，尤其在密集预测任务中表现明显。

Method: 受 U-Net 启发，UPrompt 在视觉和文本模态中构建并行多粒度表征：粗到细的级联增强传播全局上下文以细化局部细节，细到粗的分层监督确保跨尺度的语义一致性。通过对称的编码器-解码器路径和跨层级连接实现多粒度信息的统一。

Result: 在 17 个基准上的实验表明，UPrompt 在 MSCOCO 上比 MAMET 和 VPKE 分别提升 4.1 和 7.3 rSum，在 base-to-novel 泛化上超越 CoCoA-Mix 5.09%，同时粗粒度版本开销极小，中粒度版本以 1/3 成本匹配 PSRC 性能。

Conclusion: UPrompt 通过 U 形多粒度学习有效解决了 VLM 提示学习中的粒度困境，在密集预测任务上实现了优异的跨任务泛化能力，且计算开销可控。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 UPrompt，一种基于 U-Net 启发的多粒度提示学习框架，用于视觉语言模型（VLM）的密集预测任务。这属于通用视觉语言模型架构与提示学习方法的改进，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a U-shaped multi-granularity prompt learning framework for VLMs, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: VLM prompt learning, multi-granularity, U-Net inspired, SOTA on 17 benchmarks, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: The prompt learning paradigm for vision-language models is effective yet faces a granularity dilemma: global prompts lack fine-grained semantic awareness, while local prompts ignore contextual associations, limiting cross-task generalization. This dilemma exists in dense prediction tasks. Inspired by U-Net, which unifies multi-level representations across granularities, we propose UPrompt, a U-shaped multi-granularity prompt learning framework for vision-language models. Similar to how U-Net integrates fine and coarse features through symmetric encoder-decoder pathways with cross-level connections, UPrompt constructs parallel multi-granularity representations in both visual and textual modalities, where coarse-to-fine cascaded enhancement propagates global context to refine local details, while fine-to-coarse hierarchical supervision ensures semantic consistency across scales. Extensive experiments on 17 benchmarks validate our effectiveness. UPrompt outperforms MAMET and VPKE by 4.1 and 7.3 rSum on MSCOCO, surpasses CoCoA-Mix by 5.09% in base-to-novel generalization, while maintaining competitive performance with minimal overhead (coarse-grained) and matching PSRC with 1/3 cost (medium-grained).

</details>


### [93] [VLT: A Vision-Language-Time Series Multimodal Foundation Model for Industrial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14510)
*Haiteng Wang, Jingheng Yan, Xiaokang Wang, Lei Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VLT 是一个面向工业智能的视觉-语言-时间序列多模态基础模型，通过频谱作为视觉桥梁连接连续时序信号与离散语义，采用 Time-MoE 捕获时序动态、Frequency-Text Augmented Learner 联合建模频谱与语义特征，以及时间中心梯度对齐机制缓解跨模态优化冲突，在少样本、噪声和不完整模态场景下表现出优越的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业时间序列方法通常局限于单模态建模，在复杂场景下泛化能力受限。虽然大语言模型为多模态学习提供了新机遇，但如何桥接连续时间序列信号与离散文本语义仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: 提出 VLT 多模态基础模型，包含三个核心组件：(1) Time-aware Mixture-of-Experts (Time-MoE) 捕获异构时序动态；(2) Frequency-Text Augmented Learner 在共享表示空间中联合建模频谱和语义特征；(3) 时间中心梯度对齐机制，通过梯度归一化和可靠性感知动态重加权缓解跨模态优化冲突。利用频谱作为视觉桥梁连接连续时序信号与离散语义。

Result: 在多个工业数据集上，VLT 在少样本、噪声和不完整模态设置下均优于现有最先进方法，展现出优越的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: VLT 通过频谱视觉桥接和创新的多模态对齐机制，有效解决了工业时序信号与文本语义的跨模态建模难题，为工业智能中的多模态基础模型提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 VLT，一个联合建模时间序列、频谱视觉表示和文本知识的多模态基础模型，属于视觉语言模型（VLM）范畴。虽然其应用领域为工业时序，但其核心是多模态联合建模架构，与 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）方向匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on industrial time-series and PHM, which is outside the user's core interests in continuous language/video generation and world models. Signals: industrial time series, multimodal foundation model, frequency spectrum bridge, Time-MoE, gradient alignment

Abstract: Industrial time series serve as the foundation for Prognostics and Health Management (PHM) to ensure the reliability and safety of industrial equipment such as aero-engines. However, existing approaches are typically limited to single-modality modeling, which restricts their generalization in complex scenarios. Although recent advances in large language models (LLMs) provide new opportunities for multimodal learning, bridging continuous time-series signals and discrete textual semantics remains an open challenge. To this end, we propose VLT, a multimodal foundation model that jointly models time-series, frequency-spectrum visual representations, and textual knowledge. A key insight is to utilize the frequency spectrum as a visual bridge to connect continuous temporal signals with discrete semantics. Specifically, a Time-aware Mixture-of-Experts (Time-MoE) is designed to capture heterogeneous temporal dynamics, while a Frequency-Text Augmented Learner enables joint modeling of spectral and semantic features within a shared representation space. Furthermore, a time-centric gradient alignment mechanism is introduced to mitigate cross-modal optimization conflicts via gradient normalization and reliability-aware dynamic reweighting. Extensive experiments on multiple industrial datasets demonstrate that VLT outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving superior robustness and generalization under few-shot, noisy, and incomplete-modality settings.

</details>


### [94] [A Modern Multimodal Assistant on a 6 GB 2011 GPU: Stage-Validated, All-GPU CUDA Inference for Fermi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14568)
*A. C. Opus, J. Q. Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.other, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (70.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文在 2011 年的 NVIDIA Tesla C2075 (Fermi, 6GB) GPU 上完整部署了现代多模态助手 MiniCPM-V-4.6，实现了全 GPU 推理。主要贡献包括：基于 8 位权重的 SGEMM 投影达到 64% FP32 峰值性能；分块 delta-rule 重写循环层比顺序扫描快 2.8 倍；发现 4 位量化在 Fermi 上反而比 8 位更慢；完整移植视觉编码器并验证每阶段精度（全塔 1.4e-5）；通过 per-head 厂商 GEMM 调用解决长上下文 O(N²) 瓶颈，预填从 21 tok/s 提升至 361 tok/s（10k tokens），端到端图像问答仅需 1.7 秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在 2011 年的老旧 Fermi GPU（6GB）上能否完整运行现代多模态助手模型，验证"模型能完全放入 GPU 显存时"的推理性能，并揭示短基准测试中隐藏的长上下文性能瓶颈。

Method: 将 MiniCPM-V-4.6（SigLIP2 视觉编码器 + 窗口注意力合并器 + 混合门控-德尔塔网络骨干）移植到 Fermi sm_20 CUDA。关键优化包括：8 位权重一次反量化后调用厂商 SGEMM；分块 delta-rule 重写循环层；per-head 厂商 GEMM 调用替代朴素注意力核以消除 O(N²) 瓶颈；逐阶段验证视觉编码器精度。

Result: SGEMM 投影达 64% FP32 峰值；循环层加速 2.8 倍；长上下文预填从 21 tok/s 提升至 361 tok/s（10k tokens），提升 17 倍；图像编码加速 6 倍至 0.93s；端到端图像问答 1.7s；视觉编码器全塔精度 1.4e-5。

Conclusion: 2011 年的 Fermi GPU 仍可运行现代多模态助手，但需要针对其架构特点（如 nibble 解包半速、无原生注意力加速）进行深度优化；短基准测试会掩盖长上下文的 O(N²) 性能墙；float 分桶的 tie-breaking 是实现定义的，应直接调用参考算子而非重实现。

Classification reason: 该论文将 MiniCPM-V-4.6 多模态助手（结合 SigLIP2 视觉编码器与混合门控-德尔塔网络骨干）部署到 Fermi GPU 上，属于通用视觉语言模型（VLM/MLLM）的工程实现与优化工作，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 "视觉语言模型与 MLLM"。

Importance reason: Focuses on legacy hardware deployment, not advancing core multimodal or continuous model methods. Signals: Legacy GPU deployment, No new model architecture, No continuous representation, No generation results, Historical hardware focus

Abstract: A companion study ran a 35B mixture-of-experts model on a 2011 NVIDIA Tesla C2075 (Fermi, sm_20, 6GB) as a GPU-prefill/CPU-decode hybrid, because the 4-bit model did not fit in device memory ( ). This report keeps the hardware and asks what a model that fits can do: we deploy MiniCPM-V-4.6, a modern multimodal assistant pairing a SigLIP2 vision encoder and window-attention merger (16x visual token compression) with a compact hybrid gated-delta-net backbone, entirely on the GPU. Three results. (i) An all-GPU engine built on measured foundations: projections that dequantize 8-bit weights once and call the vendor SGEMM still in the last Fermi toolchain (64% of FP32 peak; our best hand-written GEMM hit 37%, wrongly called the ceiling); a chunked delta-rule rewrite of the recurrent layers, 2.8x faster than the sequential scan once attribution exposed one bad kernel; and a measured negative: 4-bit weights make decode slower than 8-bit here, since Fermi issues nibble-unpacking shifts at half rate. (ii) The vision side is a port with a proof obligation: we translate tower, merger, and projector to sm_20 CUDA, validating every stage against a locally generated reference forward (full tower 1.4e-5). One failure, position-embedding bucketization differing on exact rational ties, generalizes to a rule: float tie-breaking in index arithmetic is implementation-defined; call the reference operator, do not reimplement it. (iii) Long context exposes an O(N^2) wall short benchmarks hide: prefill falls from 114 tok/s at 2k tokens to 21 at 10k in a naive attention kernel; per-head vendor-GEMM calls writing into the existing score buffer (zero extra memory) restore a flat profile (408 at 2k, 361 at 10k; 17x), verified by exact needle retrieval from 60% depth. The same rewrite cuts image encoding 6x, to 0.93s. The system answers an image question end-to-end in 1.7s.

</details>

### [95] [MonteRET: AI Agent Enhancing Multimodal LLMs with Multi-granularity Knowledge Retrieval for Chest CT Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14264)
*Yi Lin, Yihao Ding, Elana Benishay, Elefterios Trikantzopoulos, David Nauheim, Hanley Ong, Jiang Bian, Hua Xu, Yuzhe Yang, George Shih, Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (44.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MonteRET是一个区域感知的检索增强框架，用于自动生成胸部CT报告。它整合全局CT特征与区域级解剖表示，通过预测的医学条件和区域级视觉-语言对齐检索临床相关知识，并利用知识引导的报告改写智能体优化初始报告。在RadGenome-ChestCT数据集（24,128次CT扫描）上训练，在内部测试集（1,564次）和外部数据集（82次）上均优于基线方法，召回率提升最为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动胸部CT报告生成面临挑战：临床可信的报告既需要全卷理解，又需要准确描述局部解剖发现。现有方法难以同时兼顾全局上下文和局部细节，导致报告质量不足。

Method: MonteRET框架包含三个核心组件：1）全局CT特征与区域级解剖表示整合模块；2）基于预测医学条件和区域级视觉-语言对齐的临床知识检索模块；3）知识引导的报告改写智能体，用于优化初始报告。模型在RadGenome-ChestCT数据集上训练。

Result: 在RadGenome-ChestCT测试集（1,564次CT扫描）和外部数据集（82次CT扫描，来自纽约长老会/威尔康奈尔医学中心）上，MonteRET在报告质量、语义相似度和临床效能方面均优于匹配基线和多种SOTA方法。召回率提升最为显著，表明遗漏发现更少。放射科住院医师的人工评估也倾向于MonteRET。

Conclusion: MonteRET通过区域感知检索增强框架有效提升了胸部CT报告生成的临床准确性和完整性，尤其减少了遗漏发现的情况，为医学影像报告自动化提供了有前景的方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于胸部CT报告生成，属于医学影像理解与自动报告生成任务，与视觉问答（VQA）和文档理解方向高度相关。虽然具体领域为医学影像报告，但核心任务是视觉输入到文本输出的理解与生成，映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）最为合适。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical report generation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: medical report generation, chest CT, retrieval-augmented generation, multimodal LLM, clinical application

Abstract: Automated chest CT report generation remains challenging because clinically faithful reporting requires both whole-volume understanding and accurate description of localized anatomical findings. Here we developed and retrospectively evaluated MonteRET, a region-aware retrieval-enhanced framework for generating chest CT findings sections. MonteRET integrates global CT features with region-level anatomical representations, retrieves clinically relevant knowledge using predicted medical conditions and region-level vision-language alignment, and refines initial reports through a knowledge-guided report rewriting agent. We trained our model on a public cohort with 24,128 CT scans from RadGenome-ChestCT. We evaluated MonteRET on the public RadGenome-ChestCT test set of 1,564 CT scans and an external cohort of 82 CT scans from NewYork-Presbyterian/Weill Cornell Medical Center. MonteRET improved report quality, semantic similarity, and clinical efficacy compared with a matched baseline and several state-of-the-art methods. Gains were most pronounced for recall, suggesting fewer omitted findings. Human expert evaluation by radiology residents also favored MonteRET.

</details>


### [96] [Beyond the Leaderboard: Design Lessons for Trustworthy Multimodal VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15241)
*Sushant Gautam, Vajira Thambawita, Michael A. Riegler, Pål Halvorsen, Steven A. Hicks*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (42.79) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文以MediaEval Medico 2025胃肠道内窥镜VQA挑战为案例，回顾分析了九个系统的设计选择，发现参数高效微调虽能取得良好成绩，但答案层面的提升并不总能转化为忠实且完整的临床推理。结构化推理与显式接地的方法在异质问题类型上表现更可靠，并提出了超越词汇重叠的评测、标准化证据链接解释、数据泄露感知治理等建议，以推动可信赖的医疗多模态VQA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗多模态AI需要结合视觉与文本证据，同时保持可靠性和可解释性。现有VQA评测多依赖词汇重叠指标，难以反映临床推理的忠实性与完整性，亟需系统性的设计分析与可信赖评测框架。

Method: 以MediaEval Medico 2025胃肠道内窥镜VQA挑战为回顾性案例研究，分析九个已记录系统的设计选择（包括骨干网络、微调策略、推理机制等），对比其在问答准确性与解释质量上的表现，并基于相关性分析总结设计经验。

Result: 参数高效微调（如LoRA）能取得强挑战性能，但答案级提升不持续转化为忠实完整的临床推理；采用结构化推理与显式接地的方法在异质问题类型上表现更可靠；证据为相关性而非消融实验性质。

Conclusion: 医疗多模态VQA需要超越词汇重叠的评测指标、标准化证据链接的解释、数据泄露感知的数据治理以及轻量级的鲁棒性与校准检查，以构建可信赖的医疗多模态AI系统。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于多模态医疗VQA系统的设计分析与评测，属于视觉问答（VQA）范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical VQA trustworthiness, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: Medical domain, VQA evaluation, parameter-efficient fine-tuning, structured reasoning, not generative or continuous representation

Abstract: Healthcare multimodal AI must combine visual and textual evidence while remaining reliable and interpretable. Using MediaEval Medico 2025 as a retrospective GI endoscopy case study, we analyze design choices across nine documented systems for question answering and explanation quality. Parameter-efficient adaptation of pretrained backbones provides strong challenge performance, but answer-level gains do not consistently translate into faithful and complete clinical reasoning. Methods enforcing structured reasoning and explicit grounding show more reliable behavior across heterogeneous question types, although the evidence is correlational rather than ablation-based. These results motivate evaluation beyond lexical overlap, standardized evidence-linked explanations, leakage-aware data governance, and lightweight robustness and calibration checks. The findings support trustworthy multimodal healthcare AI based on data fusion, explainability, and resilient evaluation.

</details>


### [97] [Multi-LLM Collaborative MRI Report Generation for Visual Instruction Tuning in Brain Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14581)
*Sinyoung Ra, Jonghun Kim, Hyunjin Park*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多LLM协作方法，利用多个大语言模型协同生成和校验脑肿瘤MRI报告，构建3D MRI-文本数据集，并训练视觉语言模型实现3D MRI报告生成与视觉问答，在脑肿瘤诊断任务上优于现有2D和3D方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学VLM主要针对2D影像（如胸部X光片），缺乏3D影像-文本配对数据，导致3D MRI报告生成困难。本文旨在解决3D医学影像报告生成的数据稀缺问题。

Method: 提出多LLM协作系统：多个LLM分别生成和校验报告，确保准确性和清晰度；利用该流程构建3D MRI-文本数据集；在此基础上训练VLM，将MRI扫描转换为token并与文本指令对齐，实现报告生成和VQA。

Result: 所提出的VLM在脑肿瘤MRI报告生成和视觉问答任务上优于其他2D和3D方法，报告质量显著提升。

Conclusion: 多LLM协作生成校验机制能有效构建高质量3D医学影像-文本数据集，基于该数据训练的VLM可提升脑肿瘤诊断和治疗中的报告生成与问答能力。

Classification reason: 该论文涉及3D MRI脑肿瘤报告生成和视觉问答，属于医学视觉问答与报告生成任务，与多模态理解方向下的"视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）子主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical report generation for brain oncology, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: Medical domain, 3D MRI, brain oncology, multi-LLM collaboration, visual instruction tuning

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) and their extension to vision-language models (VLMs) have made it easier to combine text and images for tasks such as report generation. Existing VLMs in medicine typically focus on 2D images (chest X-rays), and their extension to 3D imaging has been difficult because of the lack of paired 3D imaging-text data. Thus, we introduce a new method for creating a 3D image-text dataset for brain oncology using 3D MRI scans of glioma and meningioma cases. We use a cooperative system in which several LLMs work together to generate and check reports, ensuring that they are accurate and clear. By leveraging the new 3D MRI-text dataset, we further build a VLM that converts MRI scans into tokens and aligns them with text instructions. Our VLM performed better in report generation and visual question answering tasks than other 2D and 3D methods. Our method not only improves the quality of reports but also helps with better diagnosis and treatment in brain oncology.

</details>


### [98] [Stop Thinking, Start Looking: Efficient Post-Training for Multimodal Document Question Answering via Reasoning-Free Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14682)
*Harikrishnan P M, Goutham Vignesh, Ganesh Parab, Saisubramaniam Gopalakrishnan, Vishal Vaddina, Varun V, Rohit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Perception-RFT，一种基于 GRPO 的多模态文档问答训练框架，跳过中间推理 token，直接对齐视觉特征与结构化定位输出。实验表明，在 4B 参数规模下，推理增强模型会抑制推理痕迹并收敛到直接感知策略，推理 token 减少 60% 以上，而纯感知训练优于推理增强 RL。此外，发现了一种新的"定位发散"现象——联合 RL 优化下语义鲁棒性与几何精度之间存在选择性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态文档问答方法分为两类：SFT 需要大量标注数据且易达到优化平台期；基于推理的 RL 依赖冗长的中间推理 token，增加了推理成本却无明显收益。本文旨在探索是否可以在不依赖显式推理链的情况下，通过 RL 直接对齐视觉特征与结构化定位输出，实现高效的多模态文档问答。

Method: 提出 Perception-RFT 框架，将 GRPO 应用于多模态文档问答，跳过中间推理 token，直接对齐视觉特征与结构化定位输出。为评估推理的必要性，在相同奖励设置下构建了推理变体进行对比。使用 Qwen3-VL-4B 作为基础模型，在文档 QA 数据集上进行训练，并分析 SFT 饱和、冷启动 RL 不稳定等现象。

Result: 推理增强模型在训练过程中会抑制推理痕迹，收敛到直接感知策略，推理 token 长度减少 60% 以上；纯感知训练优于推理增强 RL。发现"定位发散"现象：联合 RL 优化下语义鲁棒性与几何精度之间存在选择性权衡。早期 SFT→RL 转换可在减少 65% 训练数据的情况下达到相当的精度。

Conclusion: 多模态文档问答中，跳过中间推理 token 的直接感知 RL 训练比推理增强 RL 更高效。推理 token 并非必要，反而增加成本。SFT 饱和与冷启动 RL 不稳定等文本域现象同样存在于多模态域。定位发散现象揭示了联合优化中不同能力维度之间的权衡，为未来多模态 RL 训练策略设计提供了指导。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦多模态文档问答（Document QA）中的视觉定位与对齐，属于视觉问答与文档理解（multimodal_vqa_doc）这一规范主题。同时涉及GRPO强化学习训练方法，但核心任务仍是文档级VQA。

Importance reason: Focuses on document QA efficiency, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: multimodal document QA, GRPO post-training, reasoning-free alignment, grounding divergence, token efficiency

Abstract: Efficient multimodal document question answering with explicit visual grounding, locating the precise document region that supports each answer remains an open challenge. Current approaches bifurcate into Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), which requires large annotated datasets and reaches optimization plateaus, and reasoning-centric Reinforcement Learning (RL), which depends on verbose intermediate traces that inflate inference token cost without clear benefit. We introduce Perception-RFT, a training framework that applies Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to multimodal document QA, bypassing intermediate reasoning tokens to directly align visual features with structured grounding outputs. To rigorously evaluate the necessity of reasoning, we construct a reasoning variant under identical reward settings. We find that reasoning-enabled models suppress their reasoning traces during training, converging to direct perception-based policies at the 4B parameter scale, reducing per-query inference token length by more than 60%, while reasoning-enabled RL underperforms perception-only training. Through a fine-grained analysis of Qwen3-VL-4B optimization dynamics, we confirm that SFT saturation and cold-start RL instability established in text-domain post-training extend to multimodal, and identify a previously uncharacterized Grounding Divergence: a selective trade-off between semantic robustness and geometric precision on two out of distribution (OOD) benchmarks (4,828 samples) under joint RL optimization. We further show that an early SFT$\rightarrow$RL transition achieves comparable precision with 65% less training data.

</details>

### [99] [VideoSEMA: a scalable and efficient Mamba-like attention for video understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14711)
*Nhat Thanh Tran, Fanghui Xue andShuai Zhang, Jiancheng Lyu, Yunling Zheng, Yingyong Qi, Jack Xin*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (54.46) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VideoSEMA 提出了一种结合 Mamba 式空间注意力（SEMA）与 softmax 时序注意力的分裂时空注意力模型，用于视频理解（分类）。在 K400 和 SSv2 基准上，该模型以更低的计算成本超越了更重的 ViT 和 Mamba 模型，且在高分辨率下性能下降更平缓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解模型（如 ViT 和 Mamba）计算成本高，且在高分辨率或长视频场景下性能退化严重。作者希望设计一种更高效、可扩展的注意力机制，在保持精度的同时降低计算开销。

Method: 提出 VideoSEMA 模型，采用分裂时空注意力：空间维度使用 SEMA（Mamba 式注意力）块，将局部窗口注意力与全局平均池化并行结合；时间维度使用 softmax 时序注意力。在特定秩条件下，证明了分裂时空注意力与全时空注意力的等价性。

Result: 在 K400 上超越更重的 ViT 和 Mamba 模型；在 SSv2 上，在相似参数量模型中取得最高 top-1 准确率。分辨率从 224² 扩展到 1024² 时，性能下降比 VideoMamba 更平缓。

Conclusion: VideoSEMA 是一种高效、可扩展的视频理解模型，在精度和计算效率之间取得了良好平衡，且有望通过稀疏/膨胀时序注意力扩展到更长视频。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 VideoSEMA，一种用于视频理解（分类）的分裂时空注意力模型，属于视频理解方向。现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视频理解与时序推理" (video_understanding_temporal) 完全匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on video understanding classification, not generation or world models, and lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or autoregressive generation. Signals: Mamba-like attention, efficient video understanding, split space-time attention, K400/SSv2 benchmarks, graceful high-resolution degradation

Abstract: We present for video understanding (classification) a split space-time attention model, VideoSEMA, consisting of a scalable and efficient Mamba-like attention (SEMA) block in space and a softmax temporal attention in time. In each frame, SEMA attention applies a local window attention in parallel with a global averaging in a Mamba macro-architecture, which is called Mamba-like. Under certain rank conditions, we prove that the computationally cheaper split space-time attention is equivalent to full space-time attention. On benchmark K400 data sets, VideoSEMA out-performs heavier vision transformer and Mamba models. On benchmark SSv2 data, VideoSEMA leads in top-1 accuracy among models of similar parameter sizes. As image resolution scales up from standard $224^2$ to $1024^2$ on K400 and without fine-tuning, VideoSEMA degrades much more gracefully than VideoMamba in accuracy. It is promising to extend VideoSEMA to longer videos with a dilated/sparse temporal attention.

</details>


### [100] [VideoChat3: Fully Open Video MLLM for Efficient and Generalist Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14935)
*Xinhao Li, Yuhan Zhu, Xiangyu Zeng, Yuhao Dong, Haoning Wu, Zhiqiu Zhang, Yuandong Yang, Changlian Ma, Qingyu Zhang, Yansong Shi, Xinyu Chen, Haoran Chen, Zizheng Huang, Jun Zhang, Kun Ouyang, Lin Sui, Ziang Yan, Yicheng Xu, Chenting Wang, Yinan He, Hongjie Zhang, Yi Wang, Yu Qiao, Yali Wang, Ziwei Liu, Kai Chen, Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 高优先级 (84.72) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VideoChat3 是一个全开源、高效且通用的 4B 参数视频 MLLM，通过 I3D-ViT 和自适应帧分辨率实现高效时空表征，并构建了覆盖通用、长视频和流式视频的多样化训练数据集，在多个基准上超越同等或更大参数量的开源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源视频 MLLM 存在三个主要局限：1）难以泛化到多种视频类型，仅在特定领域有效；2）计算开销大，效率低；3）大多仅部分开源（训练代码、策略或数据集未公开），阻碍可复现性和社区发展。因此需要构建一个全开源、高效且通用的视频理解模型。

Method: 1）提出 Inflated 3D Vision Transformer (I3D-ViT)，将 2D ViT 膨胀为 3D 以捕获时空特征，同时引入 Adaptive Frame Resolution for Streaming Video Perception 降低流式视频处理成本。2）开发可扩展的视频数据合成流水线，构建三个高质量数据集：VideoChat3-Academic2M（通用）、VideoChat3-LV116K（长视频）、VideoChat3-OL617K（流式视频），覆盖不同场景以提升泛化能力。

Result: 在通用、长视频和流式视频基准测试上，VideoChat3（4B 参数）超越了此前同等或更大参数量的开源模型，同时具有更高的计算效率。

Conclusion: VideoChat3 通过高效的 I3D-ViT 架构和多样化的数据合成策略，在视频理解领域实现了泛化能力与计算效率的平衡，为社区提供了一个全开源的高性能视频 MLLM 基线。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 VideoChat3，一个面向通用、长视频和流式视频的全开源视频 MLLM，属于视频理解方向。映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视频理解与时序推理" (video_understanding_temporal)，该子主题涵盖视频 LLM、视频问答、长视频理解等，与本文高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with unified multimodal model and efficient inference priorities, with fully open-source code and datasets. Signals: unified multimodal model, efficient inference, fully open-source, video understanding, 4B parameter model

Abstract: Recent advances in video understanding have spanned motion, long video, and streaming interaction, driving this field toward real-world applications. Despite this progress, current open-source models remain limited in several ways. They often struggle to generalize across diverse video types, making them effective only in specific domains. High computational demands further restrict their efficiency and scalability. Moreover, most models are only partially open, with key components such as training code, strategy, or datasets unavailable, which hinders reproducibility and slows community-driven development. To address these issues, we introduce VideoChat3, a fully open, efficient, and generalist video-centric MLLM. VideoChat3 advances video understanding through two complementary designs. For efficiency, we introduce Inflated 3D Vision Transformer (I3D-ViT) and Adaptive Frame Resolution for Streaming Video Perception, which enables efficient spatiotemporal representation and reduces the cost of processing video inputs during training and inference. For effectiveness, we develop a scalable video data synthesis pipeline that curates three diverse, high-quality training datasets: VideoChat3-Academic2M, VideoChat3-LV116K, and VideoChat3-OL617K, covering general, long-form, and streaming video scenarios, improving the model's generalization across domains. By integrating these designs, VideoChat3 achieves a rare balance of broad generalization and computational efficiency. Experiments across general, long-form, and streaming benchmarks demonstrate that VideoChat3 surpasses prior open-source models with equal or larger parameter counts with only 4B parameters and higher efficiency.

</details>
